śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ
kṛtyakalpataruḥ
brahmacārikāṇḍam
[prārthanā]
[viṣṇuḥ]
devaḥ spuran mahimatattadanantamatsya-
kūrmādijanmakathitādbhutavaibhavo yaḥ |
brahmāṇḍabudbudamayo sphuradaṃśaleśaḥ
māyām aphohatu sa bodhamahodadhir vaḥ || 1 ||
[śivaḥ]
jāne yasya lalāṭalocanaśikhā lekhāpi candrī vibho-
raṃmāśī samayaṃ (?) jagajjanayituṃ bījāya saṃjāyate |
tasyormivyapayogiyogijanatāhṛtpadmasadmasthita-
sphāradhvāntaraharaṃ harasya daharasthānaṃ mahāmo mahaḥ || 2 ||
[manuḥ]
suhṛdyaḥ sukṛtāpuṇyakṣīranīravivecanaḥ |
manur mānasam āviśya rājahaṃsaḥ punātu vaḥ || 3 ||
[2]
[rājñaḥ praśaṃsā]
śrīmanmānavavāsavaḥ sa vijayī govindacandro ‘sti yaḥ
krīḍātarjitagauḍagarjita[bha]yastambhībhava[tpārthivaḥ] |
sarvorvīndrajayapratiśrutiviyatsaṅkocanāśaṅkinā
yenātmāpi jitas tataḥ śamabhṛtā manye kim anye pare || 4 ||
eṣa jñānaparākramaikavasatir govindacandraḥ paraṃ
kandarpasya ca vairiṇāṃ ca viśikhaiḥ rakto ‘bhitaḥ śobhate |
māyām apy avanīśamaṇḍalam api svacchandam ucchindatā
yenātmā kṛtinā dinaiḥ katipayair advaitam āropitaḥ || 5 ||
kośān geheṣv amuñcan pathi karituragān bāndhavān ardhamārge
durge hy antaḥpurāṇi pratiravacakitāḥ parvatebhyo nivṛttāḥ |
yasyodyoge dravante ‘samasamarasamārambhagambhīrabherī-
bhāṃkārākīrṇakarṇajvarabharataralaprekṣitāḥ śātraveśāḥ || 6 ||
sa kila sakaladṛptakṣatranakṣatralakṣmī-
haraṇakiraṇamālī kasya na syān namasyaḥ |
asamasamarasampallampaṭaḥ śauryabhājām
avadhiravadhi yuddhe yena hammīravīraḥ || 7 ||
[granthakartuḥ praśaṃsā]
vidyāvallivilāsabhūruhavaro vīrasya viprottamaḥ
śrīlakṣmīdhara ity acintyamahimā tasyāsti mantrīśvaraḥ |
bhūbhartuḥ padacakravākamithunakrīḍārtham āsūtritā
yenānekanarendraratnamukuṭajyotirmayī vāhinī || 8 ||
hutvā lakṣam arātibhūpatiśiraḥpadmāni śauryānale
pṛthvīṃ sādhayatā samudravasanāṃ śrīdevakāśīpateḥ |
[3]        etenottamasāddhakena kati na krūrāśayāḥ prāpitāḥ
saṃgrāmavratanaiṣṭhikena vilayaṃ vidyārthinā prārthitāḥ || 9 ||
[granthasya praśaṃsā]
tenāyaṃ vedamūlaḥ smṛtinicayamayaskandhavān yaḥ purāṇ-
vyūhavyutpannaśākhaḥ prakaraṇanikaroddaṇḍakāṇḍapracaṇḍaḥ |
unmīladvākyapatraḥ sphuradativiśadajñānaramyaḥ prasūnaḥ
śrīmaddharmārthakāmāmṛtamayaphaladas tanyate kalpavṛkṣaḥ || 10 ||
paurāṇīr eva vāṇīḥ kvacid akṛtakṛtau kvāpi bhūyaḥ smṛtīnāṃ
gopālas tadvayasyaḥ svakṛtiviracanaṃ vākyarūpeṇa cakre |
śrautasmārtādisārair vibudhajanamanohāri kāriṣyate ‘yaṃ
mīmāṃsottaṃsitārthair apṛthur atha kathātīarandhraḥ prabandhaḥ || 11 ||
yasyotthitau sthitimatī na mahārṇave śrīḥ
nidrāṃ dadhāti yad adhaḥ kila kāmadhenuḥ |
tasyātaniṣyati ratiṃ vibudhadvijānāṃ
ānandanaḥ kim u na kalpataroḥ prarohaḥ || 12 ||
kā cintaiva mahārṇavānusaraṇe kā kāmadhenuspṛhā
kṣudrāḥ kasya parisphuranti hṛdaye te ratnamālādayaḥ |
śrīlakṣmīdharabuddhivaibhavasudhāsekaprabhāvād ayaṃ
yenātraiva jagattrayopakṛtaye kalpadrumaḥ kalpate || 13 ||
ekārtheṣv ekam ekaṃ kvacid aparam api svīkṛtaṃ kāryayogāt
nyastaṃ vijñānamūlaṃ pracarad api parityaktam ajñānamūlam |
śiṣṭaiḥ samyag gṛhītaṃ vacanam abhihitaṃ spaṣṭitaṃ cāsphuṭārthaṃ
yatrāpāro virodhaḥ sphurati viracitā tena tatra vyavasthā ||
[4]        lakṣmīdhareṇa munimukhyavacaḥsamudraṃ
nyāyādriṇā kila viloḍya samuddhṛto ‘yam |
kāṇḍaiś caturdaśabhir adbhutaparvabandhaiḥ
vāñchocitaṃ phalati kalpatarur na kasya || 15 ||
[brahmacārikāṇḍopakramaślokaḥ pratijñā ca]
vitrastāḥ vimukhāḥ kṣatendriyasukhāḥ kṣmātalpanidrālavo
bhikṣāvṛttijuṣas tvacā paricitāḥ kaupīnamātrāmbarāḥ |
nītāḥ kāśyadhipasya yena ripavas te brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ
kāṇḍe sa prathāśramaṃ prathamataḥ prastauti lakṣmīdharaḥ || 16 ||
kṛtyakalpatarāv ādye kāṇḍe ‘smin brahmacāriṇaḥ |
svarūpaphalato mānanimittaḥ dharmanirṇayaḥ ||
paribhāṣātha saṃskāraḥ tathopanayanakriyā |
vidhir ācamanasyātha nimittaṃ śaucam eva ca ||
saṃdhyāvidhyagnikārye ca abhivādopasaṃgrahau |
mānyā ācāryagurvādisaṃjñāḥ tanmānanakramaḥ ||
gurvācāryānuvṛttis tu putrabhāryānuvartanam |
indriyāṇāṃ nigrahaś ca niyamā brahmacāriṇām ||
adhyāpyo ‘dhyāpanavidhiḥ svādhyāyadivasetarau |
adhyeyaṃ caturāśramyavyavsthā naiṣṭhikakriyā ||
ācāryadakṣiṇāsnāne iti dvāviṃśatikramāt |
parvāṇy abhinirūpyante vistṛtāni vibhāgaśaḥ ||
[5]
|| 1 ||
dharmanirṇayaḥ
atha svarūpaphalapramāṇanimittaiḥ pravṛttyaupayikaṃ dharmanirūpaṇam | tatra ko dharma ity apekṣāyāṃ dharmasvarūpaphalapramāṇanimittaiḥ pravṛttyaupayikaṃ dharmanirūpaṇam |
manuḥ [2.1] |
vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhiḥ nityam adveṣarāgibhiḥ |
hṛdayenābhyanujñāto yo dharmas taṃ nibodhata ||
vidvadbhiḥ vedārthavidbhiḥ | sevitaḥ satatam anuṣṭhitaḥ | sadbhiḥ sādhubhiḥ | nityam aninditaṃ prathamatayā | adveṣarāgibhiḥ vidhyanumitarāgadveṣaśūnyaiḥ | hṛdayenābhyanujñāto nirvicikitsam kartavyatvenānumataḥ |
viśvāmitraḥ |
yamārthāḥ kriyamāṇaṃ hi śaṃsanty āgamavedinaḥ |
sa dharmo yaṃ vigarhanti tam adharmaṃ pracakṣate ||
[6]
āpastambaḥ [1.10.6–7] |
na dharmādharmau carata āvāṃ sva iti | na devā na gandharvā na pitara ity ācakṣate ‘yaṃ dharmo ‘yam adharma iti | yaṃ tv āryāḥ kriyamāṇaṃ praśaṃsanti sa dharmo yaṃ vigarhanti so ‘dharmaḥ |
ayam arthaḥ — ātmakathanāya sarvalokapratyakṣaṃ dharmādharmāvāyāṃ vartāvaha iti bruvāṇau na bhramataḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
dharmaḥ śreyaḥ samuddiṣṭaṃ śreyo ‘bhyudayalakṣaṇam |
dharmaḥ śreya ity śreyaḥ, dharmeṇa tatsādhanaṃ lakṣyate | mukhyaṃ punaḥ śreyo ‘bhyudayarūpam| tad āha śreyo ‘bhyudayalakṣaṇam iti |
atha dharmaviśeṣāḥ
tatra hārītaḥ |
athāśramāṇām pṛthagdharmo viśeṣadharmaḥ samānadharmaḥ kṛtsnadharmaś ceti | pṛthagāśramānuṣṭā[nā?]t pṛthagdharmo, yathā cāturvarṇyadharmāḥ | svāśramaviśeṣānuṣṭhānād viśeṣadharmo [yathā]naiṣṭhikayā[7]yāvarānujñāyi-cāturāśramyasiddhānām | sarveṣāṃ yaḥ samānaḥ [sa samānadharmaḥ | naiṣṭhikaḥ] kṛtsnadharmaḥ |
anujñāyiko vānaprathaviśeṣaḥ | cāturāśramyasiddho ayam iti śeṣaḥ | naiṣṭhikaḥ kṛtnasharma [iti | niṣṭhā] saṃsārasamāptiḥ, ātmajñānavataḥ prayavāyaparīhārāya nityanaimittikānuṣṭhānam iti yāvat [tatprayojanako naiṣṭhikaḥ] |
dharma ity anuvṛttau bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
sa tu pañcavidhaḥ prokto vedamūlaḥ sanātanaḥ |
varṇadharmaḥ smṛtas tv eko āśramāṇām ataḥ param |
varṇāśramas tṛtīyas tu gauṇo naimittikas tathā ||
varṇatvam ekam āśritya yo dharmaḥ saṃpravartate |
varṇadharmaḥ [sa] uktas tu yathopanayanaṃ nṛpa ||
yas tv āśramaṃ samāśritya adhikāraḥ pravartate |
sa kalv āśramadharmas tu bhikṣādaṇḍādiko yathā ||
varṇatvam āśramatvaṃ ca yo ‘dhikṛtya pravartate |
sa varṇāśramadharmas tu mauñjīyā mekhalā yathā ||
yo guṇena pravarteta guṇadharmaḥ sa ucyate |
yathā mūrdhābhiṣikta[sya] prajānāṃ parimālanam ||
nimittam ekam āśritya yo dharmaḥ saṃpravartate |
naimittikaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ prāyaścittavidhir yathā ||
adhikāro atra dharmaḥ |
[8] kālikāpurāṇe |
iṣṭāpūrtau smṛtau dharmau śrutau vai śiṣṭasaṃmatau |
pratiṣṭḥādyaṃ tayoḥ pūrtam iṣṭaṃ yajñādilakṣaṇam |
bhuktimuktipradaṃ pūrtam iṣṭaṃ bhogārthasādhanam ||
pratiṣṭhādyaṃ devatāpratiṣṭhājalāśayotsargādi | tayoḥ [iṣṭāpūrtayoḥ madhye] |
śrīmahābhārate |
ekāgnikarmahavanaṃ tretāyāṃ yatra hūyate |
antarvedyāṃ ca yad dānam iṣṭaṃ tad abhidhīyate ||
vāpīkūpataḍāgāni devatāyatanāni ca |
annapradānam ārāmāḥ pūrtam āryāḥ pracakṣate ||
ekāgnikarmahavanam ekasminn agnau āvasathyādau homarūpakarma | tretāyām agnitraye |
manuḥ [1.88–91] |
adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |
dānaṃ pratigrahaṃ caiva brāhmaṇānām akalpayat ||
prajānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyādhyayanam eva ca |
viṣayeṣv aprasaktiṃ ca kṣatriyasya samādiśat ||
paśūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyādhyayanam eva ca |
[9]        vaṇikpathaṃ kusīdaṃ ca vaiśyasya kṛṣim eva ca ||
ekam eva tu śūdrasya prabhuḥ karma samādiśat |
eteṣām eva varṇānāṃ śuśrūṣām anasūyayā ||
viṣayāprasaktiḥ indriyavijayaḥ | vaṇikpatham vāṇijyam | kusīdam vṛddhyarthaṃ dhanaprayogaḥ |
mahābhārate |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca paraṃtapa |
karmāṇi pravibhaktāni svabhāvaprabhavair guṇaiḥ ||
śamo damas tapaḥ śaucaṃ kṣāntir ārjavam eva ca |
jñānaṃ vijñānam āstikyaṃ brahmakarma svabhāvajam ||
vijñānam yajñādikarmakauśalam |
śauryaṃ tejo dhṛtir dākṣyaṃ yuddhe cāpy apalāyanam |
dānam īśvarabhāvaś ca kṣātraṃ karma svabhāvajam ||
śauryam vikarmaśīlatvam | tejaḥ adhṛṣyataupādhiko guṇaviśeṣaḥ | dākṣyam utsāhaḥ | īśvarabhāvaḥ prabhutvam |
kṛṣigorakṣavāṇijyaṃ vaiśyakarmasvabhāvajam |
[10]        paricaryātmakaṃ karma śūdrasyāpi svabhāvajam ||
gorakṣam paśupālyam |
sve sve karmaṇy abhirataḥ saṃsiddhiṃ labhate naraḥ |
tathā [18.47] |
śreyān svadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmāt svanuṣṭhitāt |
svadharme nidhanaṃ śreyaḥ paradharmo bhayāvahaḥ ||
svadharmaḥ svavarṇāśramadharmaḥ |
mahābhārate |
ānṛśaṃsyam ahiṃsā cāpramādaḥ saṃvibhāgitā |
śrāddhakarmātitheyaṃ ca satyam akrodha eva ca ||
sveṣu dāreṣu saṃtoṣaḥ śaucaṃ nityānurūpataḥ |
ātmajñānaṃ titikṣā ca dharmaḥ sādhāraṇo nṛpa ||
ānṛśaṃsyam akrūratvam | ātmajñānam nityatvajñānam | titikṣā kṣamā |
tathā sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ
viṣṇuḥ [2.16–17] |
kṣamā satyaṃ damaḥ śaucaṃ dānam indriyasaṃyamaḥ |
ahiṃsā guruśruśrūṣā tīrthānusaraṇaṃ tathā ||
[11]        ārjavaṃ lobhaśūnyatvaṃ devabrāhmaṇapūjanam |
anabhyasūyā ca tathā dharmaḥ sāmānya ucyate ||
sāmānyaḥ caturṇām api varṇānām |
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskāra, 489] |
dayā kṣamānasūyā ca śaucānāyāsamaṅgalam |
akārpaṇyam aspṛhatvaṃ sarvasādhāraṇāni ca ||
śrīmahābhārate |
satyaṃ damas tapaḥ śaucaṃ saṃtoṣo hrīḥ kṣamārjavam |
jñānaṃ śamo dayā dānaṃ eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
śaucaṃ dānaṃ tapaḥ śraddhā gurusevā kṣamā dayā |
vijñānaṃ vinayaḥ satyam iti dharmasamuccayaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.8] |
ijyācāradayāhiṃsādānasvādhyāyakarmaṇām |
ayaṃ sa paramo dharmo yad yogenātmadarśanam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskāra 490, 492, 495, 496, 498–500]
pare vā bandhuvarge vā mitre dveṣṭari vā sadā |
[12]        āpanne rakṣitavyaṃ tu dayaiṣa parikīrtitā ||
bāhye vādhyātmike caiva duḥkhe cotpādite kvacit |
na kupyati na vā hanti sā kṣamā parikīrtitā ||
na guṇān guṇino hanti stauti mandaguṇān api |
nānyadoṣeṣu ramate sānasūyā prakīrtitā ||
abhakṣyaparihāraś ca saṃsargaś cāpy aninditaiḥ |
svadharme ca vyavasthānaṃ śaucam etat prakīrtitam ||
śarīraṃ pīḍyate yena suśubhenāpi karmaṇā |
atyantaṃ tan na kartavyam anāyāsaḥ sa ucyate ||
praśastācaraṇaṃ nityam apraśastavisarjanam |
etad dhi maṅgalaṃ proktam ṛṣibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ ||
stokād api hi dātavyam adīnenāntarātmanā |
ahany ahani yat kiṃcid akārpaṇyaṃ [hi] tat smṛtam ||
yathotpannena saṃtoṣaḥ kartavyo hy atha vastunā |
parasyācintayitvārthaṃ sāspṛhā parikīrtitā ||
mahābhārate |
satyaṃ bhūtahitaṃ proktaṃ manaso damanaṃ damaḥ |
tapaḥ svadharmavartitvaṃ śaucaṃ saṅkaravarjanam ||
saṃtoṣo viṣayatyāgo hrīr akāryanivartanam |
kṣamā dvandvasahiṣṇutvam ārjavaṃ samacittatā ||
jñānaṃ tattvārthasaṃbodhaḥ śamaś cittaprasannatā |
[13]        dayā bhūtahitaiṣitvaṃ dhyānaṃ nirviṣayaṃ manaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
vratopavāsaniyamaiḥ śarīrottāpanaṃ tapaḥ ||
tathā |
pratyayo dharmakāryeṣu tathā śraddhābhyudāhṛtā |
nāsti hy aśraddhadhānasya dharmakāryaprayojanam ||
devīpurāṇe |
kāyakleṣair na bahubhir na caivārthasya rāśibhiḥ |
dharmaḥ saṃprāpyate sūkṣmaḥ śraddhāhīnaiḥ surair api ||
devalaḥ |
vigarhātikramākṣepahiṃsābādhavadhātmanām |
anyamanyusamutthānadoṣāṇāṃ marṣaṇaṃ kṣamā ||
tathā |
svaduḥkheṣv iva kāruṇyaṃ paraduḥkheṣu sauhṛdam |
dayeti munayaḥ prāhur anukrośaṃ ca jantuṣu ||
[14]        yat punar vaidikīnāṃ ca laukikīnāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
dhāraṇā sarvavidyānāṃ vijñānam it kīrtyate ||
vinayaṃ dvividhaṃ prāhuḥ śaśvad damaśamāv api |
śarīroparatiḥ śāṇtir jñānaṃ prajñāprasādajam ||
śrūyatāṃ dharmasarvasvaṃ śrutvā caivāvadhāryatām |
ātmanaḥ pratikūlāni pareṣāṃ na samācaret ||
dakṣaḥ |
yathaivātmā paras tadvad draṣṭavyaḥ sukham icchatā |
sukhaduḥkhāni tulyāni yathātmani tathā pare ||
atha phalato dharmanirūpaṇam
tatra manuḥ (2.9) |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhan hi mānavaḥ |
iha īrtim avāpnoti preetya cānuttamaṃ sukham ||
anuttamaṃ sukham iti yathāprāptatattatphalopalakṣaṇārtham |
gautamaḥ (11.31–32) |
varṇāśramāś ca svasvadharmaniṣṭhāḥ pretya karmaphalam anubhūya tataḥ śeṣeṇa viśiṣṭa[deśa]jātikularūpāyuḥśrutavṛtta[vitta]sukhamedhaso janma pratipadyante | viṣavañco viparītā naśyanti |
[15] tataḥ śeṣeṇeti tasyaiva karmaṇaḥ śeṣeṇeti bhartṛjñaḥ | tasmād anyena śeṣeṇa karmaṇeti bhaṭṭapādāḥ | yad āhuḥ gautamīye ‘pi tacceṣas tasmāc cittādyapekṣayeti | medhasa iti sakārāntaṃ vīryavācī | viṣvañcaḥ sarvato gāminaḥ, yatheṣṭaceṣṭā iti yāvat | viparītā narakādau pāpaphalam anubhūya naśyanti viruddhadeśādau janma pratipadya pāparatā bhavanti |
āpastamba [2.2.2–5] |
sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharmānuṣṭhāne paramaparimitaṃ sukham | tataḥ parivṛttau karmaphalaśeṣeṇa jātiṃ rūpaṃ varṇaṃ balaṃ medhāṃ prajñāṃ dravyāṇi dharmānuṣṭhānam iti prapadyante | tac cakravad ubhayalokayoḥ sukha eva vartate | yathauṣadhivanaspatīnāṃ bījasya kṣetre karmaviśeṣeṇa phalavṛddhir evam | etena doṣaphalaparivṛddhir uktā |
param śreṣṭham | aparimitam cirakālānuvṛtti | parivṛttiḥ punarāvṛttiḥ | karmaphalaśeṣeṇa karmaphalasādhanaśeṣeṇety arthaḥ | etac ca nityānām eva karmaṇāṃ phalāntaram iti kapardibhāṣyam | [16] tat tasmāt sukha eva vartate na duḥkham | kṣetre bījasyoptasya [karṣaṇādinā yathā] phalaparivṛddhiḥ sarvataḥ parimāṇataḥ vīryādibhir vṛddhiḥ tathā āvaśyakakarmānuṣṭhātari puruṣe |
tathā manuḥ [4.153] |
ācārāl labhate hy āyur ācārād īpsitāḥ prajāḥ |
ācārād dhanam akṣayyam ācāro hanty alakṣaṇam ||
ācāraḥ āvaśyakaṃ karma | akṣayyam atiprabhūtam ity arthaḥ | alakṣaṇam aśubhasūcakadehacihnaṃ skandhādau tilakālakādi tatsūcitam adharmam anārabdhaphalaṃ hantīty arthaḥ |
manu-vasiṣṭha-yamāḥ [Manu 4.158; Vasiṣṭha 6.8] |
sarvalakṣaṇahīno ‘pi yaḥ sadācāravān naraḥ |
śraddhadhāno ‘nasūyaś ca śataṃ varṣāṇi jīvati ||
hārītaḥ [Vasiṣṭha 6.1] |
ācāraḥ paramo dharma iti vedavido viduḥ |
na hy anācāriṇo lokān pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
tathā |
kāmyaiḥ kecid yajñadānais tapobhir
labdhvā lokān punar āyānti janma |
kāmaiḥ muktāḥ satyayajñāḥ sudānās
taponiṣṭhā akṣayān yānti lokān ||
[17] etena kāmanāsadasadbhāvanibandhanaḥ phalabhedo darśitaḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
phalaṃ vināpy anuṣṭhānaṃ nityānām iṣyate sphuṭam |
kāmyānāṃ svaphalārthaṃ tu doṣaghātārtham eva ca ||
naimittikānāṃ karaṇaṃ trividhaṃ karmaṇāṃ phalam |
kṣayaṃ kecid upātt[sya] duritasya pracakṣate ||
anutpattiṃ tathā cānye pratyavāyasya manyate |
nityakriyāṃ tathā cānye anuṣaṅgaphalaṃ śrutim ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskā. 11.229] |
śrutismṛtī cakṣuṣī dve dvijānāṃ nyāyavartmani |
mārge muhyanti taddhīnāḥ prayatanti pathaścytāḥ ||
nyāyavartmani nyāyārthe, dharma iti yāvat |
atra manuvasiṣṭhau [manu 4.157]; vasiṣṭha 6.6] |
durācāro hi puruṣo loke bhavati ninditaḥ |
duḥkhabhāgī ca satataṃ vyādhito ‘lpāyur eva ca ||
durācāro ninditācāraḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.12.11–12] |
yatra [tu] prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttir na tv atra śāstram asti | tad anuvarta[māno] narakāya rādhyate |
[18] yatra prītyupalabdhitaḥ iti | laukikapramāṇataḥ prītisādhana[tva]jñānād yatra pravartate, na tatra śāstravyāpāraḥ | narakāya rādhyati narakavaśo bhavati, radhyatir vaśagamana iti niruktakāravacanāt [Nirukta 6.32] |
aṅgirāḥ |
svābhiprāyakṛtaṃ karma yat kiṃcij jñānavarjitam |
krīḍākarme ca bālānāṃ tat sarvaṃ niṣprayojanam ||
jñānavarjitam śāstrīyajñānavarjitam |
āpastambaḥ [1.20.1–2] |
nemaṃ laukikam arthaṃ puraskṛtya dharmāṃś caret | niṣphalā hy abhyudaye bhavanti |
imaṃ dhanakīrtyādikam, puraskṛtya uddiśya, abhyudaye śāstrīyaphalaviṣaye |
manuḥ [4.242] |
tasmād dharmaṃ sahāyārthaṃ nityaṃ saṃcinuyāc chanaiḥ |
dharmeṇa hi sahāyena tamas tarati duḥsaham ||
tamaḥ pāpam | tathā [4.171] |
sīdann api hi dharmeṇa mano dharme na vārayet |
adhārmikāṇāṃ pāpānām āśu paśyan viparyayam ||
[19] adhārmikāṇāṃ vihitam akurvatām, pāpānāṃ niṣiddhakāriṇām | āśu iti nindātiśayārtham |
mahābhārate |
yasya notkrāmati matir dharmamārgānusāriṇī |
tam āhuḥ puṇyakarmāṇam aśocyaṃ mitrabāndhavaiḥ ||
sopānabhūtaṃ svargasya mānuṣyaṃ prāpya durlabham |
tathātmānaṃ samādadhyān na bhraśyeta yathā punaḥ ||
tathā |
dharmāya ye ‘bhyasūyanti buddhimohānvitā narāḥ |
apathā gacchatāṃ teṣām anuyātāpi naśyati ||
idaṃ ca tvām atra paraṃ bravīmi
puṇyapradaṃ tāta mahāviśiṣṭam |
na jātu kāmān na bhayān na lobhād
dharmaṃ jahyāj jīvitasyāpi hetoḥ ||
atraiva taittirīyaśrutiḥ |
dharmo viśvasya jagataḥ pratiṣṭhā, loke dharmiṣṭhaṃ prajā upasarpanti | dharmeṇa pāpam apanudanti, dharme sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam, tasmād dharmaṃ paramaṃ vadanti |
[20]
atha pramāṇataḥ
tatra manuḥ [2.6] |
vedo ‘khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnāṃ ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca ||
akhilaḥ vidhiniṣedhādyātmakaḥ | dharmaśabdaś cājahatsvārthalakṣaṇayā dharmādiparaḥ, vedasya dharmapramitimūlatvāt | anuvādaś cāyaṃ taddvāreṇa smṛtyādīnāṃ vedamūlatvapratipādanārtham | śīlaṃ adrohādi | tathā ca mahābhārate |
adrohaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ karmaṇā manasā girā |
anugrahaś ca dānaś ca śīlam etad vidur budhāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
brahmaṇyatā devapitṛbhaktatā saumyatā aparopatāpitā anasūyatā mṛdutā apāruṣyatā maitratā priyavāditā kṛtajñatā śaraṇyatā [praṇati]ś ceti trayodaśavidhaṃ śīlam | tasya cācāravantaḥ pramāṇam |
tadvidāṃ vedārthavidām | ācāro vivāhādau kaṅkaṇabandhanādyanuṣṭhānam | ātmatuṣṭḥ adharmasaṃdehe sati vaidikasaṃskāravāsitāntaḥkaraṇānāṃ sādhūnāṃ ekatra pakṣe manaḥ paritoṣaḥ | [21] yad uktaṃ bhaṭṭapādaiḥ |
etena vaidikānekadharmadhīsaṃskṛtātmanām |
ātmatuṣṭeḥ pramāṇatvaṃ [siddhiṃ dharmaprasiddhaye] ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.7] |
śrutiḥ smṛtiḥ sadācāraḥ svasya ca priyam ātmanaḥ |
samyaksaṃkalpajaḥ kāmo dharmamūlam idaṃ smṛtam ||
priyaśabdena prītir lakṣyate | svasya ātmanaḥ dharmamūlaṃ na sarvasyeti | samyaksaṃkalpaja iti samyaksaṃkalpāj jātaḥ | kāmaḥ śāstrāviruddhe arthe [yathā] mayā bhojanavyatirekeṇa udakaṃ na pātavyam iti viśvarūpaḥ | atha vā samyaksaṃkalpāt rāgādirahitasaṃkalpāt, jñānād upajātaṃ brāhmaṇopacikīrṣādi [yad eva] manunā śīlatvenoktaṃ [tad eva] samyak saṃkalpaja ity anenaivocyate |
tathā [1.3] |
purāṇanyāsamīmāṃsādharmaśāstrāṅgamiśritāḥ |
vedāḥ sthānāni vidyānāṃ dharmasya ca caturdaśa ||
[22] vidyānāṃ puruṣārthajñānānām, dharmasya ca sthānāni vyavasthāpakāni, bodhakatvena |
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe [3.6.28–29] |
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
dharmaśāstraṃ purāṇaṃ ca vidyā hy etāś caturdaśa ||
āyurvedo dhanurvedo gāndharvaś ceti te trayaḥ |
arthaśāstraṃ caturthaṃ ca vidyās tv aṣṭādaśaiva tāḥ ||
caturdaśa dharmapramitiṃ prati pradhānāni | catasraḥ punar dṛṣṛṭapradhānāḥ kvacid alaukikam arthaṃ pramāṇayantyo dharme ‘pi pramāṇam |
sumantuḥ |
vedavedāṅgetihāsapurāṇatarkmīmāṃsādharmaśāstrāṇi kulagaṇadeśajāti-varṇadharmapākhaṇḍāśramaśrotriyadharmavyavasthāpravartakāni |
ādyo dharmaśabdo varṇaparyantaiḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | kuladharmaḥ cūḍākaraṇakālādiḥ | gaṇadharāś ca vakṣyante | deśadharmāḥ strīṇāṃ vāsovinyāsaviśeṣādayaḥ | jātidharmāḥ [sūtādīnāṃ aśvasārathyādayaḥ | varṇadharmāḥ] upanayanādayaḥ | pākhaṇḍādayaḥ vedabāhyāḥ | āśramadharmāḥ bhikṣādaṇḍādayaḥ | śrotriyaś chando’dhyāyī, taddharmāḥ [23] kārīryādyadhyayanabhūmau bhojanādayaḥ | punaḥ dharmagrahaṇaṃ pākhaṇḍasaṃbandha-vyavacchedārtham |
pāraskaraḥ |
vidhir vidheyas tarkaś ca vedaḥ ṣaḍaṅgātmakaḥ |
vidhiḥ ajñātajñāpako vedabhāgaḥ | vedheyo mantraḥ | tarko mīmāṃsā |
devalaḥ |
ṛgyajuḥsāmārthavāṇo vedāḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
smṛti[r] dharmaśāstrāṇi | teṣāṃ praṇetāro manuviṣṇuyamadakṣāṅgiro’tri-bṛhaspaty-uśana-āpastambavasiṣthakātyāyanaparāśaravyāsaśaṅkhalikhita-saṃvartagautama-śātātapahārītayājñavalkyaprācetasādayaḥ |
yamaḥ |
manur yamo vasiṣtho ‘trir dakṣo viṣṇus tathāṅgirāḥ |
uśanā vākpatir vyāsa āpastambho ‘tha gautamaḥ ||
kātyāyano nāradaś ca yājñavalkyaḥ parāśaraḥ |
saṃvartaś caiva śaṅkhaś ca hārīto likhitas tathā ||
[24]         etair yāni praṇītāni dharmaśāstrāṇi vai purā |
tāny evātipraṇītāni na hantavyāni hetubhiḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitagranthau | ādiśabdagrāhyāś caite — budhadevalasomajamadagniviśvāmitra-pajāpativṛddhaśātātapa[sumantu]paiṭhīnasīpitāmahabaudhāyanachāgaleyajābālacyavana-marīcikāśyapāḥ |
tathā ca bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇeṣu yāni vākyāni putraka |
tāny ālocya mahābāho tathā smṛtyantareṣu ca ||
manvādismṛtayo yās tu ṣaṭtriṃśatparikīrtitāḥ |
tāsāṃ vākyāni kramaśaḥ samalocya bravīmi te ||
iti guhaṃ prati īśvaravākye |
manvādismṛtayaḥ tāś cānantaroktābhir eva sarvaparigṛhītābhiḥ pūryante | vṛddhaśātātapa-yogiyājñavalkyavṛddhavasiṣṭhavṛddhamanulaghuhārītādīni tu prasiddhaṣaṭtiṃśadantargata-kartṛkāny eva avasthābhedena tair eva kā[ka]raṇāt |
[25] tathā ca yājñavalkyenoktam “yogaśāstraṃ ca yat proktam” iti | tathā |
snānam abdaivatair mantrair yat tvayoktaṃ purānagha | iti |
yāni tu gṛhyatatpariśiṣṭādīni tāni bhinnakoṭīny eva purāṇavat [pramāṇāni] | “tathā smṛtyantareṣu ca” iti bhedenābhidhānāt | yāni tu viṣṇudharma-śivadharma-mahābhārata-rāmāyaṇādīni tāny api “tathā smṛtyantareṣu ca” ity anenaivopāttāni | tathā coktaṃ bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇāni rāmasya caritaṃ tathā |
viṣṇudharmādiśāstrāṇi śivadharmāś ca bhārata |
kārṣṇaṃ ca pañcamo vedo yan mahābhārataṃ smṛtam |
saurāś ca dharmā rājendra mānavoktā mahīpate ||
jayeti nāma caiteṣāṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
yad atra śloke smṛtiḥ iti kṛtvā pramāṇikānāṃ vacanam, tad api mahājanaparigṛhītatvena pramāṇatvāt | “tathā smṛtyantareṣu [26] ca” ity anenaiva saṃgṛhītaṃ veditavyam | yat tu ṣaṭtriṃśanmatādi kaiścid eva parigṛhītād vijñānāc ca pramāṇam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
āmnāyaprāmānyād ācāraḥ sarveṣam upadiśyate |
ācāryaiḥ [ity arthaḥ] |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.3] |
sādhavaḥ kṣīṇadoṣās tu sacchabdaḥ sādhuvācakaḥ |
teṣām ācaraṇaṃ yat tu sadācāraḥ sa ucyate ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [1.4–5] |
śrutismṛtivihito dharmaḥ | tadalābhe śiṣṭācāraḥ pramāṇam |
baudhāyano yamaś ca [Baudhāyana 1.1.5–6] |
śiṣṭāḥ khalu vigatamatsarāḥ nira[haṃkārāḥ, kumbhīdhānyā alolupā dambhadarpalobhamohakrodhavarjitāḥ |
dharmenādhigato yeṣāṃ vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
śiṣṭās tadanumānajñāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ ||
matsaraḥ parotkarṣāsahiṣṇutvam | nirahaṃkārāḥ nirabhimānāḥ | kumbhīdhānyāḥ alpasaṃtuṣṭā iti vivakṣitāḥ] alolupāḥ viṣayālampaṭāḥ, dambhaḥ khyātyarthaṃ [27] dharmānuṣṭhānam | dharmeṇādhyayanam itikartavyatayā | saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ sāṅgetihāsapurāṇaḥ | tadaanumānajñāḥ vede tūhāpohakuśalāḥ | śrutir vedaḥ pratyakṣaḥ śravaṇendiryagrāhyo hetur upāyo dharmajñāne yeṣāṃ te tathā [uktāḥ] |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [6.43] |
pāraṃparyāgato yeṣāṃ vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
śiṣṭāḥ śrutismṛtivihitāvasthitāḥ pratipattavyāḥ |
avasthitāḥ jñānānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhāḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.29.16] |
strībhyaḥ sarvavarṇebhyaś ca dharmaśeṣān pratīyād ity eke |
eke ye pūrvaṃ āpastambena strīṣu śudreṣu cātharvaṇasya vedasya śeṣa ity upadiśyantīty uktāḥ | śiṣṭācāraprāmānye ‘tiprasaṅganivāraṇāyāha āpastambaḥ [2.13.7–8] |
dṛṣṭo dharmavyatikramaḥ sāhasaṃ caiva pūrveṣām | teṣāṃ tejoviśeṣeṇa pratyavāyo na vidyate | tad anvīkṣya prayuñjānaḥ sāidaty avarajo ‘balaḥ |
vyatikramaḥ buddhipūrvaṃ rāgād ācaraṇam, yathendrasyāhalyāgamanam | sāhasaṃ krodhādinā avimṛṣyakaraṇam, yathā vasiṣṭhasya jalapraveśaḥ | abalaḥ atejaskaḥ abrahmajña iti [28] yāvat | avarajaḥ indānīṃtanaḥ | tejo brahmajñānam | tadviśeṣeṇa sākṣātkaraṇam | yathā puṣkarapālāśa āpo na śliṣyanti | “evam evaṃ viditapāpaṃ karma na śliṣyata” iti brahmajñānina eva pāpānāśleṣa[bodhaka]śruteḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
āpatsv api hi kāṣṭhāsu parasvaṃ naiva kāmayet |
nābhakṣyaṃ bhakṣayet kiṃcin na pūrvacaritaṃ caret ||
tejomayāni pūrveṣāṃ navīnānīndriyāṇi ca |
pāpais te nopalipyante padmapatram ivāmbhasā ||
surāṃ pibanti tridaśāḥ yā cāpeyā dvijātibhiḥ |
haranti vittaṃ yat siddhāḥ tad vāsiddhe na vidyate ||
pūrvacaritaṃ niṣiddhamātrābhipretam | tejomayāni jñānapradhānāni |
mahābhārate |
kṛtāni yāni karmāṇi daivatair munibhis tathā |
nācaret tāni dharmātmā śrutvā cāpi na kutsayet |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.2.1–9] |
pañcadhā vipratipattir dakṣiṇatas tathottarataḥ | yāni dakṣiṇatas tāni vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |yathaitad anupanītena saha bhojanam [29] striyā saha bhojanam paryuṣitabhojanam mātulapitṛsvasṛduhitṛpariṇayanam iti | athottarataḥ ūrṇāvikrayaḥ sīdhupānam ubhayatodadbhir vyavahāraḥ āyudhīyatā samudrayānam iti | itarad itarasmin kurvan [duṣyatītarad itarasmin] | tatra tatra deśaprāmāṇyam eva syāt | mithyaitad iti gautamaḥ | ubhayaṃ tv etan nādriyeta | śiṣṭasmṛtivirodhadarśanāt |
vipratipattiḥ dharmaśāstraviruddhapratipattiḥ | tatra deśaprāmāṇyam eva syāt iti pūrvapakṣaḥ | śeṣaḥ sugamam |
atra purāṇalakṣaṇam ucyate
matsyapurāṇe [53.65] |
sargaś ca pratisargaś ca vaṃśo manvantarāṇi ca |
vaṃśānucaritaṃ caiva purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam ||
pratisargaḥ saṃhāraḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.6.20–24] |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇāni purāṇajñāḥ pracakṣate |
brāhmaṃ pādmaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ ca śaivaṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā ||
tathānyaṃ nāradīyaṃ ca mārkaṇḍeyaṃ ca saptamam |
āgneyam aṣṭamaṃ caiva bhaviṣyaṃ navamaṃ smṛtam ||
daśamaṃ brahmavaivartaṃ liṅgam ekādaśaṃ smṛtam |
[30]        vārāhaṃ dvādaśaṃ caiva skāndaṃ cātra trayodaśam ||
caturdaśaṃ vāmanaṃ ca kaurmaṃ pañcadaśaṃ smṛtam |
mātsyaṃ ca gāruḍaṃ caiva brahmāṇḍaṃ ca tataḥ param ||
[mahāpurāṇāny etāni hy aṣṭādaśa mahāmune] ||
matsyapurāṇe [53.60–64] |
pādme purāṇe yat proktaṃ narasiṃhopavarṇanam |
tad aṣṭādaśasāhasraṃ nārasiṃham ihocyate ||
nandāyā yatra māhātmyaṃ kārtikeyena varṇyate |
nandīpurāṇaṃ tallokaiḥ nandākhyamiti kīrtyate ||
yat tu sāmbaṃ puraskṛtya bhaviṣyati kathānakam |
procyate tat punar loke sāmbam etan munivratāḥ ||
[evam ādityasaṃjñā ca tatraiva parigadyate |
aṣṭādaśabhyas tu pṛthak purāṇaṃ yat tu dṛśyate] ||
vijānīdhvaṃ dvijaśreṣṭāḥ tad etebhyo vinirgatam |
vinirgatam udbhūtam, yathā kālikāpurāṇādi |
devalaḥ |
śikṣāvyākaraṇaniruktachandaḥkalpajyotiṣāṇi (?) vedāṅgāni | tathā ārṣāḥ pūrvavṛttāntāśrayāḥ pratipattiphalā ityhāsāḥ |
ārṣāḥ ṛṣipraṇītāḥ |
bhaviṣye |
dṛṣṭārthā tu smṛtiḥ kācid adṛṣṭārthā tathāparā |
[31]        dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārtharūpānyā nyāyamūlā tathā parāḥ ||
anuvādasmṛtis tv anyā śiṣṭair dṛṣṭā tu pañcamī |
sarvā etā vedamūlā dṛṣṭārthā parihṛtya tu ||
tathā |
ṣāḍguṇyasya prayogasya prayogaḥ kāryagocarāt |
sāmādīnām upāyānāṃ yogo vyāsasamāsataḥ ||
adhyakṣāṇāṃ [ca] nikṣepaḥ kaṇṭakānāṃ nirūpaṇam |
dṛṣṭārtheyaṃ smṛtiḥ proktā ṛṣibhir garuḍāgraja ||
samdhyopāstiḥ sadā kāryā śuno māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayet |
adṛṣṭārthā smṛtiḥ proktā ṛṣibhir jñānakovidaiḥ ||
pālāśaṃ dhārayed daṇḍam ubhayārthaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
vibodhe tu vikalpaḥ syāj japahomaśrutau yathā ||
śrutau dṛṣṭaṃ tathā kārtyaṃ smṛtau na tādṛśaṃ yadi |
anuktavādinī sā tu privrājyaṃ tathā gṛhāt ||
ṣāḍguṇaḥ saṃdhivigrahādayo vakṣyante | prayogaḥ kāryagauravāt ṣāḍguṇyasyaiva vyāsasamāsābhyāṃ kartavya ity arthaḥ | sāmādīnām api kāryagauravād vyāsasamāsābhyāṃ prayogaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | japahomaśrutāv iti, sūryodayāvadhi sāvitrījapo ‘nuditahomaviṣayo yathā | anuktavādinī anuditavādinī | yathā putraiṣaṇāyāś ca vittaiṣaṇāyāś ca lokaiṣaṇāyāś ca vyutthāya [yathāprāpta] [32] bhykṣacaryaṃ caranti ity anayānūditaṃ “brahmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhād” iti manur vadati [6.38] vidatte ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ [12.105–06] |
pratyakṣam anumānaṃ ca śabdaṃ vividhāgamam |
trayaṃ suviditaṃ kāryaṃ dharmaśuddhim abhīpsatā ||
ārṣaṃ dharmopadeśaṃ ca vedaśāstrāvirodhinā |
yas tarkeṇānusaṃdhatte sa dharmaṃ veda nāparaḥ ||
śabdaṃ śabdajñānād asaṃnikṛṣṭe ‘rthe vijñānam | vividhāgamaṃ vividhaḥ śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇarūpa āgamo yasya jñānasya karaṇaṃ tad vividhāgamam | ārṣaṃ vaidikam | tarkeṇa mīmāṃsādinā | etac ca pramāṇamātropalakṣaṇārtham | tathā cārthāpattyupamānābhāvānām api dharmaprayoga ity upamāyogaḥ kathito bhavati |
vyāsaḥ |
dravyaśuddhim abhīpsadbhir na vedād anyad iṣyate |
[33]        dharmasya kāraṇaṃ śuddhaṃ miśram anyat prakīrtitam ||
ataḥ sa paramo dharmo yo vedād avagamyate |
avaraḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ yaḥ purāṇādiṣu sthitaḥ ||
etebhyo ‘pi yad anyat syāt kiṃcid dharmābhidhāyakam |
tad adbhutavaco viddhi mohas tasyāśrayo mataḥ ||
vedārthavittamaiḥ kāṛyaṃ yat smṛtam ṛṣibhiḥ purā |
tad yatnenānutiṣṭhet tu tanniṣiddhaṃ tu varjayet ||
te hi vedārthatattvajñāḥ lokānāṃ hitamāmyayā |
pradiṣṭavanto yad dharmaṃ taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet ||
vedārtho yaḥ svayaṃ jñātas tatrājñānaṃ bhaved api |
ṛṣibhir niścite tatra kā śaṅkā syān manīṣiṇām ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
rāgadveṣāgnidagdhānām ajñānaviṣapāyinām |
cikitsā dharmaśāstrāṇi vyādhīnām iva bheṣajam ||
rāgadveṣāṃśena tanmūlaṃ bhrāntijñānaṃ lakṣyate ||
yamaḥ |
yathā hi mṛnmayaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ duṣṭaṃ varṣaśatair api |
punardāhena sudhyeta dharmaśāstais tathā dvijaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
ajñānatimirāndhānāṃ [bhrāmitānāṃ] kudṛṣṭibhiḥ |
dharmaśāstrapradīpo ‘yaṃ dhāryo mārgānudeśataḥ ||
[34] kudṛṣṭibhiḥ kudarśanaiḥ bauddhādibhiḥ |
yamaḥ |
vedāḥ pramāṇaṃ smṛtayaḥ pramāṇaṃ
dharmārthayuktaṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇam |
yasya pramāṇaṃ na bhavet pramāṇaṃ
kas tasya kuryād vacanaṃ pramāṇam ||
na tasya vedā na ca dharmaśāstraṃ
na vṛddhavākyaṃ hi bhavet pramāṇam |
so ‘dharmakṛd dṛṣṭihato durātmā
nātmāpi tasyeha bhavet pramāṇam ||
[vedāḥ pramāṇam iti] samudāyasya pramākaratvavivakṣayā ekavacanam | dharmārthayuktaṃ vacanam iti ca sadvacanābhiprāyam | yasya pramāṇaṃ ca bhavet pramāṇam iti anantaroktaṃ vedādipramāṇaṃ yo na manyata ity arthaḥ |
atra vasiṣṭhaḥ [1.16] |
traividyavṛddhā yaṃ brūyuḥ dharmaṃ darmavido janāḥ |
patane pāvane caiva sa dharmo nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
trividhā vidyā trividyā tāṃ vedety arthe ‘ṇpratyayaḥ | tisro vidyā vedeti tu kriyamāṇe “dvigor luk” (Pāṇini 4.1.88) iti lukprasaṅga iti mahābhāṣyaṭīkākāraḥ | atha vā traividye tisṛṣv api vidyāsu ye vṛddhāḥ jñānavantaḥ | traividyam iti [35] svārth ‘py aṇ | patane patananimitte pāpe, pāvane snānādau yaṃ dharmaṃ brūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ |
manuḥ [12.108, 110]:
anājñāteṣu dharmeṣu kathaṃ syād iti ced bhavet |
yaṃ śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ syād aśaṅkitaḥ ||
daśāvarā vā pariṣad yaṃ dharmaṃ parikalpayet |
tryavarā vāpi vṛttasthā taṃ dhamaṃ na vicālayet ||
daśāvarā daśāvare alpe yasyāṃ sā tathā | etenaikādaśādīn anujānāti | anājñāte daśāvaraiḥ śiṣṭair ūhāvidbhir alubdhaiḥ praśastaṃ kāryam | anājñāte anavagate saṃdigdhe vā |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
śrutivirodhe trividyasamavāyeṣu nirṇayaḥ daśāvareṣu | śrutigrahaṇaṃ smṛtyācāradharmapramāṇamārtopalakṣaṇārtham |
atha parṣallakṣaṇam
tatra manu-pārāśarau [Manu 12.11] |
traividyo hetukas tarkaḥ nairukto dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
[36]        trayaś cāśramiṇaḥ pūrve parṣat syāt sā daśāvarā ||
hetuko ‘tra vedāviruddhānumānavyavahārī | tarko vakṣyamāṇo vikalpaḥ |
vasiṣṭha-baudhāyanau |
cāturvidyo vikalpī ca aṅgavid dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
āśramasthās trayo mukhyāḥ parṣad eṣā daśāvarā ||
daśāvarā mukhyāḥ |
yamaḥ |
chandogo bahvṛco ‘dhvaryur atha vā dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
tathaivāśramiṇaḥ pūrve parṣad eṣā daśāvarā ||
vedapūtā vrate mlānā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
yad brūys te mahātmānas tat pramāṇaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
[37] śaṅkha-likhitau |
ṛgyajuḥsāmam atharvavedaṣaḍaṅgavid uñchavṛttir naiṣṭhiko brahmacārī pañcāgnir iti daśāvarā parṣat | tat samavāye dharmavyavaharaṇam |
naiṣṭhikagrahaṇam upakurvāṇavyavacchedārtham |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
alolupā hy adāmbhikāḥ darmaśāstrapraṇetāraḥ, amatsariṇaḥ, daśaiva tad dharmaṃ vyavasyantīti vedapāragāḥ |
praṇetāraḥ pravartayitāraḥ | atra itikaraṇo hetau | ity ataḥ vedapāragāḥ dharmaṃ vyavasyanti | daśeti puruṣābhiprāyam | na guṇabhedena guṇavatparam | guṇānām alpabhūyasāṃ tatra tatrābhidhānāt |
śakkha-likhitau |
śreyāṃś catvāraḥ vedavido dharmaśāstravid iti | pañcāvarām eke | daśāvareṣu dharmānuvyavaharaṇaṃ yathāvalepaṃ śāstratas te ‘nubrūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ |
yathāvalepaṃ yathādoṣam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur dharmapāṭhakāḥ |
vedapāraga ekaś ca tat pramāṇam aśaṃsayam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.9] |
catvāro vedadharmajñāḥ parṣat traividyam eva ca |
sā brūte yaṃ sa dharmaḥ syād eko vādhyātmavittamaḥ ||
sākṣātkṛtabrahmatatvaḥ |
[38]        vasiṣṭhaḥ [3.7] |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur dharmapāragāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ ||
aṅgirāḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi vedavanto ‘gnihotriṇaḥ |
brāhmaṇā samavāyasthāḥ parṣad ity abhidhīyate ||
anāhitāgnayo ye tu kevalaṃ vedapāragāḥ |
pañcaite vedaśāstrajñāḥ parṣatsaṃjñā hy udāhṛtāḥ ||
ye tu dharmavido viprāḥ kāryākāryavicakṣaṇāḥ |
prāyaścittapraṇetāraḥ saptaite parikīrtitāḥ ||
ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyātair mīmāṃsāvedapāragaiḥ |
vedāṅgakuśalaś caiva parṣatvaṃ prakalpayet ||
yatīnāṃ satyatapasāṃ jñānavijñānavedinām |
śirovratena snātānām eko ‘pi pariṣad bhavet ||
ye tu pūrvaṃ mayā proktāḥ sthitā yāyāvare pathi |
trayo ‘pi pariṣaj jñeyāḥ sarvapāpāpahāriṇaḥ ||
eṣā tu laghukāryeṣu madhyameṣu ca madhyamā |
mahāpātakasādhyeṣu śataśo bhūya eva ca ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu ye viprā kevalaṃ nāmabodhakāḥ |
pariṣattvaṃ na teṣv asti sahasraguṇiteṣv api ||
samavāyasthāḥ militāḥ | vedaśāstrajñā vedasya upakaraṇaṃ śāstraṃ mīmāṃsā tasyāṃ vihitaniṣiddhatattvajñāḥ | [39] guṇāpakarṣād ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyatvam | jñānaṃ tattvārthabodhaḥ | vijñānaṃ laukikavaidikavidyādhāraṇam | śirovrataṃ vedādhyayanasamāptau sarvavidyāvratam | pariṣad bhavet pariṣadvad bhavet | yady api “hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī” iti tair anugraho niṣiddhaḥ, tathāpi “ārtānāṃ mārgamāṇānām” ityādivacanād upasthitasya prāyaścittādāne doṣaśruteḥ śāstrānanumatānugrahaniṣedhaparatāpy asyeti gamyate pūrvaṃ “vedavanto ‘gnihotriṇaḥ” ity atra śloke | yāyāvarāḥ śiloñchādivṛttayo vrataśīlāgnihotriṇaḥ | laghuṣu svalpapāpeṣu | mahāpātakasādhyeṣu mahāpātakaśodhane anyat |
hārītaḥ |
cāturvidyāvid vikalpāṅgavid dharmavit trayāśramiṇa iti pariṣadaṃ daśāvarām āhuḥ | catvāraḥ tāsāṃ pūrvā śreyasyo bhavanti |
gautamaḥ [28.50–52] |
catvāraś caturṇāṃ pāragāḥ [vedānāṃ] prāguttamāḥ trayaḥ āśramiṇaḥ pṛthagdharmavidas traya etān daśa[ā?]varān [pariṣad ity ācakṣate] | asaṃbhave tv eteāṃ śrotriyo vedavic chiṣṭo vipratipattau [yad āha] | yato ‘yam aprabhavo bhūtānām |
[40] uttamāḥ pravrajitāḥ | vipratipattau ity atra yadā tat kāryam iti śeṣaḥ | ayam iti yogyatvāt vedaḥ parāmṛśyate | aprabhavaḥ apauruṣeyaḥ |
manu-vasiṣtha-yama-[parāśara]-baudhāyanāḥ, paiṭhīnasiś ca prathame |
avratānām amantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām |
sahasraśaḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate ||
yaṃ vadanti tamobhūtā mūrkhā dharmam atadvidaḥ |
tatpāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā tān vaktṝn anugacchati ||
aṅgirāḥ |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ pāragā ye dvijottamāḥ |
yathā vede vināpy aṅgaiś cāturvidyam iti smṛtam ||
dharmasya parṣad asyaiva prāyaścittakramasya ca |
trayāṇāṃ ca pramāṇajñaḥ sa vikalpī bhaved vijaḥ ||
[śabde chandasi kalpe ca śikṣāyāṃ ca suniṣṭhitaḥ | ]
jyotiṣām ayane caiva sanirukte ‘ṅgavid bhavet ||
vedavidyāvratasnātaḥ satyasaṃdho jitendriyaḥ |
[41]        anekadharmaśāstrajñaḥ procyate dharmapāṭhakaḥ ||
brahmacaryāśramād ūrdhvaṃ vṛddhā āśramiṇas trayaḥ |
vadeyus tasya te dharmaṃ ye mayā parikīrtitāḥ ||
tathā [Aṅgirāḥ 3.16] |
parṣad yā brāhmaṇānāṃ tu sā rājñāṃ dviguṇā smṛtā |
vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā caiva parṣadvac ca vrataṃ smṛtam ||
jyotiṣām ayane jyotiḥśāstre | sanirukte niruktasahite | idaṃ dvaiguṇyādi kṣatriyādīnāṃ śodhyānām | yac ca vrate dvaiguṇyam, tat pratilomotkṛṣṭavarṇavadhatatstrīgamanādiviṣaye coktam |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
parṣadvidhānam atraiva proktam āṅgirasena tu |
dviguṇā kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā smṛtā ||
caturguṇā tu śūdrāṇāṃ parṣad uktā mahātmanām |
parṣadvac ca vrataṃ proktaṃ śuddhaye pāpakarmaṇām ||
anena jāyate vīra varṇānām uttamād ṛte |
aghamānāṃ tu varṇānām utkṛṣṭahanane guha |
doṣo gurutaro jñeyaḥ kṣatrādīnāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
atha manuḥ (2.14–15) |
śrutidvaihaṃ tu yatra syāt tatra dharmāv ubhau smṛtau |
ubhāv api hi tau dharmau samyag uktau manīṣibhiḥ ||
[42]        udite ‘nudite caiva samayādhyuṣite tathā |
sarvathā vartate yajña itīyaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ ||
samayādhyuṣite uṣaḥkāle |
gautamaḥ [1.6] |
tulyabalavirodhe vikalpaḥ |
jābālaḥ |
śrutismṛtivirodhe tu śrutir eva garīyasī |
avirodhe sadā kāryaṃ smārtaṃ vaidikavat sadā ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
tulyaśrutivirodhe vikalpāḥ śrutayaḥ | śrutir hi garīyasī | sa āmnāyaḥ |
āmnāyatvena nirapekṣatvam uktam |
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃsk. 13] |
vedārthopanibandhatvāt prāmāṇyaṃ tu manoḥ smṛtam |
manvarthaviparītā tu yā smṛtiḥ sā na śasyate ||
vedārthaḥ upanibandho yasya sa vedārthopanibandhaḥ | upanibandhyate ‘nenety upanibandho granthaḥ | vedaś ca “yad vai kiṃcana manur avadat tad bheṣajaṃ bheṣajatāyai” iti |
[43]        gautamaḥ |
deśajātikuladharmāś cāmnāyair aviruddhāḥ pramāṇam |
āmnāyaiḥ vedādyaiḥ śrutismṛtiyādibhiḥ |
smṛtyanuvṛttau bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
śrutyā saha virodhe tu bādhyate viṣayaṃ vinā |
vyavasthayāvirodhena kāryo ‘nyatra parīkṣakaiḥ ||
smṛtyarthena virodhe tu arthaśāstrasya bādhanam |
parasparavirodhe tu nyāyayuktaṃ pramāṇavat ||
adṛṣṭārthe vikalpas tu vyavasthāsaṃbhave sati |
smṛtiśāstravikalpas tu ākāṅkṣāpūraṇe sati ||
avirodhe vyavasthāyā anyatra viṣayaḥ kārya ity arthaḥ | smṛtyarthena dharmaśāstrārthena | arthaśāstrasya manvādipraṇītasyaiva [44] rājanītyādeḥ | vyavasthāsaṃbhave vyavasthāyām asati adṛṣṭārthayoḥ vikalpaḥ | ākāṅkṣāpūraṇe etena sākāṅkṣatvena smṛtyādīnāṃ samuccayaḥ sūcitaḥ |
atha aṅgirāḥ |
pramāṇāni pramāṇajñaiḥ paripālyāni yatnataḥ |
sīdanti hi pramāṇāni pramāṇair avyavasthitaiḥ ||
pramāṇasahitaṃ yat syāt tat sarvaṃ gamyate sukham |
apramāṇaprameyasya duḥkhenādhigamo bhavat ||
atha nimittataḥ
tatra śaṅkha-likhitau |
tatra dharmalakṣaṇāni — deśaḥ kāla upāyo dravyaṃ śraddhā pātraṃ tyāga iti | samasteṣu dharmodayaḥ sādhāraṇo ‘nyathā viparītaḥ | śraddhāpātrasaṃpanno dharmaḥ kālāpekṣaḥ | śraddhādravyotpattir iti kālas tanmūlo deśaḥ |
deśaḥ brahmāvartādiḥ | kālaḥ saṃkrāntyādiḥ | upāyaḥ itikartavyatā | [dravyaṃ gavādi] | śraddhā śāstrārthe dṛḍhapratyayaḥ | śraddhā dṛḍhadravyotpattir iti kālaḥ | ayam api [45] saṃkrāntyādivad dānādidharmakālaḥ | tanmūlo deśaḥ | evaṃbhūtakālasaṃpattau deśo ‘pi dharmaḥ saṃpadyata ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [2.3.1] |
uttaraṃ yat samudrasya himādrer eva dakṣiṇa |
varṣaṃ tad bhārataṃ nām bhāratī yatra santataiḥ ||
tathā [2.3.23–25] |
atra janmasahasrāṇāṃ sahasrair api sattama |
kadācil labhate jantur mānuṣyaṃ puṇyasaṃcayāt ||
gāyanti devāḥ kila gītakāni
dhanyās tu ye bhāratabhūmibhāge |
svartāpavargāspadamārgabhūte
bhavanti bhūyaḥ puruṣāḥ suratvāt ||
karmāṇy asaṃkalpitatatphalāni
satyasya viṣṇoḥ paramātmabhūte |
avāpya tāṃ karmamahīm anante
tasmin layaṃ ye tv amalāḥ prayānti ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
brahmāvartaḥ paro deśaḥ ṛṣideśas tva anantaraḥ |
[46]        madhyadeśas tato nyūna āryāvartas tv anantaraḥ ||
anantaro īṣannyūnam | nañ īṣadarthe, alavaṇā yavāgūr itivat |
manuḥ [2.18–24] |
sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyor devanadyor yad antaram |
taṃ devanirmitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate ||
tasmin deśe ya ācāraḥ pāraṃparyakramāgataḥ |
varṇānāṃ sāntarālānāṃ sadācāraḥ sa ucyate ||
kurukṣetraṃ ca matsyāś ca pāñcālāḥ sūrasenakāḥ |
eṣa brahmarṣideśo vai brahmāvartād anantaraḥ ||
etaddeśaprasūtasya sakāśād agrajanmanaḥ |
svaṃ svaṃ caritraṃ śikṣeran pṛthivyāṃ sarvamānavāḥ ||
himavadvindhyyor madhye yat prāg vinaśanād api |
pratyag eva prayāgāc ca madhyadeśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
ā samudrāt tu vai pūrvād ā samudrāt tu paścimāt |
tayor evāntaraṃ giryor āryāvartaṃ pracakṣate ||
kṛṣṇasāras tu carati mṛgo yatra svabhavataḥ |
[47]        sa jñeyo yajñiyo deśo mlecchadeśas tv ataḥ param ||
etān dvijātayo deśān saṃśrayeran prayatnataḥ |
śudras tu yasmin tasmin vā nivased vṛttikarṣitaḥ ||
antarālāḥ saṃkarajātayaḥ | matsyādiśabdāḥ bahuvacanāntarā deśaparāḥ | matsyo virāṭadeśaḥ | pāñcālāḥ kānyakubjādideśāḥ | śūrasenajāḥ mathurādideśāḥ | agrajanmanaḥ brāhmaṇasya | vinaśanaṃ sarasvatyā nadyā antardhāndeśaḥ | svabhāvataḥ annapānādinā | yajñiyaḥ yajñārhaḥ | mlecchadeśaḥ yajñādyanarhaḥ | vṛttiḥ svakuṭumbabharaṇocitadhanopāyaḥ | tadabhāvena karṣitaḥ pīḍītaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [1.8–15] |
dharmaḥ āryāvarte prāgpardaśaṇāt pratyak kālakāvanād udak pāriyātrād dakṣiṇena himavataḥ uttareṇa vindhyādreḥ | [tasmin deśe] ye dharmā ye ca ācārās te sarve pratyetavyāḥ | na hy anye pratilomakakṣadharmāṇaḥ | etam āryāvartam ity ācakṣate | gaṅgāyamunayor antarāpy eke | yāvad vā kṛṣṇamṛgo vicarati tāvad brahmavarcasam ity anye | athāpi bhāllavino nidāne gāthām udāharanti |
[48]                paścāt sindhur vitaraṇī sūraysodayanaṃ puraḥ |
yāvat kṛṣṇo ‘bhidhāvati tāvad vai brahmavarcasam ||
[pratilomakakṣadharmāṇaḥ] viparītā asphuṭasvabhāvāḥ | kakṣaśabdena ‘nvarthasaṃjñāpradarśanārthaḥ | āryās trayo varṇāḥ, āvartante yasminn iti | tataś cāryair atraiva vastavyam | brahmavacasaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyasaṃpattiḥ | bhāllavinaḥ chandogaśākhinaḥ | nidānaṃ chandogasūtram | sindhur nadīviśeṣaḥ | vataraṇī janakaparvatasamīpe mālavadeśe paraśurāmeṇa dhanuḥ koṭyā kuṇḍaṃ bhittvā nirmiteti tatratyajanaprasiddhā |
śaṅkha-likhitau |
deśa āryo guṇavān prāg astād arvāg udayād yāvad uccāgirayo yāvat puṇyāḥ saritaḥ, prāk siddhatvād deśaḥ sanātanaḥ puṇyaḥ | kṛṣṇamṛgo vā yāvad vicarati tāvad ācāraḥ syāt | prāk sindhusauvīrād dakṣiṇena himavataḥ, paścāt kāmpilyād udak pariyātrād anavadyaṃ brahmavarcasaṃ sarveṣām ato anyadeśajātikula-dharmaṇām anvayāḥ |
anavadyam avikalam | anvayāḥ santānāḥ | pratyetavyā iti śeṣaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ā himavataḥ, ā ca kumāryāḥ, sindhur vaitaraṇī nadi, sūryasyodayanaṃ puraḥ, yāvad vā kṛṣṇamṛgo vicarati tatra dharmaḥ catuṣpādo vicarati |
[49] saṃvartaḥ |
svabhāvād yatra viramet kṛṣṇasāraḥ sadā mṛgaḥ |
dharmadeśaḥ sa vijñeyo dvijānāṃ dharmasādhanaḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [84.1–4] |
na mlecchaviṣaye śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | na gacchen mlecchaviṣayam | paranipāteṣv apaḥ pītvā tatsāmyam upagacchati |
cāturvarṇyavyavasthānaṃ yasmin deśe na dṛśyate |
taṃ mlecchadeśaṃ jānīyād āryāvartam ataḥ param ||
baudhāyanaḥ [1.2.14] |
ānartakāṅgamagadhāḥ surāṣṭrā dakṣiṇāpathāḥ |
upāvṛtsindhusauvīrā ete saṃkīrṇayonayaḥ ||
āraṭṭān kāraskarān puṇḍrān sauvīrān vaṅgān kaliṅgān prāsūnān iti ca gatvā punastomena yajeta sarvapṛṣṭhayā vā | athāpy udāharanti |
padbhyāṃ sa kurute pāpaṃ yaḥ kaliṅgān prapadyate |
ṛṣayo niṣkṛtiṃ tasya prāhur vaiśvānaraṃ haviḥ ||
ānartakaḥ dvārakā yasmin deśe | aṅgaḥ campāpradeśaḥ |
[50]
|| 2 ||
atha paribhāṣā
tatra bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
samyak saṃsādhanaṃ karma kartavyam adhikāriṇā |
niṣkāmena sadā vīra kāmyaṃ kāmānvitena tu ||
ācārayuktaḥ śraddhāvān vedajño ‘dhyātmavittamaḥ |
karmaṇāṃ phalam āpnoti nyāyārjitadhanaś ca yaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ | saṃsādhanaṃ yathāvihitasādhanam | adhikāriṇā arthinā viduṣā samarthena | samyak [yathāsāmarthyam] prathamakalpādinā karma kartavyam | kāmye kāmānvitatvaṃ viśeṣa iti | ācāro manvādibhir ukto ‘tithipūjādi | adhyātmavittamaḥ paralokabhāginy ātmani dṛḍhapratyayavān | nyāyārjitadhanaḥ svavṛttyārjitakarmāṅgadhanaḥ | phalaṃ saṃpūrṇam iti śeṣaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.29.1–2] |
prayojayitānumantā karteti svarganarakaphaleṣu bhāginaḥ | yo bhūya ārabhate tasmin phalaviśeṣaḥ |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
vidhidṛṣṭaṃ ca yat karma karoty avidhinā tu yaḥ |
[51]        phalaṃ na kiṃcid āpnoti kleśamātraṃ tu tasya tat ||
vidhidṛṣṭaṃ yathāvihitam |
manuḥ [11.31] |
prabhuḥ prathamakalpasya yo ‘nukalpena vartate |
na sāṃparāyikaṃ tasya durmater vidyate phalam ||
prabhuḥ samarthaḥ | sāṃparāyikaṃ pāralaukikam |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.3.1–3] |
akriyā trividhā proktā vidvadbhiḥ karmakāriṇām |
akriyā ca paroktā ca tṛtīyā vāyathākriyā ||
svaśākhāśrayam utsṛjya paraśākhāśrayaṃ ca yaḥ |
kartum icchati durmedhā moghaṃ tat tasya yat kṛtam ||
yan nāmnātaṃ svaśākhāyāṃ paroktam arirodhi yat |
vidvadbhis tad anyṣtheyam agnihotrādikarmavat ||
paroktā paraśākhoktā | ayathākriyā vihitetaraprakāreṇānuṣṭhānam | [yath] agnihotraṃ yajurvedaśākhāsu vihitaṃ chandogādibhir aniṣthīyate | śākhāntarādhikāraṇanyāyena sarvasmṛtiṣu yathāpratyayatve gṛhyeṣv api prasakte ‘pavādam āha |
gṛhyapariśiṣṭakāraḥ |
bahv alpaṃ vā svagṛhyoktaṃ yasya yāvat prakīrtitam |
tasya tāvati śāstrārthe kṛte sarvaḥ kṛto bhavet ||
yathā [chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ] – [1.3.4–6] |
pravṛttam anyathā kuryād yadi mohāt kathaṃcana |
yatas tadanyathābhūtaṃ tata eva samāpayet ||
samāpte yadi jānīyān mayaitad ayathākṛtam |
tāvad eva punaḥ kuryān nāvṛttiḥ sarvakarmaṇaḥ ||
pradhānasyākriyā yatra sāṅgaṃ tat kriyate punaḥ |
tadaṅgasyākriyāyāṃ tu nāvṛttir na ca tatkriyā ||
pravṛttaṃ prārabdham | anyathā viparītam | tatkarmakaraṇe saty eva kramādyanyatvena, yato yasmāt padā[rthā]d ārabhya tatkarmānyathā jātaṃ ta[ta] evārabhya punar yathoktaprakāreṇa kuryād ity arthaḥ | samāpte iti, samāpte tu karmaṇi anyathākaraṇajñāne sati, yat padārthānyathākaraṇaṃ jñā[taṃ] tam eva padārthaṃ punaḥ kuryāt | na punas taduttarān api padārthān | etad anyathākaraṇaviṣayam | pradhānasyetyādi tv akaraṇaviṣayam | pradhānasya karmaṇaḥ yatrākaraṇaṃ bhavati tat sāṅgam eva punaḥ kartavyam | tadaṅgasya vākaraṇe na sāṅgapradhānāvṛttir nāpi tāvanmātrasyāṅgasya karaṇam, kiṃ tu prāyaścittam eva kāryam |
[53]        hārītaḥ |
aṅguṣṭhasyottarā rekhā brāhmaṃ tīrtham, kaniṣṭhikāyāḥ, paścāt prājāpatyam, agre ‘ṅgulīnāṃ daivam, aṅguṣṭhapradeśinyor antarā pitryam, madhye āgneyam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [3.66] |
aṅgulyagreṣu mānuṣam |
śaṅkhaḥ |
kāyaṃ kaniṣṭhikāmūle tīrtham uktaṃ dvijasya tu |
aṅguṣṭhamūle ca tathā prājāpatyaṃ prakīrtitam ||
kāyaṃ ko brahmā sāsya devatety arthe “kasyet” ity aṇ itvaṃ ca |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
madhye saumyaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
madhye pāṇimadhye |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.8.12–13] |
aṅgulimūle [brāhmaṃ tīrtham | aṅguṣṭhāgraṃ pitryam aṅgulyagraṃ] deivam aṅgulyagra[?limūla]m ārṣam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
[aṅguṣṭhamūlasyottarataḥ prāgagrāyāṃ rekhāyāṃ brāhmaṃ tīrtham, [54] pradeśinyaṅguṣṭhayor antarā pitryam, kaniṣṭhikātalayor antaraṃ prājāpatyam, pūrveṇāṅguliparvāṇi daivam |
evaṃ ca kanīnakāmūlasya] tīrthasya prājāpatyakāyadaivasaṃjñāḥ, āgneyasaumyabrāhmatīrthasaṃjñā hastamadhyasya, aṅgulyagrasya daivamānuṣārṣatīrthasaṃjñāḥ, [aṅguṣṭhamūlasya prājāpatyabrāhmatīrthasaṃjñe, taduttararekhayā brahmatīrthasaṃjñā], tarjanyaṅguṣṭhamadhyasya pitṛtīrthasaṃjñety uktaṃ bhavati |
manuḥ [2.58] |
brāhmeṇa vipras tīrthena nityakālam upaspṛśet |
kāyatraidaśikābhyāṃ vā na pitryeṇa kadācana ||
traidaśikaṃ daivam |
hārītaḥ |
[upasparśanaṃ brāhmeṇa] ācamanahomatarpaṇāni prājāpatyena kuryāt | mārjanārcanabalikarmabhojanāni daivena | pitryarthān pitryeṇa | pratigraham āgreyena pratigṛhṇīyāt |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
kamaṇḍalūpasparśanaṃ dadhiprāśanam eva ca |
somatīrthena rājendra sadā kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
[55]        mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.109] |
nāndīmukhānāṃ kurvīta prājñaḥ piṇḍodakakriyām |
prājāpatyena tīrthena yac ca kiṃcit prajāpateḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
[mūlarekhāmukhāṅguṣṭhamaṇibandheṣu madhyagam |]
prājāpatyaṃ mahātīrthaṃ vipras tenācamet sadā ||
dhanāyurdārarekhāsu somatīrthaṃ tu madhyamam |
jālādihavanaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ vapanaṃ tathā ||
vapanaṃ vrīhyādinirvāpaḥ |
[chāgaleyaḥ] |
hastamadhye brahmatīrthaṃ dakṣiṇāgrahaṇe ca yat |
oṃkāra ity anuvṛttau, āpastambaḥ [1.13.8–9] |
yajñeṣu caitadādayaḥ prasavāḥ |
praṇaya ityādayaḥ prasavā anujñānavākyāni |
[loke ca bhūtikarmasu etadādīny eva vākyāni syuḥ yathā puṇyāhaṃ svasty ṛddham iti |
bhūtikarmasu pāṇigrahaṇādiṣu | etadādīni oṃkārādīni svastyādivacanāni bhaveyuḥ ||]
[56]        [mahābhārate] |
tasmād om ity udāhṛtya yajñadānatapaḥkriyāḥ |
pravartante vidhānoktāḥ satataṃ brahmavādinām ||
tathā |
trimātras tu prayoktavyaḥ karmārambheṣu sarvaśaḥ |
tisraḥ sārdhās tu kartavyā mātrāḥ sarvārthacintakaiḥ ||
devatādhyānakāleṣu plutaṃ kuryān na saṃśayaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
puṇyāhavācanaṃ daive brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
etad eva niroṃkāraṃ brūyāt kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [64.40] |
snāto ‘dhikārī bhavati daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
pavitrāṇāṃ tathā japye dāne ca vidhinodite ||
pavitrāṇāṃ mantrāṇāṃ pāvamānyādīnām | “mantraḥ pavitram ity ucyate” [5.6] iti niruktakāravacanāt |
vāyupurāṇe |
kriyāṃ yaḥ kurute home dhautavastradharo bhavet |
alaṃkṛtaḥ śucir maunī śrāddhādau vijitendriyaḥ ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2.15.5] |
kāṣāyavāsāḥ [yān] kurute japahomaparigrahān |
[57]        na tad devagamaṃ bhavati havyakavyeṣ atho haviḥ ||
kāṣāyavāsāḥ vṛkṣakvāthādinā raktavāsāḥ |
vyāsaḥ |
ārdravāsās tu yaḥ kuryāj japahomaparigraham |
sarvaṃ tad rākṣasaṃ vidyād bahirjānu ca yat kṛtam ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
paridhānād bahiḥkakṣā nibaddhā hy āsurī bhavet |
dharmakarmāṇi vidvadbhir varjanīyā prayatnataḥ ||
bahiḥkakṣā bahirnirgatety arthaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.20] |
homadevārcanādyās tu kriyāsv ācamane tathā |
naikavastraḥ pravarteta dvijavācanake jape ||
dvijavācanake dvijasvastivācanādau |
śātātapaḥ |
savyād aṃsāt paribhraṣṭaḥ kaṭideśe dhṛtāmbaraḥ |
ekavastraṃ tu taṃ vidyād daive pitrye ca varjayet ||
śāṭyāyanaḥ |
dānam ācamanaṃ homaṃ bhojanaṃ devatārcanam |
[58]        prauḍhapādo na kurvīta svādhyāyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
āsanārūḍhapādas tu jānunor jaṅghayos tathā |
kṛtāvasakthiko yas tu prauḍhapādaḥ sa ucyate ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
sūryodayaṃ vinā naiva snānadānādikāḥ kriyāḥ |
agner viharaṇaṃ caiva kratus tāvan na lakṣyate ||
sūryodayaṃ vinā iti sūryodayaśabdena uṣaḥkālo lakṣyate | taṃ vinā rātrau na kāryaṃ snānadānādīti tātparyārthaḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
devakāryāṇi pūrvāhṇe manuṣyāṇāṃ tu madhyame |
pitṝṇām aparāhṇe ca kāryāṇīti viniścayaḥ ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.1.6–8] |
yatropadiśyate karma kartur aṅgaṃ na tūcyate |
dakṣiṇas tatra vijñeyaḥ karmaṇāṃ pāragaḥ karaḥ ||
[59]        yatra diṅniyamo nāsti japahomādikarmasu |
tisras tatra tu vijñeyā aindrīsaumyāparājitāḥ ||
āsīna ūrdhvaḥ prahvo vā niyamo yatra nedṛśaḥ |
tad āsīnena kartavyaṃ na prahveṇa na tiṣṭhatā ||
saumyā uttarā aparājitā aiśānī |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
ārṣaṃ chandaś ca daivatyaṃ viniyogas tathaiva ca |
veditavyaṃ prayatnena brāhmaṇena viśeṣataḥ ||
aviditvā tu yaḥ kuryād yājanādhyāpanaṃ tathā |
homam antarjalādīni tasya cālpaphalaṃ bhavet ||
antarjalādīni jalamadhye kriyamāṇāny aghamarṣaṇādīni |
tathā |
yaś ca jānāti tattvena ārṣaṃ chandaś ca daivatam |
viniyogaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca mantrārthaṃ jñānakarma ca ||
jñānarūpā kriyā jñānakarma | anuṣṭheyārthasmaraṇaṃ prayojanam iti yāvat |
ekaikasya ṛṣeḥ so ‘pi vandyo hy atithivad bhavet |
devatāyāś ca sāyujyaṃ gacchaty atra na saṃśayaḥ ||
sāyujyaṃ sahayogitvam |
[60]        pūrvoktena prakāreṇa ṛṣyādīn vetti yo dvijaḥ |
adhikāro bhavet tasya rahasyādiṣu karmasu ||
[rahasyādiṣu] rahasyajapādiṣu |
yena yad ṛṣiṇā dṛṣṭaṃ siddhiḥ prāptā ca yena vai |
mantreṇa tasya tat proktaṃ ṛṣer bhāvas tad ārṣakam ||
chādanāt chanda uddiṣṭaṃ vāsasī iva cākṛteḥ |
ātmā saṃchādito devair mṛtyor bhītais tu vai purā ||
ādityair vasubhī rudrais tena chandāṃsi tāni vai |
ākṛteḥ śarīrasaṃsthānasya |
[yasya yasya tu mantrasya uddiṣṭā devatā tu yā |
tadākāraṃ bhavet tasya devatvaṃ devatocyate ||]
yasya yasya tu mantrasya yā yā devatā yena yenendrādirūpeṇoddiṣṭā prādhānyena pratipāditā tadākāram eva [tasya mantrasya] devatvam | tasyeti [anena] vyavahitena mantrasyety anena yojyam | tenaitad uktaṃ bhavati | tasya tasya mantrasya sā vai devatā |
[yogiyājñavalkyaḥ] |
purā kalpe samutpannā mantrāḥ karmārtham eva ca |
anenedaṃ tu kartavyaṃ viniyogaḥ sa ucyate ||
niruktaṃ yatra mantrasya viniyogaḥ prayojanam |
pratiṣṭhānaṃ stutiś caiva brāhmaṇaṃ tad ihocyate ||
[61] pratiṣṭhānaṃ viniyogaḥ | anye tu pratiṣṭhānaṃ āśraya iti vadanti | “agnīṣomapraṇayinīnām ṛcām asāv āditya ekaviṃśati uttamā pratiṣṭha” iti taittirīyabrāḥmaṇe |
tathā |
evaṃ pañcavidhaṃ yogaṃ japyakāle hy anusmaret |
home cāntarjale yoge svādhyāye yājane tathā ||
yogam ṛṣyādisaṃbandham | antarjale antarjalakārye snānādau | yoge yogāṅgapraṇavajapādau |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
sadopavītinā bhāvyaṃ sadā baddhaśikhena tu |
viśikho vyupavītaś ca yat karoti na tat kṛtam ||
āpastambaḥ [1.15.1] |
upāsane gurūṇāṃ vṛddhānām atithīnāṃ homejyakarmaṇi bhojane ācamane svādhyāye ca yajñopavītī syāt |
nigamapariśiṣṭe |
vāmaskandhe yajñopavītī daive, prācīnāvītam itarathā pitṛyajñe, nābhyāṃ dvikaṇṭhā[va?]saktam abhicāre, nivītaṃ āpṛṣṭhadeśāvalambi grāmyadharmeṣu |
itarathā vāmetaraskandhe | dvikaṇṭhāvasaktaṃ kaṇṭhapārśvadvayāvalambitam |
[62] baudhāyanaḥ [1.8.5–8] |
yajñopavītam ā nābher dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum avadhāya śiro ‘vadadhyāt | viparītaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ | kaṇṭhāvasaktaṃ nivītam adho’vasaktam adhovītam |
manu-baudhāyana-paiṭhīnasayaḥ |
agnyāgāre gavāṃ goṣṭhe brāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃnidhau |
svādhyāye bhojane caiva dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim uddharet ||
āpastambaḥ |
devāgāre tathā śrāddhe gavāṃ goṣṭhe tathādhvare |
dāne hy ācamane caiva saṃdhyayoḥ sādhusaṃgame ||
agnyāgāre vivāhe ca svādhyāye bhojane tathā |
uddhared dakṣiṇāṃ pāṇiṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kṛpāpathe ||
uddhared dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim iti savye aṃse vastraṃ nidhāya dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uttarīyād bahiḥ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.10.18] |
karmayukto nābhe adhaṃ sparśaṃ varjayet |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
raudrapitryāsurān mantrāṃs tathā caivābhicārakān |
[63]        vyāhṛtyālabhya cātmānam apaḥ spṛṣṭvānyad ācaret ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.2.13–14] |
pitryamantrānudravaṇe ātmālambhe avekṣaṇe |
aghovāyusamutsarge prahāse ‘nṛtabhāṣaṇe ||
mārjāramūṣikāsparśe ākruṣṭe krodhasaṃbhave |
nimitteṣu ca sarvatra karma kurvann apaḥ spṛśet ||
pitryamantrānudravaṇe pitryamantroccāraṇe | ātmālambhe ātmādhiṣṭhānahṛdayasparśe yajñādau vihite | avekṣaṇe tasyaiva [hṛdayasyaiva] | etad ubhayaṃ yajñādau vihitam | ākruṣṭe paruṣabhāṣaṇe |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
yadi vāgyamalopaḥ syāj japādiṣu kathaṃcana |
vyāhared vaiṣṇavaṃ mantraṃ smared vā viṣṇum avyayam ||
tathā |
ajñānād atha vā mohāt pracyavetādhvareṣu tat |
[64]        smaraṇād eva tad viṣṇoḥ saṃpūrṇaṃ syād iti śrutiḥ ||
mohāt viparyayajñānāt |
tathā |
na kuryāt kasyacit pīḍāṃ karmaṇā manasā girā |
ācarann abhiṣekaṃ tu karmāṇy anyāni cācaran ||
baudhāyanaḥ [1.3.40]
bhojanaṃ havanaṃ dānam upahāraḥ parigrahaḥ |
bahirjānu na kāryāṇi tadvad ācamanaṃ smṛtam ||
upahāraḥ devatādyartham annādyupaḍhaukanam |
aṅgirāḥ |
saṃdhyayor ubhayor japye bhojane dantadhāvane |
pitṛkārye ca daive ca tathā mūtrapurīṣayoḥ ||
gurūṇāṃ saṃnidhau dāne yoge caiva viśeṣataḥ |
eṣu maunaṃ samātiṣṭhan svargaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
dānaṃ pratigraho homo bhojanaṃ balir eva ca |
sāṅguṣṭhena sadā kāryam asurebhyo ‘nyathā bhavet ||
sāṅguṣṭhena aṅgulīsahitāṅguṣṭhena |
etāny eva tu karmāṇi dānādīni viśeṣataḥ |
antarjānu viśeṣeṇa tadvad ācamanaṃ nṛpa ||
[65] antarjānu jānunor madhye yathā bāhū bhavataḥ tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.2.2; 2.1.2–3] |
haritā yajñiyā darbhāḥ pītakāḥ pañcayajñiyāḥ |
samūlāḥ pitṛdaivatyāḥ kalmāṣā vaiśvadevikāḥ ||
[savye pāṇau kuśān kṛtvā kuryād ācamanakriyām |
hṛsvāḥ pracaraṇīyāḥ syuḥ kuśā dīrghās tu barhiṣaḥ ||
darbhāḥ pavitram ity uktam ataḥ saṃdhyādikarmasu |
savyaḥ sopagrahaḥ kāryo dakṣiṇaḥ sapavitrakaḥ ||]
pracaraṇīyāḥ pracaraṇam anuṣṭhānaṃ tatsādhanībhūtāḥ tattadavasthāpannāś caitat kāryaviśeṣaviniyuktāḥ | ataḥ saṃdhyādikarmasu darbhamātraṃ pavitram |
tathā hi [1.2.10–12] |
anantagarbhiṇaṃ sāgraṃ kauśaṃ dvidalam eva ca |
prādeśamātraṃ vijñeyaṃ pavitraṃ yatra kutracit ||
etad eva hi puñjūlyā lakṣaṇaṃ samudāhṛtam |
ājyasyotpavanārthe yat tad apy etāvad eva tu ||
[66]        etat pramāṇam evaike kauśīm evārdramañjarīm |
śuṣkāṃ vā śīrṇakusumāṃ puñjūlīṃ paricakṣate ||
gṛhyapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
[darbhāḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ mantrā brāhmaṇā havir agnayaḥ |]
ayātayāmāny etāni niyojyāni punaḥ punaḥ ||
ayātayāmaṃ navam | etena karmārhatā lakṣyate |
[marīciḥ] |
ye ca piṇḍāstṛtā darbhā yaiḥ kṛtaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam |
amedhyāśuciniṣṭhā ye teṣāṃ tyāgo vidhīyate ||
marīciḥ |
māse nabhasyam āvāsyā tasyāṃ darbhoccayo mataḥ |
ayātayāmās te darbhā viniyojyāḥ punaḥ punaḥ ||
[67]        laghuhārītaḥ |
citidarbhāḥ patidarbhā ye darbhā yajñabhūmiṣu |
staraṇāsanapiṇḍeṣu ṣaṭ kuśān parivarjayet ||
piṇḍārthaṃ ye stṛtā darbhā yaiḥ kṛtaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam |
mūtrocchiṣṭaiḥ kṛtā ye tu teṣāṃ tyāgo vidhīyate ||
[kecit tu] |
nīvīmadhye tu ye darbhāḥ brahmasūtre tu ye kṛtāḥ |
pavitrāṃs tān vijānīyād yathā kāyās tathā kuśāḥ ||
nīvī paridhānavastragranthiḥ |
[laghuhārītaḥ] |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ pavitreṇa dvijottamaḥ |
nocchiṣṭas tu bhavet tatra bhuktaśeṣaṃ tu varjayet ||
jape home tathā dāne svādhyāye pitṛtarpaṇe |
aśūnyaṃ tu karaṃ kuryāt suvarṇarajataiḥ kuśaiḥ ||
darbhahīnā tu yā saṃdhyā yac ca dānaṃ vinodakam |
asaṃkhyātaṃ tu yaj japtaṃ tat sarvaṃ niṣprayojanam ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.8.17–18] |
nāṅguṣṭhād adhikā kāryā samit sthūlatayā kvacit |
na niyuktā tvacā caiva na sakīṭā na pāṭitā ||
[68]        prādeśān nādhikā nonā na tathā syād viśākhikā |
na saparṇā na nirvīryā homeṣu ca vijānatā ||
viśākhikā vividhaśākhāyuktā |
samid iti anuvṛttau brahmapurāṇe |
pālāśāśvatthanyagrodhaplakṣavaikaṅkatodbhavāḥ |
aśvattodumbaro bilvaś candanaḥ saralas tathā ||
śālaś ca devadāruś ca khadiraś ceti yajñiyāḥ |
vaikaṅkataḥ śruvāvṛkṣaḥ |
marīciḥ |
prāgagrāḥ samidho deyās tāś ca kāmyeṣv apāṭitāḥ |
kāmyeṣu ca saśākārdrā viparītā jighāṃsataḥ ||
viśīrṇā vidalā hrasvā vakrā sasuṣirāḥ kṛśāḥ |
dīrghāḥ sthūlā ghuṇair juṣṭāḥ karmasiddhivināśakāḥ ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [ii, p. 33] |
yatoktavastvasaṃpattau grāhyaṃ tadanukāri ca |
yavānām iva godhūmā vrīhīṇām iva śālayaḥ ||
vrīhīṇāṃ ṣaṣṭikānām |
[tathā 1.7.16] |
ājyaṃ dravyam anādeśe juhotiṣu vidhīyate |
[69]        mantrasya devatāyāś ca prajāpatir iti sthitiḥ ||
anādeśe anabhidhāne | mantrasya devatāyāś ca anādeśa ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | prajāpatir devatā [yataḥ] samastā vyāhṛtayaś ca [prājāpatyāḥ] |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kāṇḍamūlaparṇapuṣpaphalapraroharasagandhādīnāṃ sādṛśyena pratinidhiṃ kuryāt | sarvālābhe yavaḥ pratinidhir bhavati |
kāṇḍaṃ nālam | prarohaḥ aṅkuraḥ |
maitrāyaṇīyapariśiṣṭam |
dakṣiṇālābhe mūlānāṃ [sarveṣāṃ] phalānāṃ bhakṣyāṇāṃ vā dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | na tv evaṃ yajeta |
tathā |
darbhāstaraṇābhāve kāśaḥ pratinidhiḥ tadalābhe parvavatībhiḥ kāṇḍavatībhir oṣadhībhiḥ staraṇārthān kurvantīti | kaṭośīraśuṇṭhanalabalvajapalālolapavarjam |
parvavatībhiḥ granthimatībhiḥ | kaṭādayas tṛṇaviśeṣāḥ |
[70]
atha idhmārthe pālāśāśvatthakhadirarohitakaudumbarāṇāṃ tadalābhe sarvavanaspatīnāṃ tilvakadhavanīpanimbarājavṛkṣaśālmalybandhakapittha-kovidāravibhītakaśleṣmātakasarvakaṇṭakivarjam |
tilvakaḥ cillaka iti prasiddhaḥ | nīpaḥ kadambaḥ | rājavṛkṣaḥ kiravālaiti prasiddhaḥ | śleṣmāntakaḥ bahuvārakaḥ |
vrīhiyavau puroḍāśarthe | tadalābhe tuṣavatībhis taṇḍulavatībhir oṣadhībhiḥ puroḍāśān kurvanti | aṇucīnakalmāṣakakuravakamāṣamasūrakodravakoradūṣaka-varjam |
purṣāśān puroḍāśārthaṇ | aṇavaḥ markaṭakāḥ | kuravakaḥ pītakulatthakaḥ | koradūṣakaḥ vanakodravaḥ |
ghṛtam ājyārthe pratinidhis tadalābhe dadhi payo vā | taṇḍulapiṣṭāni yavapiṣṭāni vādbhiḥ saṃspṛṣṭāni [ājyārthā]n kurvanti |
vāyupurāṇe |
kaṇḍanaṃ peṣaṇaṃ caiva tathaivollekhanaṃ sadā |
sakṛd eva pitṝṇāṃ syād devānāṃ ca trir ucyate ||
[71] ullekhanaṃ hy aparāṅmukhatvena rekhākaraṇam |
yajñapārśvaḥ |
pitryarthaṃ nirvaped bhūmau sūrye vā darbhasaṃyute |
yajñapārśvaḥ |
garbhādhānādi sarveṣu brāhmaṇān bhojayed daśa |
āvasathye trayoviṃśaty agnyādheye daśāt param ||
āgrayaṇe prāyaścitte brāhmaṇān daśa pañca ca |
sahasraṃ bhojayet some brāhmaṇānāṃ śataṃ paśau ||
cāturmāsye tu catvāri tathā pañca surāgrahe |
ayutaṃ vājapeye tu aśvamedhe caturguṇam ||
catvāri śatānīti saṃbandhaḥ |
chandopariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1.9.11–15] |
pāṇyāhutir dvādaśaparvapūrikā
kaṃsādinā cet sruvapūramātrikā |
daivena tīrthena ca hūyate haviḥ
svaṅgāriṇi svarciṣi tac ca pāvake ||
yo ‘narciṣi juhoty agnau vyaṅgāriṇi ca mānavaḥ |
mandāgnir āmayāvī ca daridraś copajāyate ||
[72]        tasmāt samiddhe hotavyaṃ nāsamiddhe kadācana |
vyaṅgāriṇi vigatāṅgāre |
ārogyam icchatāyuś ca śriyam ātyantikīṃ tathā ||
juhūṣaṃś ca hute caiva pāṇiśūrpasphyadārubhiḥ |
na kuryād agnidhamanaṃ na kuryād vyajanādinā ||
mukhenaiva dhamed agniṃ mukhād dhy eṣo ‘dhyajāyata |
nāgniṃ mukheneti ca yal laukike yojayanti tat ||
juhūṣan hotum icchan |
[73]
