atha saṃskāraḥ
atra manuḥ [2.26] |
vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyaiḥ niṣekādir dvijanmanām |
kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāraḥ pāvanaḥ pretya ceha ca ||
vaidikaiḥ vihitābhimarśanādibhiḥ | puṇyaiḥ atiśayajanakaiḥ | śarīrasaṃskāraḥ śarīraśabdo ‘tra ajahatsvārthalakṣaṇayā tatkāraṇe ‘pi vartate | pāvanaḥ pāpanibarhaṇaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.10] |
brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdrā varṇās tv ādyās trayo dvijāḥ |
niṣekādyāḥ śmāśānāntās teṣāṃ vai mantrataḥ kriyā ||
dvir jāyata iti dvijaḥ | dvitīyaṃ janma upanayanam | śmaśānāntāḥ tatsaṃbandhi karma ca |
upanayanapūrvasaṃskārānuvṛttau yamaḥ |
śūdro ‘py evaṃvidhaḥ kāryo vinā mantreṇa saṃskṛtaḥ |
na kenacit samasṛjan chandasā taṃ prajāpatiḥ ||
[74] vinā mantreṇety atra hetuḥ na kenacid ityādiḥ | chandasā vedena | samasṛjan saṃbandhaṃ janitavān |
brahmapurāṇe |
vivāhamātraṃ saṃskāraṃ śūdro ‘pi labhatāṃ sadā |
vivāhamātraṃ ity anena prāptaḥ vivāhavidhiḥ | mātraśabdas tu vihitetarasaṃskāranivṛttyarthaḥ | amantrās tu avidyatvād devatejyānivṛttayaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.13] |
tūṣṇīm etāḥ kriyāḥ strīṇāṃ vivāhas tu samantrakaḥ |
aṅgirāḥ |
citrakarma yathānekaiḥ aṅgair unmīlyate śanaiḥ |
brāhmaṇyam api tadvat syāt saṃskārair vidhipūrvakaiḥ ||
manuḥ [2.27] |
gārbhair homaiḥ jātakarmair cauḍamauñjīnibandhanaiḥ |
baijikaṃ gārbhikaṃ caino dvijānām apamṛjyate ||
[75] baijikaṃ śukrabhavam | gārbhikaṃ garbhāśayaḥ śroṇī tadbhavam | enaḥ aśucitvam |
hārītaḥ |
garbhādānavad upeto brahmagarbhaṃ saṃdadhāti | puṃsavanāt puṃsīkaroti | phalasnapanāt mātāpitṛjaṃ pāpam aphohati | brāhmanyaretotthagarbhopaghātaḥ pañcaguṇaḥ | jātakarmaṇā prathamam aphohati | namakaraṇena dvitīyam | prāśanena tṛtīyam | cūḍākaraṇena caturtham | snānena pañcamam | vratair aṣṭabhir garbhasaṃskāraiḥ garbhopaghātāt pūto bhavati | upanayanādibhir aṣṭābhir antarvrataiḥ cāṣṭābhiḥ | svacchandasaṃmitā brāhmaṇāḥ paraṃ pātraṃ devapitṝṇāṃ bhavanti | chandasāṃ vāyatanam |
garbhādhānavat garbhādhānavidhinā “viṣṇur yoniṃ kalpayatu” ityādinā | upetaḥ striyam upagataḥ | brahmagarbhaṃ saṃdadhātīti brahma vedas tadgrahaṇayogyaṃ garbhaṃ saṃdadhāti saṃjanayati | puṃsīkaroti avyaktaliṅgagarbhaṃ pumāṃsaṃ karoti, “pumāṃsaṃ putraṃ vidanva” iti mantravarṇāt | phalasnapanāt phalasnapanāṅgakasīmantonnayanāt | mātāpitṛjaṃ mātāpitṛdvārā jātam | garbhasthasya āśrayasthasya āśrayasaṃskāradvārā apatyasaṃskāraḥ | upaghātaḥ pāpasaṅghaḥ | pañcaguṇaḥ pañcaprakāraḥ | “upapātakajātibhraṃśakarasaṃkaraṇā-pātrīkaraṇamalinīkaraṇarūpapāpapañcakasaṃkrānteti” jayasvāmī | snānaṃ cātra samāvartanam | aṣṭau vratacaryāḥ upanayanādi mekhaletyādi ūrdhvaretā ity atra vakṣyamāṇāḥ | antarvratāni sāvitrīgodānakādityavrataupaniṣadaṣṭakāmāsikamahānāmnīmahāvratikāni | svacchandaḥ saṃmitāḥ svavedayuktāḥ |
[76]        sumantuḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ garbhādhāna-sīmantonnayana-jātakarma-nāmakaraṇa-annaprāśana-cūḍopanayana-vratacaryā-adhyayana-samāvartana-vivāha-yajñādīni samānāni |
gautamaḥ [8.13–19] |
garbhādhāna[puṃsavana]sīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇānnaprāśana-caulopanayanam, catvāri vedavratāni, snānam, sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogaḥ, pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānam, devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtabrahmaṇām eteṣāṃ ca | aṣṭakā pārvaṇaḥ śrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇyāgrahāyanī caitryāśvayujīti pākayajñasaṃsthāḥ | agnyādheyam agnihotraṃ darśapūrṇamāsāv āgrayaṇaṃ cāturmāsyāni nirūḍhapaśubandhaḥ sautrāmaṇīti sapta haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ | [agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo ‘tirātro ‘ptoryāma iti sapta somasaṃsthāḥ] | ity ete catvāriṃśat saṃskārāḥ |
catvāri vedavratāni prativedaṃ āmnātāni | snānam samāvartanam | sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogo vivāhaḥ | eteṣāṃ ceti vakṣyamāṇānām aṣṭakādīnām api saṃbandhaḥ |
[77]        hārītaḥ |
dvividha eva saṃskāro bhavati brāhmo daivaś ca | garbhādhānādiḥ snānānto brāhmaḥ | pākayajñā haviryajñā saumyāś ceti daivaḥ |
brāhmasaṃskārasaṃskṛtaḥ ṛṣīṇāṃ samānatāṃ salokatāṃ sāyojyatāṃ ca gacchati | daivenottareṇa saṃskṛto devānāṃ samānatāṃ salokatāṃ sāyojyatāṃ ca gacchati |
saumyāḥ somayāgā agniṣṭomādayaḥ | samānatā samānī yaḥ tena tulyatoktā | sāyojyatā samānam āyojyaṃ śarāīraṃ yasya saḥ sāyojyaḥ |
manuḥ [2.28] |
svādhyāyena vratair homaiḥ traividyenejyayā sutaiḥ |
mahāyajñaiś ca yajñaiś ca brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ ||
svādhyāyena vedagrahaṇena | vrataiḥ sāvitryādibhiḥ | traividyena trayyarthajñānena | ijyayā devādipūjanena | brāhmī brahmasaṃbandhinī tajjñānadvārā |
[78]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
pākayajñā haviryajñāḥ somasaṃsthās tathaiva ca |
[saṃskārā hy agnihotrāntā agni]hotraṃ tu juhvataḥ ||
saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ pūrvair uttarair anusaṃskṛtaḥ |
nityam aṣṭaguṇair yukto brāhmaṇo brāhmalaukikaḥ ||
brāhmaṃ padam avāpnoti yasmān na cyavate punaḥ |
nākapṛṣṭhaṃ yaśo dharmaṃ trirījānas tripiṣṭapam ||
agnihotriṇaḥ darśapūrṇamāsaṃ vināpi agnihotrānuṣṭhānaṃ vihitam asti tasyāgnihotraparyantāḥ [saṃskārāḥ] | jātakarmapūrvaiḥ garbhādānādibhiḥ, uttarair agnyādheyādibhiḥ | aṣṭaguṇair dayādibhiḥ | brāhmalaukikaḥ brahmalokārhaḥ | nākapṛṣṭhaṃ [merupṛṣṭham] | triviṣṭapaṃ devalokaḥ | trirījānaḥ pākayajñahaviḥsomasaṃsthābhiḥ | “triḥ somayāgeneṣṭavān” iti mādhavasvāmī |
budhaḥ |
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ simantonnayanaṃ sūtikāgṛhasaṃskāraḥ soṣyantīkarma |
soṣyantīkarma śīghraprasavāya gobhilādyuktam |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.11–12] |
garbhādhānam ṛtau puṃsaḥ savanaṃ spandanāt purā |
ṣaṣṭhe ‘ṣṭame vā sīmanto māsy ete jātakarma ca ||
[79]        ahany ekādaśe nāma caturthe māsi niṣkramaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhe ‘nnaprāśāanaṃ māsi cūḍā kāryā yathākulam ||
ete ā ite āgate, kumāre jāte ity arthaḥ |
gobhilagṛhye [2.5.8–10] |
yadartumatī bhavaty uparataśoṇitā tadā saṃbhavakālaḥ | dakṣiṇena pāṇinopastham abhimṛśed viṣṇor yoniṃ kalpayatv ity etayarcā garbhaṃ dhehi śinīvālīti samāpyarcau saṃbhavataḥ |
ṛcau samāpya saṃyogaṃ kurutaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.16.11–12] |
prajām utpādayet | auṣadhamantrayogena |
[80]        pāraskaraḥ [1.13.1] |
sā yadi garbhaṃ na dadhīta siṃhyāḥ śvetapuṣpāyāḥ upoṣya puṣyeṇa mūlam utkhāya caturthyāṃ snātāyāṃ niśāyām udapeṣaṃ piṣṭvā dakṣiṇasyāṃ nāsikāyām āsiñcatītyam oṣadhī trāyamāṇeyādinā |
siṃhī kaṇṭakārikā | upavāsamūlakhananayoḥ patyuḥ kartṛtvam |
vaijavāpaḥ |
sā yadi nādadhīta siṃhyāḥ śvetapuṣpāyā upoṣya puṣyeṇa mūlam utthāpya caturthyāṃ snātāyāṃ niśi prājāpatyaṃ sthālīpākaṃ śrapayitvā prajāpataye hutvā paścād agner vīraśayane prācīm uttānāṃ saṃveśya catvābhyām oṣadhīm udapeṣaṃ piṣṭvā dakṣiṇasyāṃ nāsikāyāṃ nastaḥ kuryāt "idam oṣadhī trāyamāṇā” iti |
“vīraṃ śayanaṃ” anantarhitā bhūmiḥ | saṃveśya śāyayitvā | catvābhyāṃ dṛṣadupalābhyām | nastaḥ nāsikārandhre |
[81]
atha garbhānavalobhanam
āśvalāyanagṛhyapariśiṣṭam |
akhaṇḍākapilaṃ peṣayitvā ṛtuvelāyāṃ udīrṣvātaḥ pativatīti dvābhyām ante svāhākārābhyāṃ nasto dakṣiṇato niṣiñcet |
akhaṇḍākapilaḥ kacchuḥ |
atha puṃsavanānavalobhanam
tatra āśvalāyanagṛhyam [1.13.1–7] |
upaniṣadi garbhalambhanaṃ puṃsavanam anavalobhanaṃ ca | yadi nādhīyāt tṛtīye garbhamāse tiṣyenopoṣitāyāḥ sarūpavatsāyāḥ gor dadhati dvau dvau tu māṣau yavaṃ ca dadhi prasṛtena prāśayet | kiṃ pibasi kiṃ pibasīti pṛṣṭvā puṃsavanaṃ puṃsavanam iti triḥ pratijānīyāt | evaṃ trīn prasṛtān | athāsyai maṇḍalāgāracchāyāyāṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ nāsikāyāṃ ajitām oṣadhīṃ nastaḥ karoti | prajāvaj jīvaputrābhyāṃ haike | [ā te garbho yonim aitu pumān bāṇa iveṣudhim | āvīro jāyatāṃ putras te daśamāsyaḥ | agnir aitu prathamo devānāṃ so ‘syai prajāṃ muñcatu mṛtyupāśāt | tad ayaṃ rājā varuṇo ‘numanyatāṃ yatheyaṃ strī pautram aghaṃ na rodād iti] | prājāpatyasya sthālī[82]pākasya hutvā hṛdayadeśam asyā ālabheta | yat te susīme hṛdaye hitam antaḥ prajāpatau | manye ‘haṃ māṃ tadvidvāṃsaṃ māhaṃ pautram aghaṃ niyām iti |
upaniṣadi upaniṣatsaṃjñake vedabhāge | “garbhalambhanaṃ puṃsavanam anavalobhanam” āmnātam iti śeṣaḥ | pumān sūyate anena karmaṇeti puṃsavanam | anavalobhanam anavalopanaṃ rakṣaṇam iti yāvat | “yadā nādhīyāt” paṭhed upaniṣadas tadā tiṣyeṇetyādikarma kartavyam ity arthaḥ | athāsyai ity asyāḥ anavalobhanakarma | asyai iti ṣaṣṭhyarthe caturthī chāndasatvāt | maṇḍalāgāracchāyā maṇḍalākṛtigṛhacchāyā | ajītā śvetadūrvā | ā te garbha ityādi prajāvān mantraḥ | agnir aitu prathama ityādi jīvaputramantraḥ |
āśvalāyanagṛhyapariśiṣṭe uktam |
tṛtīye māsi puṃsavanaṃ caturthe garbharakṣaṇam |
sāṃkhyāyanagṛhyam [1.20.1–2] |
tṛtīye māsi puṃsavanam, tiṣyeṇa śravaṇena vā |
tathā [1.21.1] |
caturthe garbharakṣaṇam |
baijavāpagṛhyam |
tatra puṃsavanānavalobhane karoti māsi dvitīye vā, purā spandata ity āpūryamāṇapakṣe puṇyāhe yad ahaḥ puṃsā nakṣatreṇa candramā yujyeta |
[83]
atha sīmantonnayanam
tatra śaṅkhalikhitau |
garbhaspandane sīmantonnayanaṃ yāvad vā na prasavaḥ |
baijavāpagṛhyam |
atha sīmantonnayanaṃ māsi caturthe pañcame [ṣaṣṭhe] vā | [āpūryamāṇapakṣe yadā puṃsā nakṣatreṇa candramā yuktaḥ syāt] |
pāraskaraḥ [1.15.1–3] |
atha sīmantonnayanaṃ puṃsavanavat prathame garbhe, māse ṣaṣṭhe ‘ṣṭhame vā |
śāṅkhāyanagṛhayam [1.22.1, 6] |
saptame māsi prathame garbhe sīmantonnayanam | tathā puṃvad upakaraṇāni nakṣatraṃ vā puṃvad iti puṃsavanam |
āśvalāyanagṛhyapariśiṣṭam |
sīmantonnayanaṃ prathame garbhe | “sīmantonnayanasaṃskāro gārbhapātrasaṃskāra” iti śrutiḥ |
[84] garbhapātrayor ayaṃ gārbhapātraḥ | garbhasthasyodaragatasya tadādhārasya ca pātrasya striyā ity arthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
sakṛt saṃskṛtasaṃskārā sīmantena dvijastriyaḥ |
yaṃ yaṃ garbhaṃ prasūyante sa sarvaḥ saṃskṛto bhavet ||
devalaḥ |
sakṛc ca saṃskṛtā nārī sarvagarbheṣu saṃskṛtā |
atha soṣyantīkarma
tatra gobhilagṛhye [1.7.13–14] |
atha soṣyantīhomaḥ | pratiṣṭhite vastau [paristīryāgnim ājyāhutīr juhoti, “yā tirañcī’ ity etayārcā “vipaścit puccham abharat” iti ca] |
“pratiṣṭhite vastau” vastideśaṃ prāpte | tatra sthite garbhe soṣyantīhomaḥ kartavyaḥ |
tatra somaḥ |
sūtikāgṛhasaṃskāraprasavāntaṃ karma jātakarma guhyanāmakaraṇam |
ayam arthaḥ | sūtikāgṛhasaṃskāraṃ kṛtvā prasavasyāntabhūtaṃ jātakarma kāryam iti | antaśabdenātrāntasaṃbandhikarma lakṣyate |
[85]        sāṃkhyāyanagṛhyam [1.23.1] |
kākādanyā mecakarātanyāḥ kośātakyāḥ bṛhatyāḥ kākātakasya mūlāni peṣayitvā upalepayet deśaṃ yasmin jāyeta rakṣasām apahatyai |
kākādanī atra kākajaṅghā | mecakarātanī kākamācikā | kośātakī ghoṣakaḥ | [bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā | kākātakaḥ kṛṣṇaguñjā | ]
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
agnyambuśūnye ca tathā niryūpe sūtikāgṛhe |
adīpaśastramusale bhūtisarṣapavarjite ||
anupraviśya yā jātam apahṛtyātmasaṃbhavam |
kṣaṇaprasavinī bālaṃ tatraivotsṛjate dvija ||
sā jātahāriṇī nāma sughorā piśitāśanā |
tasmāt saṃrakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ yatnataḥ sūtikāgṛhe ||
yūpo ‘tra [yūpākāra]kāṣṭhaviśaṣaḥ |
[atha jātakarma]
hārītaḥ |
jāte kumāre pitṝṇām āmodāt puṇyaṃ tadahas tasmād tilapūrṇāni pātrāṇi sahiraṇyāni brāhmaṇān āhūya pitṛbhyaḥ [86] svadhā kuryāt prājāpataye ceti | prāṅ nābhicchedanāt saṃskārapuṇyārthān kurvanti [chinnāyām aśaucam] |
“jātakarmaṇi bālānām” ityādivacanavihite vṛddhiśrāddhe hiraṇyapātradravyavidhiḥ | svadhāśabdena śrāddhopasthānāt | prājāpataye sahiraṇyaṃ tilapātraṃ tyajet | saṃskāro jātakarma | puṇyārthān puṇyaprayojanān dānādīn |
brahmapurāṇe |
devāś ca pitaraś caiva putre jāte dvijanmanām |
āyānti tasmāt tadahaḥ puṇyaṃ pūjyaṃ ca sarvadā ||
tatra dadyāt suvarṇaṃ ca bhūmiṃ gāṃ turagaṃ tathā |
chatraṃ chāgaṃ ca mālyaṃ ca śayanaṃ cāsanaṃ gṛham ||
jāte śrāddhe na dadyāc ca pakvānnaṃ brāhmaṇeṣv api |
baijavāpaḥ |
jāte kumāre ‘raṇibhyām agniṃ mathitvā tasminn āyuṣyahomān juhoti | tasmiṃś cūḍākaraṇopanayanavratādeśagodānakriyāḥ tasminn etam udvāhayeyuḥ |
tasmin śālāgnau |
manuḥ |
prāṅ nābhivardhanāt puṃso jātakarma vidhīyate |
mantravat prāśanaṃ cāsya hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣām ||
vardhanaṃ chedanam |
[87]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
prasūte jātakarma yavapiṣṭamadhusarpirbhir mantravat prāśanam | tasya suvarṇāntarthitayānāmikayā medhādhikaṃ brāhmaṇasya karoti | evam itarayoḥ |
medhādhikaṃ medhājananam | itarayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ | anāmikā kanīnikāsamīpāṅguliḥ |
āśvalāyanagṛhyam |
kumāraṃ jātaṃ purānyair ālambhāt sarpirmadhunī hiraṇyanikāṣaṃ hiraṇyena prāśayet |
purānyair ālambhāt, purā pūrvam anyaiḥ [upasūtiketaraiḥ] ālambhāt sparśāt |
baijavāpagṛhyam |
atra jātasya kumārasyāchinnāyṃ nāḍyāṃ hiraṇyamiśrābhir adbhir ācāmet, nānāmantrair hiraṇyena dadhimadhughṛtaṃ prāśayati “bhūs tvayi dadhāmi” iti |
pāraskaraḥ [1.16.3–4] |
jātasya kumārasyāchinnāyāṃ nāḍyāṃ medhajananāyuṣye kareti [karoti?] | anāmikayā suvarṇāntarhitayā madhughṛte prāśayati ghṛtaṃ vā “bhūs tvayi dadhāmi” ityādi |
“āyuṣyaṃ agnir āyuṣmān” ityādijapaḥ |
[88]        baijavāpagṛhyam |
athāsya medhājananaṃ karoti | dakṣiṇaṃ karṇam abhinidhāya vāg vāg iti trir athāsya guhyaṃ nāma kareti [karoti?] |
abhinidhāya abhimukhībhūya | guhyaṃ mātāpitṛbhrātṛvedyam |
aṅgirāḥ |
sūtikāvāsanilayā janmadā nāma devatāḥ |
tāsāṃ yāganimittārthaṃ śuddhir janmani kīrtitā ||
ṣaṣṭhe ‘hni rātriyāgaṃ tu janmadānāṃ tu kārayet |
atha nāmakaraṇam
atra manuḥ [2.30] |
nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ tu dvādaśyāṃ vāsya kārayet |
puṇye tithau muhūrte vā nakṣatre vā guṇānvite ||
daśamyām ity etac ca yeṣāṃ daśāhāt prāk śuddhis teṣām | puṇye tithau na riktāyām | muhūrte guṇānvite abhijidādau |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ [?] tu kecid icchanti pārthiva |
dvādaśyāṃ atha vā rātrau māse pūrṇe tathāpare ||
axṭādaśe ‘hani tathā vadanty anye manīṣiṇaḥ |
puṇye tithau muhūrte vā nakṣatre vā guṇānvite ||
[89]        yājñavalkya-bṛhaspatī [Yājñ 1.12; Bṛh saṃs. 58] |
ahany ekādaśe nāma |
viṣṇuḥ [27.5] |
āśaucavyapagame nāmadheyam | etena daśamyām iti nivṛttāśaucaviṣayam |
gobhilaḥ [2.8.8] |
jananād daśarātre vyuṣṭe śatarātre saṃvatsare vā nāmadheyakaraṇam |
vyuṣṭe atikrānte |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
daśamyām utthāpya piṇḍavardhanaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tatra sāṃnidhyaśruteḥ | brahmaṇe cāhutiṃ pūrvaṃ hutvā pitṝn anumānya tadahar eva nāmakaraṇaṃ [kuryāt] | kuladevatānakṣatrābhisambaddhaṃ pitā kuryād anyo vā kulavṛddhaḥ | caturakṣaraṃ dvyakṣaraṃ vā ghoṣavad ādyantarantasthaṃ puṃsām (?), īkārāntaṃ strīṇām eva kṛte nāmni, śuci tatkulaṃ bhavati |
utthāpya sūtikām iti śeṣaḥ | piṇḍavardhanaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryād ity ārthaḥ |
[90]        pāraskaraḥ [1.6.17.1–5] |
daśamyām utthāpya braḥmaṇān bhojayitvā pitā nama [karoti | dvyakṣaraṃ caturakṣaraṃ vā ghoṣavad ādyantarantasthaṃ dīrghābhiniṣṭhānaṃ kṛtaṃ kuryān na taddhitam | anujākṣaraṃ ākārāntaṃ striyai tadhitam] |
vargagṛtīyacaturthapañcamā hakāraś ca goṣavantaḥ | antaḥ madhye, antasthāḥ yaralavāḥ | abhiniṣṭhānaṃ samāptiḥ | kṛtaṃ kṛtpratyayāntam, na taddhitapratyayāntam | striyāḥ punar ayujākṣaram ākārāntaṃ taddhitāntaṃ vā ayujākṣaram |
baijavāpagṛhyam |
pitā nāma karoty ekākṣaraṃ dvyakṣaraṃ caturakṣaraṃ vā aparimitaṃ vā tathā tyakṣaram īkārāntaṃ striyai |
aparimitākṣaram uktākṣarasaṃkhyātiriktasaṃkhyam |
manuḥ [2.31] |
maṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya syāt kṣatriyasya balānvitam |
vaiśyasya dhanasaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya tu jugupsitam ||
maṅgalyaṃ maṅgalapratipādakam, yathā lakṣmīdharaḥ | balānvitaṃ śauryapratipādakam, yathā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ | dhanasaṃyuktaṃ dhanapratipādakaśabdasaṃyuktam, yathā mahādhanaḥ | jugupsitaṃ nindāpratipādakaśabdasaṃyuktam, yathā naradāsaḥ |
yamaḥ |
śarma devaś ca viprasya varma rājā ca bhūbhujaḥ |
[91]        gupto dattaś ca vaiśyasya dāsaḥ śūdrasya kārayet ||
manuḥ [2.32–33] |
[śarmavad brāhmaṇasya syād rājño rājasamanvitam] |
vaiśyasya puṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya preṣyasaṃyutam ||
strīṇāṃ sukhodyam krūraṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ manoramam |
maṅgalyaṃ dīrghavarṇāntam āśīrvādābhidhānavat ||
puṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ yathā dhanapuṣṭa iti | sukhodyaṃ sukhoccāryaṃ [yaśodā ityādi] | akrūraṃ abhayānakam | āśīrvādābhidānavat yathā subhadreti |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.10.9–11] |
guptadāsātmakaṃ nāma praśastaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ |
nārthahīnaṃ na cāśastaṃ nāpaśabdayutaṃ tathā ||
nāmaṅgalyaṃ jugupsyaṃ vā nāma kuryāt samākṣaram |
nātidīrghaṃ nātihrasvaṃ nātigurvakṣarānvitam ||
[sukhoccāryaṃ tu tannāma kuryād yat pravaṇākṣaram] |
aśastaṃ yathā bhūtāntādi |
atha niṣkramaṇam
tatra bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
dvādaśe ‘hani rājendra śiśoḥ niṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt |
[92]        caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ tathānyeṣāṃ mataṃ vibho ||
dvādaśe ‘hanīti śākhābhedavikalpaḥ |
yamaḥ |
tataḥ tṛtīye kartavyaṃ māsi sūryasya darśanam |
caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ śiśoś candrasya darśanam ||
atulyārthatvāt sūryacandradarśanayoḥ samuccayaḥ |
laugākṣiḥ |
tṛtīye ‘rdhamāse darśanam ādityasya |
tṛtīye pakṣa ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [27.10] |
caturthe māsy ādityasya darśanam |
atrāpi śākhābhedād vikalpaḥ | anayos tu candrasūryadarśanayor ābhyudayikaṃ śrāddhaṃ nāsti | [yathā]
sūryendvoḥ karmaṇī ye ca tayoḥ śrāddhaṃ na vidyate |
iti chandogapariśiṣṭe |
annaprāśanam
[atha yamaḥ |
tato ‘nnaprāśanaṃ māsi ṣaṣṭhe] kāryaṃ yathāvidhi |
[93]        aṣṭame māsi kartavyaṃ yad veṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kule ||
manuḥ |
[ṣaṣṭhe ‘nnaprāśanaṃ māsi yad veṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kule] |
yadveti kālaprakramāt tadgocara evāyaṃ vikalpaḥ | tenāyam arthaḥ – yat kālavac ca annaprāśanam iṣṭam abhimataṃ kule bhavati tasmin vā kāle kartavyam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
saṃvatsare ‘nnaprāśanam |
laugākṣiḥ |
[ṣaṣṭheṣu annaprāśanam] | jāteṣu danteṣu vā |
atraiva guṇaphalāni
atra pāraskaraḥ [1.19.5–13] |
[prāśanānte] sarvān rasān sarvam annam ekata uddhṛtyā[thainaṃ] prāśayet tūṣṇīṃ hanteti vā | [“hantakāraṃ manuṣyā” iti śruteḥ | bhāradvājyā māṃsena vākprasārakāmasya | kapiñjalamāṃsenānnādyakāmasya | matsyair javanakāmasya | kṛkaṣāyā āyuṣkāmasya | āṭyā brahmavarcasakāmasya | sarvaiḥ sarvakāmasya | annaparyāya vā [tato brāhmaṇabhojanam] |
[bhāradvājyā bhāradvājapakṣijātīyastriyāḥ] | vākprasāritaṃ vākprasaraḥ | kapiñjalo gauratittiriḥ | kṛkaṣā [94] godhā | [āṭyāḥ śarālijātīyapakṣistriyāḥ] | annaparyāya annaparipāṭyā |
śāṅkhyāyanagṛhyam [1.27.1–6] |
ṣaṣṭhe māsi annaprāśanam | ājam annādikāmaḥ | taittiraṃ brahmavarcasakāmaḥ | mātsyaṃ javanakāmaḥ | ghṛtaudanaṃ tajaskāmaḥ | dadhimadhughṛtamiśram annaṃ prāśayet |
javanaṃ bījam |
atha cūḍākaraṇam
tatra manuḥ [2.35] |
cūḍakarma dvijātīnāṃ sarveṣām eva dharmataḥ |
prathame ‘bde tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śruticodanāt ||
prathame ‘bde vartamāne annaprāśanānantaram |
yamaḥ |
tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe cūḍakarma vidhīyate |
dvitīye vā tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śruticodanāt ||
dvitīye pūrṇe ity anuṣaṅgaḥ. | anyathā saṃvatsare pūrṇe ity anenaiva vā tallabdhatvād dvitīye ity avacanīyaṃ syāt | “tṛtīye bhūyiṣṭhe gate” iti laugākṣivacanāt |
[95]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
tṛtīye varṣe cūḍākaraṇaṃ pañcame vā |
āśvalāyanagṛhyam [1.17.1] |
tṛtīye varṣe caulaṃ yathākuladharmaṃ vā |
laugākṣiḥ |
tṛtīyaasya varṣasya bhūyiṣṭhe gate cūḍākaraṇam | dakṣiṇataḥ kamuñjā vasiṣṭhānām | ubhayato ‘trikāśyapānām | muṇḍā bhṛgavaḥ | pañcacūḍā aṅgirasaḥ | vājim eke maṅgalārtham, śikhino ‘nye | yathākuladharmaṃ vā | śuddhasya puṇyāhe vā |
bhūyiṣṭhe bahutame | kamuñjā cūḍā | vājiḥ keśapaṅktiḥ | śuddhasya śuklasya |
āśvalāyanagṛhyam [1.4.1–2] |
udagayana āpūryamāṇapakṣe puṇye nakṣatre caulakarmopanayanagodānavivāhāḥ | sarvakālam eke vivāham |
[96] ayaṃ cūḍākaraṇakālānāṃ vikalpaḥ śākhābhedāpekṣayā vyavasthito boddhavyaḥ |
strīṇāṃ saṃskārāḥ
atha manuḥ [2.66–68] |
amantrikā tu kāryeyaṃ strīṇām āvṛd aśeṣataḥ |
saṃskārārthaṃ śarīrasya yathākālaṃ yathākramam ||
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇāṃ saṃskāro vaidikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
patisevā gurau vāso gṛhārtho ‘gniparikriyā ||
[eṣa prokto dvijātīnām aupanāyaniko vidhiḥ] ||
āvṛt itikartavyatā | aupanāyanikaḥ upanayanakārī | cūḍākaraṇāntasaṃskārānantaram |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.13] |
tūṣṇīm etāḥ kriyāḥ strīṇāṃ vivāhas tu samantrakaḥ |
anupanītadharmāḥ
idānīm upanayane vaktavye tatpūrvakāle pratiṣiddhāny ucyante | tatra manuḥ [2.172] |
nābhivyāhārayet brahma svadhāninayanād ṛte |
śūdreṇa hi samas tāvad yāvad vede na jāyate ||
svadhāninayanāt śrāddhamantrāt |
[97]        vasiṣṭhaḥ [2.3–7] |
athāpy udāharanti —
na hy asmin vidyate karma kiṃcid ā mauñjibandhanāt |
[vṛtya śūdrasamo hy eṣa yāvad vede na jāyate] ||
anyatrodakakarmasvadhāpitṛsaṃyuktebhyaḥ |
pretapitrādyudakatarpaṇaśrāddhapitṛsaṃyuktabhūmicchatrādidānād anyatrety arthaḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
jātamātraḥ śiśus tāvad yāvad aṣṭau samā vayaḥ |
sa hi garbhasamo jñeyo vyaktimātrapradarśitaḥ ||
samā varṣāṇi | vyaktimātreṇa na tu karmādhikārikatvena |
upanītasya doṣo ‘sti kriyamāṇair vigarhitaiḥ |
aprāptavyavahāro ‘sau bālaḥ ṣoḍaśavārṣikaḥ ||
anena dhanaprayogādivyavahāre svātantryābhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ |
gautamaḥ [2.1–9] |
prāg upanayanāt kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ | ahutāt | brahmacārī | yathopapādamūtrapurīṣo bhavati | nāsyācamanakalpo vidyate | anyatrāpamārjanapradhāvanaprokṣaṇebhyaḥ | na [98] tadupasparśanād aśaucam | na ev evainam agnihavanabaliharaṇayor niyuñjyāt | na brahmābhivyāhārayed anyatra svadhāninayanāt |
kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ ity anena icchayā aślīlabhāṣaṇaparyuṣitabhakṣaṇādīni na niṣiddhānīti pratipāditam | yāni punar atyantapatanīyāni tāni niṣiddhāny eva, smṛtyantare prāyaścittadarśanāt | ahutād iti hutaṃ vinā, hutasyābhakṣaṇam | brahmacārī maithunavarjī | yathopapādamūtrapurīṣaḥ udaṅmukhādiniyamavān na syād ity arthaḥ | ācamanakalpaḥ prāṅmukhatvaśrotrasparśādi | ācamanaṃ tu karoty eva | sarvatra kalpaniṣedhe prāpte ‘pavādam āha anyatra iti | apamārjanam ucchiṣṭādiliptasya udakena pāṇinā nirmalīkaraṇam | pradhāvanaṃ purīṣādiliptasya prakṣālanam | prokṣaṇam aśucispṛṣṭasyodakenābhyukṣaṇam | etena kṛtāpamārjanādeḥ sparśe nāśaucam | agnihavanabaliharaṇayor niyuñjyād iti, sahakāritvenāpīti śeṣaḥ |
na vai kanyā na yuvatir nālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ |
iti bālasya sākṣāddhomakartavyaniṣedhāt |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
prāk cūḍākaraṇād bālaḥ prāg annaprāśanāc chiśuḥ |
kumāraḥ sa tu vijñeyo yāvan mauñjīnibandhanam ||
[99]        śiśor abhyukṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ bālasyācamanaṃ smṛtam |
rajasvalādisaṃsparśe snātavyaṃ tu kumārakaiḥ ||
varjayed ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [2.26] |
madyaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
mātāpitror athocchiṣṭaṃ bālo bhuñjan sukhī bhavet |
āpastambaḥ [2.15.19–25] |
ānnaprāśanād garbhā nāprayatā bhavanti | ā parisaṃvatsarād ity eke | yāvatā vā diśo na prajānīyuḥ | puropanayanād ity aparam | atra hy adhikāraḥ śāstrair bhavati | sā niṣṭhā | smṛtiś ca |
[100]
|| 4 ||
athopanayanam
tatra devalaḥ |
prathamaṃ mātāpitṛbhyāṃ garbhādhānādibhiḥ saṃskṛto garbhāṣṭame varṣe upanayanārho bhavati | tatropādhyāyaḥ pitā, mātā gāyatrī | evam upanīto dvipitṛṅo dvijātiḥ syāt |
dvipitṛko dvimātāpitṛko ekaśeṣāt |
manuḥ [2.169] |
mātur agre ‘dhijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhanāt |
tṛtīyaṃ yajñadīkṣāyāṃ dvijasya śruticodanāt ||
āpastambaḥ [1.1.19] |
vasante brāhmaṇam upanayīta, grīṣme rājanyam, śaradi vaiśyam, garbhāṣṭameṣu brāhmaṇam, garbhaikādaśeṣu rājanyam, garbhadvādaśeṣu vaiśyam |
[budhaḥ] |
garbhāṣṭame varṣe vasante brāhmaṇa ātmānam upanāyayet | ekādaśe kṣatriyo grīṣme, dvādaśe vaiśyo varṣāsu |
[101] ātmānam upanāpayed iti, pitrādyadhikārivirahe | varṣāśabdena “ṛtuḥ saṃvatsara” iti śaradas tadantargatatvāt saivopāttā |
viśvāmitra-yājñavalkyau [Yājja. 1.14] |
garbhāṣṭame vābde brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam |
rājñām ekādaśe saike viśām eke yathākulam ||
upanayanam evopanāyanam | “viśāṃ saike ekādaśe” ity anvayād dvādaśa ity arthaḥ | yathākulam iti, kuladharmānusāreṇa kālāntare ‘pīty arthaḥ |
upanayanakālakrame pāraskaraḥ [2.2.4] |
yathāmaṅgalaṃ vā sarveṣām |
yasminn api kāle kriyamāṇaṃ kule maṅgalāya tat bhavati tatrāpi kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
laugākṣiḥ |
saptame varṣe brāhmaṇasyopanayanam, navame rājanyasaikādaśe vaiśyasya |
saptame varṣe ityādi laugākṣimanuvacanaikavākyatārthaṃ na garbhāditvaṃ kiṃ tu janmāditvam eva |
[102]        paiṭhīnasiḥ |
garbhāt pañcame ‘bde vā brāhmaṇam upanayed garbhāṣṭame vāṣṭame vā, garbhāṣṭame rājanyaṃ garbhadvādaśe vā, garbhadvādaśe vaiśyaṃ garbhaṣoḍaśe vā |
upanayed ity anuvṛttau āpatambaḥ [1.1.20–26] |
atha kāmyāni | saptame brahmavarcasakāmam | aṣṭame āyuṣkāmam | navame tejaskāmam | daśame ‘nnādyakāmam | ekādaśa indriyakāmam | dvādaśe paśukāmam |
gautamaḥ [1.1.7–9, 13] |
upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasyāṣṭame | navame pañcame vā kāmyam | garbhādisaṃkhyā varṣāṇām | ekādaśadvādaśayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ |
manuḥ [2.37] |
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāryaṃ viprasya pañcame |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyehārthino ‘ṣṭame ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
dvādaśaṣoḍaśaviṃśatiś cety atītā aviruddhakālā bhavanti |
atreyaṃ vyavasthā — “vasante brāhmaṇam upanayīta” ity āpastambavacanena vasantādikālīnatvam upanayane vihitam | manu-viśvāmitra-yājñavalkya-pāraskara-laugākṣi-paiṭhīnasivākyeṣu vinopanibandhanena śravaṇād brāhmaṇasya garbhāṣṭamagarbhapañcama[103]janmāṣṭamāḥ, kṣatriyasya garbhāṣṭamajanmanavama-garbhaikādaśagarbhadvādaśāḥ, vaiśyasya garbhaikādaśagarbhadvādaśagarbhaṣoḍaśā nityāḥ | yathākulaṃ cānyo ‘pi kālo nityaḥ sarvavarṇānām | atrāpi prādhānyāt sarvasmṛtikārasaṃmatatvāc ca garbhāṣṭamagarbhaikādaśagarbhadvādaśā yathākramaṃ brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ mukhyāḥ |
tathā ca manuḥ [2.36] |
garbhāṣṭame ‘bde kurvīta brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam |
garbhād ekādaśe rājño garbhāt tu dvādaśe viśaḥ ||
garbhapañcamasya tu paiṭhīnasinānupabandham eva smaraṇadṛṣṭasya ca sadyaḥ sāvitrīpaṭhanasya tadavadhāraṇasya ca kenacid evātiprājñena spaṣṭavācā kartuṃ śaktyatvā nityatvam eva | pratinityaṃ sāvitrīpaṭhanaṃ ca sadya eva gāyatrīpravaraṇaṃ brāhmaṇasyeti śruteḥ | kṣatriyasya garbhāṣṭamanavamau kulavyavasthayā nityau | evam ekādaśo vaiśyasya | anye cāṣṭamādayo dvādaśādi varṇakrameṇāviruddhā | “dvādaśaṣoḍaśaviṃśatiś cety atītā aviruddhakālā bhavanti” iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt teṣāṃ ca pūrvapūrvāsaṃbhave uttarottaravidhinā atītatvenāvirodhavacanāt, asamaviśiṣṭavikalpāc ca | “ūrdhvaṃ tu dvādaśāt” ity ā ṣoḍaśadvāviṃśaticaturviṃśatir yāvat yathākramaṃ varṇānāṃ svalpaḥ pratyavāyaḥ | dvādaśaparyantam evāvirodhismṛteḥ | ūrdhvaṃ tu vrātyā vrātyastomena vopanayanārhāḥ | pañcamādiṣu kāmanopanibandhatve tu saṃyogapṛthaktvena nityāḥ kāmyāś ca | yeṣāṃ tu “rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭha” ityādīnāṃ kāmanopanibandhenaiva smaraṇam, teṣāṃ kāmyatvam | [104] kiṃ tu svarūpasiddhes tasmād evopanayanarūpāḥ saṃskāro nityaḥ siddhaḥ | godohanena praṇayanasiddher yathā kramakārārthaṃ camasena na punaḥ praṇayanam | nāpi trivarṣasyopanayanam | tatra viśeṣo lekhanābhāvenāpakṣakasyābhāvāt, śarādinā yathā na praṇayanam iti | athopanayanātikrame vrātyatā | tatra sāpekṣavākyeṣu varṣopanayanavarṇakramānuvṛttir mantavyā |
manuḥ [2.38] |
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate |
ā dvāviṃśāt kṣatrabandhor ā caturviṃśater viśaḥ ||
abhividhāvāṅ,
aupanāyanikaḥ kālaḥ paraḥ ṣoḍaśavārṣikaḥ |
dvāviṃśatiḥ paro hy asya syāc caturviṃśatiḥ paraḥ ||
iti vyāsavacanāt |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.38] |
ata ūrdhvaṃ patanty ete sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā vrātyastomād ṛte kratoḥ ||
manuḥ [2.40] |
naitair apūtaiḥ vidhivad āpady api hi karhicit |
brāhmān yaunāṃś ca sambandhān ācared brāhmaṇaiḥ saha ||
apūtaiḥ akṛtaprāyaścittaiḥ | brahmagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham |
[105]
athopanetur upaneyasya ca nirūpaṇam |
tatra ācāryalakṣaṇam
yamaḥ |
satyavāg dhṛtimān dakṣaḥ sarvabhūtadayāparaḥ |
āstiko vedanirataḥ śucir ācārya ucyate ||
vedādhyayanasaṃpanno vṛttimān vijitendriyaḥ |
dakṣaḥ vratotsāhī | vṛttimān yathāvihitajīvanahetuvṛttimān |
hārītaḥ |
na yājayed vṛttihīnaṃ vṛṇuyāc ca na taṃ gurum |
na hi majjñā karau digdhau rudhireṇa viśudhyataḥ ||
majjñā iti majjanśabdasya tṛtīyā |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nonmattamūkān saṃskuryāt |
viṣṇuḥ [29.4–6] |
nāparīkṣitaṃ yājayet | nādhyāpayet | nopanayet |
āpastambaḥ [1.1.6] |
aśūdrāṇām aduṣṭakarmaṇām upāyanam |
aduṣṭakarmaṇāṃ madyapānādirahitānām |
[106]        kātyāyanaḥ |
sāvitryā brāhmaṇam upanayīta, triṣṭubhā rājanyam, jagatyā vaiśyam, sarveṣāṃ vā sāvitrī |
[laugākṣiḥ] |
tat savitur vareṇyam iti sāvitrī brāhmaṇasya | tāṃ savitur iti rājanyasya | viśvārūpāṇīti vaiśyasya |
yamaḥ |
amantram api vā kuryāt sarvam āvṛtya dakṣiṇam |
etac ca ṣoḍaśavarṣaparyantaṃ kasyāṃcid āpadi mantrasyopanetur abhāve upanayanakālātikramasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ boddhavyam |

“praga eva” prātar eva |

brāhmaṇān bhojayet |

athopavītādīni
tatra manuḥ [2.44] |
kārpāsam upavītaṃ syād viprasyordhavrataṃ [vṛtam?] trivṛt |
śaṇasūtramayaṃ rājño vaiśyasyāvikasautrikam ||
āvikasautrikam ūrṇāsūtranirmitam |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.8.5] |
kauśaṃ sautraṃ vā triḥ trivṛdyajñopavītam ā nābheḥ [dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddhṛtya savyam avadhāya śiro ‘vadadhyāt] |
kauśaṃ kṛmikauśodbhavam, paṭṭasūtramayam iti yāvat |
tathā ca ādityapurāṇe |
kṣaumajaṃ vātha kārpāsaṃ paṭṭasūtram athāpi vā |
dadhyād yajñopavītaṃ yo na taṃ hiṃsanti vāyasāḥ || iti |
sautraṃ kārpāsam | triḥ trivṛt, navaguṇam | “nava vai trivṛt” iti śruteḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ tu trivṛtaṃ kāryaṃ tantutrayam adhovṛtam |
trivṛtaṃ copavītaṃ syāt tasyaiko granthir iṣyate ||
[108] vāmāvartaṃ triguṇaṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāvartanaṃ navaguṇaṃ syāt | tad evaṃ ca trisaraṃ kāryam ity etad uktam “trivṛtaṃ copavītam” ity anena |
tathā |
pṛṣṭadeśe ‘tha nābhyāṃ ca dhṛtaṃ yad vindate kaṭim |
tad dhāryam upavītaṃ syān nāsti nīcaṃ na cocchritam ||
vāmaskandhakṛtaṃ nābhipṛṣṭhavaṃśayor dhṛtaṃ yathā kaṭiparyantaṃ prāpnoti tāvatparimāṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanenopavītāvasthāpanasyānuvādena parimāṇaviśeṣavidhiḥ |
atha gautamaḥ [1.19–23] |
vāsāṃsi śāṇakṣaumacīrakutapāḥ sarveṣām | kārpāsaṃ vāvikṛtam | kāṣāyam apy eke | vārkṣyaṃ brāhmaṇasya | māñjiṣṭhahāridre itarayoḥ |
śaṇamayaṃ śāṇam | atasī kṣumā | cīraṃ kuśādiniṣpannam | kutapaḥ pārvatīyachāgaromanirmitaḥ | avikṛtaṃ rañjakadravyāsaṃpṛktam | kāṣāyapakṣe brāhmaṇasya vṛkṣakāṣāyāktam, kṣatriyavaiśyayor māñjiṣṭhahāridrāktam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.64–67] |
śuklam ahataṃ vāso brāhmaṇasya | kārpāsaṃ māñjiṣṭhaṃ kṣatriyasya | hāridrakauśeyaṃ vaiśyasya | sarveṣāṃ v tāntavam araktam |
kauśeyaṃ kṛmikośottham | tāntavaṃ atra kārpāsam |
[109]        śaṅkhaḥ |
kṣaumadukūlasautrāṇi vāsāṃsi trayāṇāṃ varṇānām |
yamaḥ |
kṣaumakārpāsakutapāś carmavalkalakambalāḥ |
sarvaṃ tu dhārayec chuklaṃ vāsas tatpāridhānikam ||
pāridhānikaṃ paridhānaprayojanam |
manu [2.41] |
kārṣṇarauravabāstāni carmāṇi brahmacāriṇaḥ |
vasīrann ānupūrvyeṇa śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca ||
kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇamṛgaḥ | ruruḥ bahuśākhāśṛṅgo mṛgaḥ | bastaḥ chāgaḥ | āvikaṃ meṣaromanirmitam |
āpastambaḥ [1.3.3–10] |
hāriṇam aiṇeyaṃ vā kṛṣṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya | kṛṣṇaṃ ced anupastīrṇāsanaśāyī syāt | rauravaṃ rājanyasya | bastājinaṃ vaiśyasya | āvikaṃ sārvavarṇikam | [kaṃbalaś ca] | brahmavṛddhim icchann ajināny eva vasīta, kṣatravṛddhim icchan vastrāṇy eva, ubhayavṛddhim icchan ubhayam iti hi brāhmṇam | ajinaṃ tv evottaraṃ dhārayet |
aiṇeyam eṇīsamudbhavam, eṇī gauramṛgī | yadā hāriṇaṃ tadā kṛṣṇaṃ tac ca nopastīrya āsīta śayīta vā | āvikaṃ meṣacarma | sārvavarṇikaṃ jātisaṃbandham | uttaram uttarīyam |
[110]        pāraskaraḥ [2.5.17–20] |
[aiṇam ajinam uttarīyaṃ brāhmaṇasya | rauravaṃ rājanyasya | ] ājaṃ gavyaṃ vā vaiśyasya | sarveṣāṃ vā gavyam asati pradhānatvāt |
yamaḥ |
[kṛṣṇājinaṃ brāhmaṇasya rauravaṃ kṣatriyasya tu |
bastājinaṃ tu vaiśyasya sarveṣāṃ rauravājinam || ]
sarveṣāṃ rauvājinam itaracarma na saṃbhavati iti śeṣaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [27.19–20] |

paiṭhīnasiḥ |

atha mekhalāḥ
tatra manuḥ [2.42] |
mauñjī trivṛt samā ślakṣṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā |
kṣatriyasya tu maurvī jyā vaiśyasya śaṇatāntavī ||
trivṛt triguṇīkṛtā | samā na kvacit sthūlā, na kvacit sūkṣmā | ślakṣṇā asthūlā |
[111]        yamaḥ |
etāsām apy alābhe tu kuśāśmantakabalvajaiḥ |
mekhalās trivṛtaḥ kāryāḥ granthinaikena vā tribhiḥ ||
trivṛttvaṃ trirveṣṭanena | granthisaṃkhyā ca ācārāt pravarasaṃkhyayā vety avasthitā |
āpastambaḥ [1.2.33–37] |
mauñjī mekhalā trivṛd brāhmaṇasya śaktiviṣaye dakṣiṇāvṛttānām | dhanurjyā rājanyasya | mauñjī vā ayomiśrā | āvīsūtraṃ vaiśyasya | sairī tāmbūlī vā ity eke |
śaktiviṣaye muñjāpāptau saṃbhavantyām | dakṣiṇāvṛttānāṃ pradakṣiṇāvartitānāṃ, yogyatvāt muñjādidravyāṇām ity arthah | ayomiśrā lohasūtramiśrā | sairī sīrasya halasya yoktram iti yāvat | tāmbūlī mūlodakavṛkṣanirmiteti kapardī |
gobhilaḥ [2.10.10] |
muñjakāśatāmbūlayo raśanāḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mauñjī mekhalā aśmantakajā vā brāhmaṇasya, bailvajā maurvī vā rājanyasya, śāṇī kṣaumī vā vaiśyasyeti |
[aśmantakaḥ kuśasadṛśaḥ tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ] |
[112]        baijavāpaḥ |
mekhalāṃ triguṇāṃ prasalavisṛṣṭāṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ triḥ parinayantīti |
prasalaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ yathā bhavaty evaṃ sṛṣṭāṃ nirmitām | pari samantāt nayanti pradakṣiṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā triḥ veṣṭayanti |
atha daṇḍaḥ
tatra bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
brāhmaṇe bailvapālāśau tṛtīyaḥ plakṣajas tathā |
nyagrodhakhādirau kṣatre tathānyo vetasodbhavaḥ ||
pailavaudumbaro vaiśye tṛtīyo ‘śvatthajas tathā |
daṇḍān etān mahābāho dharmato ‘rhanti dhāritum ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
parṇapippalabilvānāṃ kramād daṇḍāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
parṇaḥ palāśaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
audumbaro khādiro vāraṇo vā vaiśyasya |
vāraṇaḥ varaṇavṛkṣodbhavaḥ |
[pāraskaraḥ — 2.5.25–27]
pālāśo daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya, bailvo rājanyasya, audumbaro vaiśyasya, sarve vā sarveṣām |
[113]        yamaḥ |
[viprasya daṇḍaḥ pālāśo bailvo vā dharmataḥ smṛtaḥ | ]
aśvattho kṣatriyasyātha khādiro vāpi dharmataḥ ||
audumbaro ‘tha vaiśyasya plakṣo vā daṇḍa ucyate |
tathā |
eteṣām apy alābhe tu sarveṣām eva yajñiyāḥ ||
manuḥ [2.46] |
keśāntiko brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ |
lalāṭasaṃmito rājñaḥ syāt tu nāsāntiko viśaḥ ||
daṇḍa ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [11.55–57] |
ghrāṇasaṃmito brāhmaṇasya, lalāṭasaṃmitaḥ kṣatriyasya, keśasaṃmito
vaiśyasya |

pālāśakhadiraudumbarā daṇḍāḥ keśāntalalāṭanāsādeśatulyāḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
keśāntalalāṭagrīvāpramāṇā brāhmaṇādinām |
manuḥ [2.47] |
ṛjavas te tu sarve syur avraṇāḥ saumyadarśanāḥ |
[114]        anudvegakarā nṝṇāṃ satvaco nāgniduḥkhitāḥ ||
gautamaḥ [1.27] |
apīḍitā yūpavakrāḥ saśalkāḥ |
apīḍitāḥ vallīveṣṭanādibhir anupahatāḥ | yūpavakrāḥ yūpavad anatāgrāḥ | saśalkāḥ savalkalāḥ | asamāsāt sarva evaṃrūpāḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.2.38] |
naiyagrodhaskandhajo ‘vāṅgro rājanyasya |
atha gāyatryupadeśaḥ
atha hārītaḥ |
tasmād upetāyopasannāya samīkṣitāya samīkṣamāṇāya brahmānubruvanti netarebhyaḥ, tān brahmacāriṇa ity ācakṣate |
brahmaśabdo atra sāvitrīparaḥ |
pāraskaraḥ [2.3.3–7] |
athāsmai sāvitrīmanvāhottarato ‘gneḥ pratyaṅmukhāyopaviṣṭāyopasannāya samīkṣamāṇāya samīkṣitāya | dakṣiṇatas tiṣṭhata āsīnāya vaike | paccho ‘rdharcaśaḥ sarvāṃ ca tṛtīyena sahānuvartayan | saṃvatsare ṣaṇmāsye caturviṃśatyahe dvādaśāhe ṣaḍahe tryahe vā | sadyas tv eva gāyatrīṃ brāhmaṇāyānubrūyāt [āgneyo vai brāhmaṇa iti śruteḥ] |
[115] pacchaḥ pādaṃ pādaṃ sarvāṃ ca tṛtīyena pādena sahānuvarayet |
yamaḥ |
oṃkārapūrvikās tisraḥ gāvitrīṃ yas tu vindati |
carati brahmacaryaṃ ca sa vai śrotriya ucyate ||
tisro mahāvyāhṛtīr iti śeṣaḥ |
manuḥ [2.80] |
etayarcā visaṃyuktaḥ kāle ca kriyayā svayā |
brahmakṣatriyaviḍyonir garhaṇāṃ yāti sādhuṣu ||
atha bhaikṣacaryā
manuḥ [2.48] |
pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam upasthāya ca bhāskaram |
pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgniṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ yathāvidhi ||
upasthāya saṃukham avasthāpa | parītya pariveṣṭya |
[116]        tathā [2.49] |
bhavatpūrvaṃ cared bhaikṣam upanīto dvijottamaḥ |
bhavanmadhyaṃ tu rājanyo vaiśyas tu bhavaduttaram ||
bhaviṣye |
mātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā mātur vā bhaginīṃ nijām |
bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ prathamaṃ yā cainaṃ nāvamānayet ||
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ ratnaṃ sā pātre ‘sya vinikṣipet |
“yā cainaṃ nāvamānayet” iti anyāpīty arthaḥ | etac ca prathamam ity upakramyābhidhānāt jñātikulādiprātyahikabhikṣāniṣedhāc copanayanaprayogāntargatabhikṣāviṣayam |
bhikṣetety anuvṛttau pāraskaraḥ [2.5.5–7] |
tisro ‘pratyākhyāyinyaḥ | ṣaḍ vā dvādaśaparimitā vā | mātaraṃ prathamām eke |
apratyākhyāyinya iti dvitīyāsthāne prathamā |
śaunakaḥ |
ācaret sapta bhikṣāṃ kṣāṃ ca hiṃ ca varjayet |
“kṣāṃ ca hiṃ ca varjayet” iti lakṣaṇayā bhikṣām iti daṇḍādivad varjayed ity arthaḥ |
[117]        tathā |
sarveṣu varṇeṣu, brāhmaṇādyāḥ savarṇasthāḥ |
bhikṣeran iti śeṣaḥ |
vyāsaḥ |

sajātīyagṛheṣv eva sārvavarṇikam eva vā ||
gautamaḥ [2.42] |
sārvavarṇikaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇam abhiśastapatitavarjam |
tathā [2.44–45] |
ācāryagurujñātiṣv alābhe ‘nyatra | teṣāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ pariharet |
manuḥ [2.183] |
vedayajñair ahīnānāṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu |
brahmacāry āhared bhaikṣaṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato ‘nvaham ||
“praśastānāṃ svakarmasu” samyak svakarmānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhānām |
tathā [2.184] |
guroḥ kule na bhikṣeta na jñātikulabandhuṣu |
alābhe tv anyagehānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayet ||
anyatrālābhe gurvādiṣu bhikṣeta | tatrāpi pūvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayed uttarottarālābe |
[118]        bhaviṣye |
sarvaṃ vā vicared grāmaṃ pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave |
antyavarjaṃ mahābāhur ity āha bhagavān vibhuḥ ||
vācaṃ niyamya prayataḥ āmiṣaṃ cāpi varjayet |
cāturvarṇyaṃ cared bhaikṣam alābhe kurunandana ||
āpastambaḥ [1.3.25] |
sarvaṃ lābham āharan gurave sāyaṃ prātar amatreṇa bhikṣācaryaṃ caret |
sarvaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ nivedayed ity arthaḥ | lābhaṃ labdham | amatreṇa [kāṃsyavyatiriktena tāmrādipātreṇa] | “bhikṣācaryaṃ caret” kurvīta |
yamaḥ |
hastadattā tu yā bhikṣā vyañjanaṃ salilāni ca |
bhuktvā tv aśucitāṃ yāti dātā svargaṃ na gacchati ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
bhaikṣaṃ cared apramattaḥ śuciḥ śānto jitendriyaḥ |
apramattaḥ avahitaḥ |
bhikṣānuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.3.31–35] |
tat samāhṛtyopanidhāya ācāryāya prabrūyāt | tena pradiṣṭaṃ bhuñjīta | vipravāse guror ācāryakulāya | tadvipravāse ‘nyebhyo ‘pi śrotriyebhyaḥ | nātmaprayojanaś caret |
[119] pradiṣṭam anujñātam | vipravāse asaṃnidhāne | ācāryakulāya ācāryaputrādeḥ | nātmaprayojanaḥ gurvādibhyo ‘nivedayan | gurusamavāye anekagurusaṃnidhāne yam anubaddhas tasmin kāle tasyādadhīta ity arthaḥ |
tathā [1.7.15–18] |
samāvṛtto mātre dadyāt | mātā bhartāraṃ gamayet | bhartā gurum | dharmakṛtyeṣu yojayet |
samāvṛttaḥ vidyāṃ gṛhītvā trivṛto samāvṛtta eva pitṛgṛhasthito ‘trābhimataḥ |
gautamaḥ [2.46–48] |
nivedya gurave ‘nujñāto bhuñjīta | asaṃnidhau tadbhāryāputrasabrahmacāri-sadbhyaḥ | vāgyatas tṛpyann alolupyamānaḥ saṃnidhāpyodakam |
sabrahmacārī sahādhyāyī | sadbhyaḥ sādhubhyaḥ |
manuḥ [2.51] |
samāhṛtya tu tadbhaikṣaṃ yāvad annam amāyayā |
nivedya gurave ‘śnīyād ācamya prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
āhāraśuddhau satvaśuddhir ity ācāryāḥ | sa dvividhaḥ | ṛtasaṃmito ‘mṛtasaṃmitaś ca | krodhadambhasevāsaṃvyavahāravarjitaṃ [120] [ṛtasaṃvitam] | bhaikṣam avokṣitaṃ paryagnikṛtam ādityadarśitaṃ gurave niveditam anujñātam amṛtasaṃmitam | prātas tad aśnan brahmacārī brahmasiddhim avāpnoti |
saṃvyavahāraḥ maitryādi | paryagnikṛtaṃ pari sarvato agniḥ kṛto yatra |
vyāsaḥ |
prakṣālya pāṇipādu tu bhuṅktvā cāpa upaspṛśet |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [12.18–20] |
prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta | tūṣṇīṃ sāṅguṣṭhaṃ kṛtsnagrāsaṃ graset | na ca śabdaṃ kuryāt |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
haviṣo hutvā saṃtarpyāgniṃ samidhaṃ datvā annaṃ yad annam aśnātīty uktvānumantrayet | triḥ sviṣṭakṛtam agnikāryaṃ kṛtvānujñāto bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ prāṅmukhaḥ ity āha |
nāty uccaiḥ sukham āsīta samadeho ‘nnatatparaḥ |
kālenādyaṃl laghu kṣipram alpam adyād dravottaram ||
haviṣaḥ bhikṣāto havirbhūtasyānnasya | dravottaraṃ draveṇa naktādinā bhojanasamāptiṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
[121]        hārītaḥ |
prāṅmukhaḥ āyuṣkāmo ‘śnīyāt | yaśo’rthī dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ | śrīkāmaḥ pratyaṅmukhaḥ | nātyāśayet | nāsane ‘śnīyāt | na kārṣṇāyase | na mṛtpātre | nāvakīrṇe |
nātyāśayet nātyaśanaṃ kuryāt | avakīrṇe apavitre |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.31] |
kṛtāgnikāryo bhuñjīta vāgyato gurvanujñayā |
āpośanakriyāpūrvaṃ satkṛtyānnam akutsayan ||
“haviṣā hutvā” ityādipaiṭhīnasivacanānusāreṇa “kṛtāgnikārya” iti tad evātrāgnikāryaṃ matam | “satkṛtya” bhūtānām utpattisthitiṣu hetutayā dhyātvā | “akutsayan” annadoṣam akīrtayan |
tathā [1.32] |
brahmacarye sthito naikam annam adyād anāpadi |
brāhmaṇaḥ kāmam aśnīyāt śrāddhe vratam apīḍayan ||
ekam annam ekabhikṣām | anāpadi āpadi bhikṣāsthānābhāvād ekāpi | śrāddhe tv anāpady api | vratam apīḍayan [122] madhumāṃsādi varjayan | brāhmaṇo yatheṣṭam aśnīyāt, na kṣatriyavaiśyau |
rājanyavaiśyayoś caiva naitat karma vidhīyate |
iti manuvacanāt [2.190] |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.1.25–26] |
sa ced vyādhīyīta kāmaṃ guror ucchiṣṭaṃ bhaiṣajyārthaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt | yenecchet tena cikitseta |
yena iti brahmacāriniṣiddhena madhumāṃsādināpi gurūcchiṣṭena |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
akāmāpannaṃ madhu vājasaneyake na duṣyati |
akāmāpannaṃ pramādādinā āpatitaṃ bhakṣitam |
manuḥ [2.54–55] |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyet prasīdec ca pratinandec ca sarvaśaḥ |
pūjitaṃ hy aśanaṃ nityaṃ balam ūrjaṃ prayacchati ||
prasādaḥ krodhādidoṣahānena manaso ‘nākulatvam | pratinandanaṃ samṛddhyāśaṃsanam | balaṃ śāmartham | ūrjam utsāham |
[123]        brahmapurāṇe |
annaṃ dṛṣṭvā praṇamyādau prāñjaliḥ kathayet tataḥ |
asmākaṃ nityam astv etad iti bhaktyātha vandayet ||
manuḥ [2.56] |
nocchiṣṭaṃ kasyacid dadyān nādyāc caiva tathāntarā |
na caivātyaśanaṃ kuryān na cocchiṣṭaḥ kvacid vrajet ||
antarā sāyaṃprātaḥkālayor madhe iti medhātithiḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.3.36–45; 1.4.1–7] |
bhuktvā svayam amatraṃ prakṣālayet | nocchiṣṭaṃ kuryāt | aśaktau bhūmau nikhanet | apsu vā praveśayet | āryāya vā paryavadadhyāt | antardhine vā śūdrāya | proṣito bhaikṣād agnau kṛtvā bhuñjīta | bhaikṣaṃ haviṣā saṃstutaṃ tatrācāryo devatārthe | āhavanīyārthe ca | taṃ bhojayitvā | yad ucchiṣṭaṃ prāśnāti | havirucchiṣṭam eva tat | yad anyāni dravyāṇi yathālābham upaharati dakṣiṇā eva tāḥ | sa eṣa brahmacāriṇo yajño nityavrataḥ | na cāsmai śrutipratiṣiddham ucchiṣṭaṃ dadyāt | yathā kṣāralavaṇamadhumāṃsānīti | etenānye niyaamā vyākhyātāḥ |
paryavadadhyāt ekatra bhājane kṛtvā samīpe dhārayet | antardhine ācāryadāsāya | proṣitaḥ ācāryapryojanenānyatra gataḥ | haviṣā haviṣṭvena (?) | yad ucchiṣṭam ācāryo bhojayitvotsṛjati | na ca iti, yathā kṣāralavaṇādīni śrutau pratiṣiddhatvān na dīyante evaṃ sākṣād ucchiṣṭam |
[124]        tathā |
pituḥ jyeṣṭhasya ca bhrātur ucchiṣṭaṃ bhoktavyam | dharmavipratipattāv abhojyam |
dharmavipratipattau adhārmikatve |
grāsasaṃkhyāṃ prakramya vasiṣṭha-āpastamba-hārīta-yamāḥ |
aparimitaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |

athācamanam
devalaḥ |
bhuktvācāmed athoktena vidhānena samāhitaḥ |
śodhayen mukhahastau ca samṛdbhir gharṣaṇair api ||
tatra manuḥ [1.53] |
upaspṛśya dvijo nityam annam adyāt samāhitaḥ |
bhuktvā copaspṛśet samyag adbhiḥ khāni ca saṃspṛśet ||
gautamah [1.37–41] |
prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā śaucam ācaret | śucau deśa āsīno dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ jānvantarā kṛtvā yajñopavītyā maṇibandhāt pāṇī prakṣālya vāgyato kṛdayaṃ spṛśaṃs triś catur vā apa ācāmet | [dviḥ parimṛjyat | pādau cābhyukṣet | khāni copaspṛśec chīrṣanyāni] |
‘vā’ iti bhāvaśuddhyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ | maṇibandhāt kaṇṭhabāhusaṃdhideśāt | hṛdayaspṛśaḥ hṛdantargatāḥ | khāni indriyāṇi | śirṣaṇyāni śirasi jātāni |
[126]        hārītaḥ |
ā maṇibandhāt pāṇī prakṣālya | ā jaṅghābhyāṃ pādau ca | jñātiśraiṣthyakāmo ‘nnādyakāmo vā dakṣiṇe caraṇāṅguṣṭhe pāṇim avasthāpya prāṇān ālabhya khāni upaspṛśet |
devalaḥ |
prathamaṃ prāṅmukhaḥ sthitvā pādau prakṣālayec chanaiḥ |
udaṅmukho vā daivatye paitṛke dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
śikhāṃ badhvā vasitvā dve nirṇikte vāsasī śubhe |
tūṣṇīṃ bhūtvā samādhāya nodgacchen na vilokayan ||
samadhāya mana iti śeṣaḥ | udgacchan uttiṣṭhan | vilokayan diśa iti śeṣaḥ, “diśaś cānavalokayan” iti śaṅkhavacanāt |
tathā |
na gacchan na śayānaś ca na calan na paraṃ spṛśan |
na hvalan naiva saṃjalpan nātmānaṃ cāpi vīkṣayan ||
hvalan kampamānaḥ | ātmānaṃ svahṛdayam | vīkṣayan iti curādiṣu bahulanidarśanāt svārthe ṇic |
keśān nīvīm adhaḥkaryaṃ na spṛśan dharaṇīm api |
yadi spṛśati caitāni bhūyaḥ prakṣālayet karam ||
adhaḥkāryaṃ nābher adhaḥ pradeśam | na spṛśan kareṇeti śeṣaḥ |
tathā |
ity evam adbhir ājānu prakṣālya caraṇau pṛthak |
[127]        hastau cāmaṇimandhābhyāṃ paścāād āsīta saṃyataḥ ||
athāpaḥ prathamāt tīrthād dakṣiṇāt triḥ pibet samam |
aśadvam anavastrāvam abahirjānv abudbudam ||
prathamāt brāhmāt | anavastrāvaṃ yathā na sravati tathā |
dvis tathāṅguṣṭhamūlena parimṛjyāt punar mukham |
nāgrāṅgulyā na pṛṣṭair vā parimṛjyāt kathaṃcana ||
tataḥ kṛtvāṅgulisparśaṃ dṛgghrāṇaśrotranābhiṣu |
mūrdhānaṃ caraṇau cādbhiḥ saṃprokṣyātha śucir bhavet ||
pṛṣṭhaiḥ aṅgulīpṛṣṭhahastaiḥ |
vyāsaḥ |
trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dvir unmṛjyāt tato mukham |
pādāv abhyukṣya mūrdhānam abhyukṣet tadanantaram ||
akṣiṇī nāsike karṇāv oṣṭhau ca tadanantaram |
tataḥ spṛśen nābhideśaṃ punar āpaś ca saṃspṛśet ||
viṣṇuḥ [62.5] |
[anagnyuṣṇābhir aphenilābhir na śūdraikakarāvarjitābhir akṣārābhir adbhiḥ śucau deśe svāsīno ‘ntarjānuḥ prāṅmukhaś codaṅmukho vā] tanmanāḥ sumanāś cācāmet |
tanmanāḥ ācamanamanāḥ |
narasiṃhapurāṇe |
dakṣiṇaṃ tu karaṃ kṛtvā gokarṇākṛtivat purā |
triḥ pibed vīkṣitaṃ toyam āsyaṃ dviḥ parimārjayet ||
[128]        bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
samau ca caraṇau kṛtvā tathā baddhaśikho nṛpa |
atyantasukhitāṃ cāpi tyaktvā rājan sudūrataḥ ||
tathā |
ghanāṅgulikaraṃ kṛtvā ekāgraḥ sumanā dvijaḥ |
aṅguṣṭhena pradeśinyā cālabhed akṣiṇī nṛpa ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ ca nāsikām ālabhen nṛpa |
madhyamābhir mukhaṃ nityaṃ saṃspṛśet kurunandana ||
kaniṣṭhakāṅguṣṭhakābhyāṃ karṇāv ālabhate tu saḥ |
aṅgulībhis tathā bāhum aṅguṣṭhena tu maṇḍalam ||
nābheḥ kurukulaśreṣṭha śiraḥ sarvābhir eva tu ||
dakṣaḥ [2.20.227] |
anenaiva vidhānena ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt |
prakṣālya pādau pāṇī ca triḥ pibed ambu vīkṣitam ||
anena vakṣyamāṇena |
saṃvṛtyāṅguṣṭhamūlena dviḥ pramṛjyāt tato mukham |
saṃhṛtya tisṛbhiḥ pūrvamāsyam evam upaspṛśet ||
“mukhaṃ samvṛtya ca tataḥ pramṛjyāt” ity anvayaḥ | evam iti savṛtyety arthaḥ |
tahā [2.24–26] |
aṅguṣṭhena pradeśinyā ghrāṇaṃ paścād anantaram |
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ ca cakṣuḥśrotre punaḥ punaḥ ||
nābhiṃ kaniṣṭhāṅguṣthābhyāṃ hṛdayaṃ tu talena vai |
sarvābhis tu śiraḥ paścād vāhū cagreṇa saṃspṛśet ||
[129] pradeśinī tarjanī | pūrvam āsyaṃ spṛṣṭvā paścād ghrāṇam | anantaraṃcakṣuṣī, punaḥ śrotre, punar nābhiṃ spṛśed ity anvayaḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ [10.7–9]
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayogena spṛśen nāsāpuṭadvayam |
aṅguṣṭhamadhyayogena spṛśen netradvayaṃ tataḥ ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu śravaṇe samupaspṛśet |
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena spṛśet skandhadvayaṃ tataḥ ||
sarvāsām eva yogena nābhiṃ ca hṛdayaṃ tathā |
saṃspṛśec ca tathā śīrṣam ayam ācamane vidhiḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
aṅguṣṭhena pradeśinyā nāsike saṃspṛśet | aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ ca netre | kanīnikāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ śrotre | madhyamikayā ca mukham | aṅguṣṭhena ca nābhim | sarvābhiḥ śiraḥ | pradeśinī vāyuḥ, anāmikā sūryaḥ, kanīnikā maghavā, madhyamikā prajāpatiḥ, agnir aṅguṣṭhaḥ | tasmāt tenaiva saha sarvāṇi sthānāni spṛśati | vāyuḥ sūrya indraḥ prajāpatir agnir ity etā devatā enaṃ punīyur iti |
tathā |
nāsikāṃ cakṣuṣī śrotre mukhaṃ nābhiṃ tataḥ śiraḥ |
spṛṣṭvā prāṇān yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pādau prokṣya tataḥ śuciḥ ||
savye ca pāṇau śeṣā āpo ninayet ||
prāṇān indriyāṇi | yathāsaṃkhyam iti, yasyendriyasya yāvatī saṃkhyā tām anatikramyety arthaḥ |
[130]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
triḥ prāśya dviḥ parimṛjya śiraś cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ ghrāṇaṃ hṛdayaṃ cālayet |
āpastambaḥ [1.16.1–8] |
tiṣṭhan nācāmet prahvo vā | āsīnas trir ācāmed hṛdayaṃgamābhir adbhiḥ | trir oṣṭhau primṛjet | divr ity eke | sakṛd upaspṛśet | dvir ity eke | dakṣiṇena pāṇinā savyaṃ prokṣya pādau śiraś cendriyāṇy upaspṛśet cakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre ca | athāpa upaspṛśet |
tiṣṭhan prahvo vā nācāmed ity arthaḥ “sakṛd upaspṛśet dvir ity eke” oṣṭhāv iti saṃbandhaḥ | savyaṃ pāṇim iti śeṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
prāṅmukhaḥ prāgudaṅmukho vopaviśyāntar ūrvor aratnī kṛtvā trir apo hārdaṃ pibet | dviḥ pramṛjyauṣṭhau sakṛn mūrdhānaṃ cakṣuḥśrotre nābhiṃ hṛdayaṃ pādau cābhyukṣyopaspṛśya prayato bhavati |
śaṅkhḥ [9.10–13] |
triḥ prāśnīyād yad ambhas tu prītās tenāsya devatāḥ |
brahmā viṣṇuś ca rudraś ca bhavatīty anuśuśruma ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva prīyete parimārjanāt |
nāsatyadasrau prīyete spṛṣṭe nāsāpuṭadvaye ||
spṛṣṭe locanayugme tu prīyete śaśibhāskarau |
karṇayugme tathā spṛṣṭe prīyete tv anilānalau ||
skandhayoḥ sparśanād eva prīyante sarvaparvatāḥ |
[131]        nābhisaṃsparśanān nāgāḥ prīyante cāsya nityaśaḥ ||
saṃspṛṣṭe hṛdaye cāsya prīyante sarvadevatāḥ ||
mūrdhnaḥ saṃsparśanād asya prītas tu puruṣo bhavet |
nāsatyadasrau aśvinau |
indriyasparśānantaram bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
yad bhūmāv udakaṃ vāri samutsṛjati mānavaḥ |
vāsukipramukhān nāgān tena prīṇāti bhārata ||
vinā yajñopavītena tathā muktaśikho dvijaḥ |
aprakṣālitapādas tu ācānto ‘py aśucir bhavet ||
bahirjānur upaspṛśya ekahastārpitair jalaiḥ |
sopānatkas tathā tiṣṭhan naiva śuddhim avāpnuyāt ||
[devalaḥ] |
sopānatko jalastho vā mukakeśo ‘pi vā naraḥ |
uṣṇīṣī vāpi nācāmed vastreṇāveṣṭya vā śiraḥ ||
na śaucaṃ varṣadhārābhir ācared vedatattvavit |
marīciḥ |
na bahirjānu tvarayā nāsanastho na cotthitaḥ |
na pādukāstho nācittaḥ śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ ||
upaspṛśya dvijo nityaṃ śuddhaḥ pūto bhaven naraḥ |
bhuktvāsanastho ‘py ācāmen nānyakāle kadācana ||
vyāsaḥ |
śiraḥ prāvṛtya kaṇṭhaṃ vā muktakacchaśikho ‘pi vā |
akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucam ācānto ‘py aśucir bhavet ||
[132]        apaḥ pāṇinakhāgreṇa ācāmed yas tu brāhmaṇaḥ |
surāpānena tat tulyam ity evam ṛṣir abravīt ||
brahmapurāṇe |
kaṇṭhaṃ śiro vā prāvṛty [rathyāpaṇa]gato ‘pi vā |
akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucam ācānto ‘py aśucir bhavat ||
āpaṇaḥ krayavikrayabhūmiḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.8.10] |
pādaprakṣālanoccheṣaṇena nācāmet, yady ācāmed bhūmau srāvayitvācāmet |
uccheṣaṇena avaśiṣṭena |
āpastambaḥ [1.15.4–5] |
na varṣadhārāsv ācāmet | tathā pradarodake | taptābhiś cākāraṇāt |
pradaraḥ svayaṃ vidīrṇā bhūmiḥ | akaraṇāt vyādhyādivyatirekeṇa |
[133] apa ācāmed ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [3.35–36] |
pradarād yā gos tarpaṇasamarthāḥ syuḥ | na varṇarasaduṣṭābhir yāś ca syur aśubhāgamāḥ |
aśubhāgamāḥ aśubhā gatayaḥ | aśubhatvaṃ ca gater ninditadeśakālādiveśeṣāt | varṇarasaduṣṭāḥ dravyāntarasaṃsargadūṣitarasavarṇāḥ |
ācāmed ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
uddhṛtaparipūtābhir adbhir avekṣitābhir akṣārābhir anadhiśritābhir aphenābhir abudbudābhiḥ | na śūdrāśucyekapāṇyāvarjitābhiḥ |
paripūtābhiḥ nirastāpadravyābhiḥ | na śūdrāśucyekapāṇyāvarjitābhiḥ iti sāmānyenaikapāṇyā-varjitenācamananiṣedhāt | “bhūmigatāsv apsv ācamya prayato bhavati | yaṃ vā prayata ācāmayet” ity āpastambavacaneṇāvarjane prayataparaniyamāc ca, svayam āvarjitenācamanaṃ na śuddhihetur ity uktaṃ bhavatīti | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kṛtvā cāvaśyakarmāṇy ācāmec chaucavittamaḥ |
tata iti prakṛtatvāt kamaṇḍalor ity anvayaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.4.20–21] |
nāgnyudakaśeṣeṇa vṛthā karmāṇi kurvītācāmed vā | pāṇisaṃkṣubdhenodakenaika-pāṇyāvarjitena ca nācāmet |
[134] agnyudakaśeṣeṇa agnir paryukṣaṇādyarthopāttodakaśeṣeṇa, vṛthā yato ‘taḥ karmāṇi na kurvītety arthaḥ | saṃkṣubdhena āloḍitena |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.22] viṣṇuḥ [62.9] |
hṛtkaṇṭhatālugābhis tu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ dvijātayaḥ |
śuddhyeran strī ca śūdraś ca sakṛt spṛṣṭābhir antataḥ ||
antataḥ āsyāntarāvayave | anena cābhakṣaṇaṃ vivakṣitam,
trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dviḥ pramṛjyāt tato mukham |
śārīraṃ śaucam anvicchan strīśudraṃ tu sakṛt sakṛt ||
iti mahārṇavalikhitmanuvacanāt [5.139] |
śuddha ity anuvṛttau brahmapurāṇe |
strī śūdro vātha nityāmbhaḥkṣālanāc ca karoṣṭhayoḥ |
śūdrādhikāre gautamaḥ [10.52] |
ācamanārthe padaprakṣālanam evaike |
ācamanārthe ācamanarūpe prayojane |
[135]
ācamananimittam
ācamananimittam ity atra tu manu-aṅgiro-bṛhaspatayaḥ |
suptvā kṣutvā ca bhuktvā ca niṣṭhīvyoktvānṛtāni ca |
pītvāpo ‘dhyeṣyamāṇaś ca ācāmet prayato ‘pi san ||
ācamananimittaviṣaye tu vāyupurāṇe |
niṣṭhīvane tathābhyaṅge tathā pādāvasecane |
[ucchiṣṭasya ca saṃbhāṣād aśucyupahatasya ca ||
saṃdeheṣu ca sarveṣu śikhāṃ muktvā tathaiva ca | ]
vinā yajñopavītena nityam evam upaspṛśet ||
[ucchiṣṭasyāpi saṃsparśe darśane cāntyajanmanām | ]
viṣṇuḥ [22.75–76] |
kṣutvā suptvā bhuktvā bhojanādhyayanepsuḥ pītvā snātvā niṣṭhīvya vāsaḥ paridhāya rathyām ākramya mūtrapurīṣe kṛtvā pañcanakhāsthy asnehaṃ spṛṣṭvācāmet | cāṇḍālamlecchasaṃbhāṣaṇe ca |
brahmapurāṇe |
kṣute śleṣmaparityāge dhīte vā bhakṣite sati |
avarṇasya ca saṃbhāṣe supte vā dantadhāvane ||
ācamya prayato bhūtvā tataḥ śuddho bhaven naraḥ |
[136] dhīte pīte | “dheṭ pāne” ity asya niṣṭhāyāṃ rūpam | avarṇo atra cāṇḍālādiḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ |
kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca snātvā bhoktumanās tathā |
bhuktvā kṣutvā tathā suptvā pītvā cāmbho ‘vagāhya ca ||
ācamanavidhyantaram devalaḥ |
retomūtraśakṛnmokṣe bhojane ‘dhvapariśrame |
śaucam evaṃvidhaṃ proktam īṣaṣ cānyatra dṛśyate ||
yair ācamanāṅgair vinā “ācānto ‘tha śucir bhavet” ity uktam, tadirāṅgatyāgenāpi “retomūtra” ityādyuktanimittād anyatrācamane kiṃcid aṅgatyāgenāpi tathāvidhaṃ saṃpadyate |
[tathā]
ucchiṣṭaṃ mānavaṃ spṛṣṭvā bhojyaṃ prāpya vinaśyati |
tathaiva hastau pādau ca prakṣālyācamya śuddhyati ||
tathāvidhaṃ ucchiṣṭam |
tathā |
yad ambhaḥ śaucanirmuktaṃ kṣitiṃ prāpya vinaśyati |
prakṣāḷyāśuciliptaṃ ca spṛṣṭvā vācamya śudhyati ||
śaucanirmuktaṃ śaucapāṇīyāmatrāt kṣiptam, tat spṛṣṭvā, tatsaṃbandhād bhūmimārdāṃ spṛṣṭvā, tathā “prakṣālyāśuciliptaṃ ca” aśuciliptam aṅgaṃ prakṣālya ca, spṛṣṭvā ca tathaivācamya śuddhyatīty arthaḥ |
[137]        āpastambaḥ [1.15.23] |
mūtraṃ kṛtvā purīṣaṃ vā mūtrapurīṣalepān annalepān uccheṣa[lepān retasaś ca ye] lepās tān prakṣālya pādau cācamya prayato bhavati |
annalepān annamayodvartanādikṛtalepān | uccheṣam ucchiṣṭam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ucchiṣṭaretomūtraṃ saṃspṛśyonmṛjyācamya prayato bhavati triḥ prakṣālya ca taṃ deṣam |
deśam ucchiṣṭādiliptam |
ācamane vakṣyamāṇe hārītaḥ |
rathyām ākramya suṣupsuḥ kṛtamūtrapurīṣo bhakṣayitvā pāṇiṃ pādaṃ prakṣālyācāmet |
āpastambaḥ [1.16.14–15] |
svapne kṣavathū śiṅghāṇikāśrvālambhe lohitasya keśānām agner gavāṃ brāhmaṇasya striyāś cālambhe mahāpathaṃ ca gatvāmedhyaṃ copaspṛśyāprayataṃ ca manuṣyaṃ nīvīṃ ca paridhāyāpa upaspṛśet | ārdraṃ vā śakṛd oṣadhīr bhūmiṃ vā |
śiṅghāṇikāśru nāsikānirgataḥ śleṣmā netrajalaṃ ca, [tayoḥ ālambhe sparśe] | atrāgnigo]brāhmaṇānāṃ sparśe yadācamanaṃ tadvihitasparśetarasparśamātranimittaṃ na tu [138] tatsparśajanyāprāyatyanimittakam | mahāpatho rājamārgaḥ | nīvīṃ paridhāya granthiṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.10.17] |
nīvīṃ visraṃsya paridhāyāpa upaspṛśet | ārdraṃ tṛṇaṃ gomayaṃ bhūmiṃ vā samupaspṛśet |
visraṃsya muktvā |
āpastambaḥ [1.15.7–11] |
riktapāṇir vayasa udyamyāpa upaspṛśet | śaktiviṣaye na muhūrtam apy aprayataḥ syāt | nagno vā | nāpsu sataḥ prayamaṇaṃ vidyate | uttīrya tv ācāmet |
riktapāṇinā loṣṭādirahitapāṇinā | vayase pakṣiṇe | udyamya utkṣipya | prakramāt pāṇim iti śeṣaḥ | prayamaṇaṃ prāyatyaṃ śaucam iti yāvat |
yamaḥ |
uttīryodakam ācamya avatīrya upaspṛśet |
evaṃ syāc chreyasā yukto varuṇaś caiva pūjitaḥ ||
uttīrya niṣkramya | avatīrya praviśya |
[139] jalam ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
nottared anupaspṛśya |
uttaret saṃtaret |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
antarudaka ācānto ‘ntaḥ śuddho bhavati | bahirudaka ācānto bahir eva śuddhaḥ syāt | tasmād antar ekaṃ bahir ekaṃ ca kṛtvā pādam ācāmet, sarvatra śuddho bhavati |
pārāśaraḥ [12.19–21] |
kṣute niṣṭhīvane caiva dantocchiṣṭe tathānṛte |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet ||
prayāgādīna tīrthāni gaṅgādyāḥ saritas tathā |
viprasya dakṣiṇe karṇe nityaṃ tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ ||
ādityo varuṇaḥ somo agnir vāyus tathaiva ca |
viprasya dakṣiṇe karṇe santīti manur abravīt ||
āpastambaḥ [1.16.10–13] |
śyāvāntaparyantāv oṣṭhāv upaspṛśyācāmet | na śmaśrubhir ucchiṣṭo bhavaty antarāsye sadbhir yāvan na hastenopaspṛśati | ya [140] āsyād bindavaḥ patanta upalabhyante teṣv ācamanaṃ vihitam | ye bhūmau na teṣv ācāmed ity eke |
śvāntaparyantau vilomakau |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.69–72] |
kṣutvā niṣṭhīvya vāsaś ca paridhāyācamed budhaḥ |
[kṣue ‘valīḍhe vānte ca tathā niṣṭhīvanādiṣu] ||
kuryād ācamanaṃ sparśaṃ gopṛṣṭhasyārkadarśanam |
kurvītālambanaṃ cāpi dakṣiṇaśravaṇasya vai ||
yathāvibhavato hy etat pūrvābhāve tataḥ param |
na vidyamāne pūrvokte uttaraprāptir iṣyate ||
atha dvirācamananimittāni
tatra āpastambaḥ [1.16.9] |
bhokṣyamāṇas tu prayato ‘pi dvir ācāmet, dviḥ parimṛjet sakṛd upaspṛśet |
etac ca bhokṣamāṇasyaiva niyataṃ sakṛd oṣṭhopasparśanam [?] | sakṛd na dviḥ | parimārjane ‘pi dvir eva iti vyavasthā |
vyāsaḥ |
prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca bhuñjāno dvir upaspṛśet |
yājñavalkya [1.196] |
snātvā pītvā kṣute supte bhuktvā rathyopasarpaṇe |
[141]        ācāntaḥ punar ācāmed vāso viparidhāya ca ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
mūtrapurīṣaniṣṭhīvanādiṣu śuktavākyābhidhāneṣu punaḥ punar upaspṛśet |
śuktaṃ paruṣam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kalilakāsaśvāsāgame rathyācatvaraśmaśānakrānteṣv ācāntaḥ punar ācāmet |
kalilaṃ kaṭhinaśleṣmā | śvāso atra vikṛto vyāyāmādikṛtaḥ | catvaraṃ bhūtādīnāṃ balidānasthānam |
athācamanāpavādaḥ
tatra manuḥ [5.141] |
nocchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo ‘ṅgaṃ na yanti yāḥ |
na śmaśrūṇi gatāny āsyaṃ na dantāntaradhiṣṭhitam ||
mukhyāḥ mukhe bhavāḥ | yanti gacchanti | śmasrūṇi mukhalomāni | āsyaṃ gatāni mukhaṃ praviṣṭāni | dantāntaḥ dantamadhye | adhiṣṭhitaṃ praviṣṭam |
gautamaḥ [1.44] |
dantaśliṣṭeṣu dantavad anyatra jihvābhimarśanāt |
yāvaj jihvāyāṃ dantebhyo bhedena nopalabhyanta ity arthaḥ |
tathā [1.45] |
prāk cyuter ity eke |
[142] saty api jihvābhimarśane yāvan na cyavate tāvac chucir ity eke manyante |
tathā [1.46] |
cyuteṣv āsrāvavad vidyān nigirann eva tacchuciḥ |
devalaḥ |
bhojane dantalagnāni nirhṛtyācamanaṃ caret |
dantalagnam asaṃhāryaṃ lepaṃ manyeta dantavat ||
na tatra bahuśaḥ kuryād yatnam uddharaṇe punaḥ |
bhavec cāśaucam atyantaṃ tṛṇavedhād vraṇe kṛte ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [3.39] |
na ca śmaśrugato lepaḥ |
aśuciḥ ity śeṣaḥ |
tathā [3.40] |
dantavad dantalagneṣu yac cāpy antarmukhe bhavet |
ācāntasyāvaśiṣṭaṃ syān nigirann eva tacchuciḥ ||
ācāntasya yad avaśiṣyate pramādāt tan nigirann eva śucir bhavati |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
bhūmigatā bandavaḥ parāmṛṣṭāḥ pūtā vipruṣaḥ śuddhā dviromaklinneṣv ācāmet |
bindavaḥ atra ācamanabindavaḥ | parāmṛṣṭāḥ spṛṣṭāḥ | pūtāḥ nāprāyatyam āpādayanti | aṅgapatitā api [143] romadvayārdrībhāvam āpādayituṃ samarthā eva ācamananimittaṃ bhavanti |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.195] |
mukhajā vipruṣo medhyās tathācamanabindavaḥ |
śmaśru cāsyagataṃ dantaśiṣṭaṃ tyaktvā tataḥ śuciḥ ||
manuḥ [5.141] |
spṛśanti bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān |
bhaumikais te samā jñeyā na tair aprayato bhavet ||
atri-aṅgirasau |
madhuparke ca some ca apsu prāṇāhutīṣu ca |
nocchiṣṭas tu bhaved vipro yathātrer vacanaṃ tathā ||
apsu iti prāṇāhutisāhacaryaprāptāpośānaviṣayam, ācamanamadhye udakapānaviṣayaṃ vā, anyatrodakapāne “pītvāpo ‘dhyeṣyamāṇaś ca” ity ācamanavidheḥ |
parāśaraḥ [7.33] |
tāmbūlekṣuphale caiva bhuktsnehānulepane |
madhuparke ca some ca nocchiṣṭaṃ manur abravīt ||
[144]        śātātapaḥ |
dantalagne phale mūle bhakṣye snehe tathaiva ca |
tāmbūle cekṣudaṇḍe ca nocchiṣṭo bhavati dvijaḥ ||
phale kaṭutiktakaṣāye jātīphalādau, satām ācārāt |
mukhaśuddhisādhane vasiṣṭhaḥ [3.42] |
pracarann avyavahāreṣu ucchiṣṭaṃ yadi saṃspṛśet |
bhūmau nikṣipya tat pātram ācamya pracaret punaḥ ||
ucchiṣṭaśabdo ‘trācamanārhāśuciviṣayaḥ | snānādyarhāśucivacanatve tv ācamanamātravidhivirodhāt |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
dravyahast ucchiṣṭo nidhāyācamyābhyukṣed dravyam |
etac cābhyavahāryadravyaviṣayam |
yat tu,
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭe dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana |
anidhāyaiva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt ||
iti manuvacanam [5.143], tad abhyavahāryānnavyañjanavyatiriktadravyahastaviṣayam iti viśvarūpa-bhartṛyajñau |
[145]
baudhāyanaḥ [1.8.22–26] |
taijasaṃ ced ādāyocchiṣṭī syāt tad udasyācamyādāsyann adbhiḥ prokṣeta | sa ced anyenocchiṣṭī syāt tad udasyācamyādāsyann adbhiḥ prokṣeta | atha ced adbhir ucchiṣṭī cyāt tad udasyācamyādāsyann adbhiḥ prokṣeta | etad eva viparītam amatre | vānaspatye ca vikalpaḥ |
“anyenocchiṣṭī syāt” anyenocchiṣṭenocchiṣṭaḥ | adbhiḥ āmacanodakapānādau | “etad eva” ādānapakṣaṃ cātra viparītatvam | tat udasya parityajya ācāmet, na punar tad gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācāra 6–8; p. 310] |
pracaran svānnapāneṣu yadocchiṣṭam upaspṛśet |
bhūmau nidhāya tad dravyam ācāntaḥ pracaret punaḥ ||
araṇye ‘nudake rātrau cauravyāghrākule (?) pathi |
kṛtvā mūtrapurīṣaṃ ca dravyahasto na duṣyati ||
śaucaṃ tu kuryāt prathamaṃ pādau prakṣālayet tataḥ |
upaspṛśya tad abhyukṣya gṛhītaṃ śucitām iyāt ||
[146]
|| 6 ||
atha śaucam
tatra śaucādiśikṣā
manu-śaṅkhau | [Manu 2.69]
upanīya guruḥ śiṣyaṃ śikṣayec chaucam āditaḥ |
ācāram agnikāryaṃ ca saṃdhyopāsanam eva ca ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.15] |
upanīya guruḥ śiṣyaṃ mahāvyāhṛtipūrvakam |
vedam adhyāpayed enaṃ śaucācārāṃś ca śikṣayet ||
ācāraḥ gurvabhivādanādiḥ |
viṇmūtrotsargaḥ
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.12–15] |
[na kṛṣṭe sasyamadhye vā govraje janasaṃsadi |
na vartmani na nadyāditīrtheṣu puruṣarṣabha ||
nāpsu naivāmbhasas tīre śmaśāne na samācaret |
utsargaṃ vai purīṣasya mūtrasya ca visarjanam ||
[147]        udaṅmukho divā mūtraṃ viparītamukho niśi |
kurvītānāpadi prājño mūtrotsargaṃ ca pārthiva ||
tṛṇair āstīrya vasudhāṃ vastraprāvṛtamastakaḥ] |
tiṣṭhen nāticiraṃ tatra naiva kiṃcid udīrayet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.154] |
dūrād ucchiṣṭaviṇmūtrapādāmbhāṃsi samutsṛjet |
manuḥ [4.151] |
dūrād āvasathān mūtraṃ dūrāt pādāvasecanam |
ucchiṣṭānnaṃ niṣekaṃ ca dūrād eva samācaret ||
niṣeka ucchiṣṭadravyaprakṣepaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.2] |
ārāc cāvasathān mūtrapurīṣe kuryād dakṣiṇāṃ dakṣiṇāparāṃ vā |
gatvā ity ashyāhāryam | dakṣiṇāparā nairtī |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.31.3] |
astam ite ca bahirgrāmād ārāc cāvasathān mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet |
ārāt dūre |
[148]        manuḥ [4.49] |
tiraskṛtyoccaret kāṣṭhaṃ patraṃ loṣṭaṃ tṛṇāni vā |
niyamya prayato vācaṃ saṃvītāṅgo ‘vaguṇṭhitaḥ ||
tiraskṛtya antardhāya | prayataḥ śuciḥ | vācaṃ niyamya ity anvayaḥ | saṃvītaṃ nivītam, “saṃvītaṃ mānuṣam” ity taittirīyaśruteḥ | tadaṅge yasya “saṃvītāṅgaḥ” |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.10.10] |
śuṣkaṃ tṛṇam ayājñikaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ loṣṭaṃ vā tiraskṛtyāhorātrayor udagdakṣiṇāmukhaḥ prāvṛtyoccaret avamehed vā |
ayājñikaṃ yajñaprayojanakuśalapalāśādivyatiriktam | avamehet mūtraṃ kuryāt |
“viṇmūtrotsargaṃ kuryāt” ity anuvṛttau vāyupurāṇe |
śuṣkais tṛṇair vā kāṣṭhair vā patrair veṇudalena vā |
mṛnmayair bhājanair vāpi antardhāya vasuṃdharām ||
kamaṇḍalvadhikāre baudhāyanaḥ [1.7.3] |
mūtramurīṣe kurvan dakṣiṇe haste gṛhṇāti savye ācamanīyam |
[149]        hārītaḥ |
ghrāṇāsye vāsasā veṣṭayitvā mṛddhātrīṃ grīvāyām āsajya dakṣiṇabāhupārśve kamaṇdalum ādhāyotsṛjet |
[mṛddhātrīṃ mṛddhāraṇapātrīm | kamaṇḍaluṃ śaucārthajalapūrṇaṃ pātram | utsṛjet] utsargaṃ kuryārt | mūtrapurīṣe iti śeṣaḥ |
yamaḥ |
śiraḥ prāvṛtya kurvīta śakṛnmūtravisarjanam |
[ayajñiyair anārdraiś ca tṛṇaiḥ saṃchādya medinīm] ||
sāṃkhyāyanagṛhyam |
yedy ekavastro yajñopavītaṃ karṇe kṛtvā | nādityābhimukho na jaghanena | ahar udaṅmukho naktaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
na gaghanena iti atyantaṃ namratayā svasyaiva prasaktaṃ jaghanasāmukhyaṃ niṣidhyate | madhyāhne dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
devalaḥ |
sadaivodaṅmukhaḥ prātaḥ sāyāhne dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
viṇmūtram utsṛjen nityaṃ saṃdhyāsu parivarjayet ||
atreyaṃ vyavasthā — devalavacanānusārāt prātaḥsāyaṃkālayoḥ trimuhūrtātmakayoḥ udagdakṣiṇāmukhatvam | evaṃ ca [150] yamavacanaṃ tadatirikte pūrvāhṇe aparāhṇe ca pratyakprāṅmukhatāprāpakam |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.1] |
prāṅmukho ‘nnāni bhuñjīta uccared dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ | udaṅmukho mūtraṃ kuryāt pratyakpādāvasecanam |
“uccared dakṣiṇāmukha” iti rātrisāyāhnaviṣayam | yamadevalavacanāt pratyakpaścimābhi-mukhaṃ bhavati | idaṃ ca vacanaṃ saṃdhyākālavyatiriktakālaviṣayam |
hārītaḥ |
ubhe saṃdhye udaṅmukhaḥ |
manuḥ [4.51] |
chāyāyām andhakāre vā rātrāv ahani vā dvijaḥ |
yathāsukhamukhaḥ kuryāt prāṇabādhābhayeṣu ca ||
chāyāndhakārayoḥ prāṇapīḍāyāṃ vyāghrādibhaye ca diṅmukhaniyamo ‘nādeyaḥ ity uktam |
[151] punaḥ manuḥ [4.45] |
na mūtraṃ pathi kurvīta na bhasmani na govraje |
na phālakṛṣṭe na jale na cityāṃ na ca parvate ||
cityām agnicayanasthāne |
tathā [4.46–48] |
na jīrṇadevāyatane na valmīke kadācana |
na sasatveṣu garteṣu na gacchan nāpi ca sthitaḥ ||
na nadītīram āsādya na ca parvatamastake |
vāyvagnivipram ādityam apaḥ paśyaṃs tathaiva gāḥ ||
na kadācana kurvīta viṇmūtrasya visarjanam |
“na ca parvata” iti parvataniṣadhakena parvatamastakaniṣedhe siddhe ‘pi puns tanniṣedho doṣabhūyastvārthaḥ | “vāyuṃ paśyan” saṃmukham upalabhamānaḥ |
devalaḥ |
vāpīkūpanadīgoṣṭhacaityāmbhaḥpathibhasmasu |
agnau kāmye śmaśāne ca viṇmūtraṃ na samācaret ||
[152] caityaḥ grāmaprasiddho vṛkṣaḥ | kāmyaḥ kamanīyaḥ pradeśaḥ udyānādiḥ |
yamaḥ |
palvalāni taḍāgāni nadīprasravaṇāni ca |
nagagomayabhasmāni phālakṛṣṭaṃ ca varjayet ||
palvalam alpasaraḥ | taḍāgaṃ kṛtrimasaraḥ |
tathā |
tuṣāṅgārakapālāni devatāyatanāni ca |
rājamārgaśmaśānāni kṣetrāṇi ca khalāni ca ||
uparuddho na seveta chāyāṃ dṛśyaṃ catuṣpatham |
dṛśyaṃ śobhanam |
tathā |
udakaṃ codakāntaṃ ca panthānaṃ ca vivarjayet |
varjayed vṛkṣamūlāni caityaśvabhrabilāni ca ||
“panthānaṃ ca vivarjayet” ity anenaiva rājamārgacatuṣpathayor niṣedhe siddhe punas tayor upādānaṃ doṣādhikyajñāpanārtham |
hārītaḥ |
na catvaropadvārayor mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt | na tīrthe | na sasyapūrṇe | na yajñabhūmau | na yajñiyānāṃ vṛkṣāṇām adhastāt |
[153] tīrthaṃ jalāvataraṇamārgaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [6.12] |
nopte, na śādvalopajīvyāsu |
śādvalaḥ haritaḥ snigdho bhūbhāgaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [60.6]
na coṣare |
tathā [60.13] |
na parāśucau |
tathā [60.20] |
nākāśe |
ākāśe aṭṭālakādisthita ity arthaḥ |
tathā [60.22–23] |
na pratyanilānalendvarkastrīgurubrāhmaṇānāṃ ca | naivāvaguṇṭhitaśirāḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.30.16–17] |
chāyāyāṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet | svāṃ tu chāyāym avamehet |
“svāṃ tu chāyāṃ” svāṃ chāyāṃ pratīty arthaḥ |
[154]        tathā [1.30.18] |
na sopānatko mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt |
tathā [1.30.23] |
agnim ādityam apo brāhmaṇaṃ godevatāś cābhimukho mūtrapurīṣayoḥ karma varjayet |
gautamaḥ [19.13] |
na vāyvagniviprādityāpo devatā gāś ca pratipaśyan vā mūtrapurīṣāmedhyāny udasyet |
yamaḥ |
pratyādityaṃ na meheta na paśyed ātmanaḥ śakṛt |
dṛṣṭvā sūryam udīkṣeta gām agniṃ brāhmaṇāṃs tathā||
pratyādityaṃ pratyanilaṃ pratigāṃ ca prati dvijam |
prati somaṃ prati jalaṃ prati saṃdhye ca nityaśaḥ ||
hanti mehayataḥ prajñāṃ prati panthānam eva ca |
mehayanti ca etāṃś ca te bhavanti gatāyuṣaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
rathyācatvaratīrtheṣu śmaśānāyataneṣu ca |
apadhvaṃsam avāpnoti āyuṣā ca viyujyate ||
āhāraṃ ca rahaḥ kuryān nirhāraṃ caiva sarvadā |
yujyate ca śriyā gupte prakāśe bādhyate śriyā ||
[155]
śaucavidhiḥ
tathā hārītaḥ |
loṣṭena pramṛjīta śuṣkakāṣṭhena vā |
pāraskaraḥ [2.7.16] |
svayaṃ praśīrṇena kāṣṭhena vā pramṛjīta |
gautamaḥ [9.15] |
na parṇaloṣṭāśmabhir mūtrapurīṣāpakarṣaṇaṃ kuryāt |
hārīte tu loṣṭavidhir itaradravyābhāve |
āpastambaḥ [1.10.24] |
aśmānaṃ loṣṭam ārdrān oṣadhivanaspatīn ūrdhvān ācchidya mūtrapurīṣayoḥ śundhanaṃ varjayet |
devalaḥ |
ā śaucān notsṛjec chiśnaṃ prasrāvoccārayoḥ svayam |
yājñavalkya-baudhāyanau |
gṛhītaśiśnaś cotthāya mṛdbhir abhyuddhṛtair jalaiḥ |
gandhalepakṣayakaraṃ śaucaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ ||
atandritaḥ saṃjātālasyaḥ |
[156]        brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
uddhṛtyodakam ādāya mṛttikāṃ caiva vāgyataḥ |
udaṅmukho divā kuryād rātrau ced dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
tathā |
sunirṇikte mṛdaṃ dadyān mṛdante tv apa eva ca |
sunirṇikte suprakṣālitapāṇipāde |
dakṣaḥ |
ekā liṅge tu savye trir ubhayor mṛddvayaṃ smṛtam |
savye ubhayoḥ iti ca hastābhiprāyeṇa | triḥ tisraḥ ity arthaḥ | etāvan mūtrotsarge śaucam |
tisro ‘pāne daśaikasminn ubhayoḥ sapta mṛttikāḥ |
gṛhasthaśaucam ākhyātaṃ triṣv anyeṣu yathākramam ||
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturthasya caturguṇam ||
manuḥ [5.134] |
viṇmūtrotsargasiddhyarthaṃ mṛdvāry ādeyam arthavat |
daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca śuddhiṣu dvādaśasv api ||
manu-viṣṇū |
vasā śukram asṛṅ majjā mūtra viṭ ghrāṇakarṇaviṭ |
śreṣmāśru dūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ ||
ekā liṅge gude tisras tathaivaikakare daśa |
ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyāḥ mṛdaḥ śuddhim abhīpsatā ||
[157]        etac chaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
triguṇaṃ tu vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam ||
yamaḥ |
śiśne tv ekā gude tisro vāme pāṇau caturdaśa |
tataḥ punar ubhābhyāṃ ca dātavyāḥ sapta mṛttikāḥ ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
mehane mṛttikāḥ sapta liṅge dve parikīrtite |
ekasmin viṃśatir haste dvayor devāś caturdaśa ||
mehanaṃ apānam |
tathā |
tisras tu mṛttikā deyāḥ kṛtvā tu nakhaśodhanam |
tisras tu pādayor deyā śaucakāmasya nityaśaḥ ||
śaucam etad gṛhasthānāṃ tathā gurunivāsinām |
dviguṇaṃ syād vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ triguṇaṃ bhavaet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mṛttikāṃ saṃgṛhya ekā liṅge apāne pañca ekasmin haste daśa ubhayoḥ sapta mṛttikā |
etac chaucaṃ gṛhāsthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
triguṇaṃ ca vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ ca caturguṇam ||
gandhalepakṣayād vā prakṣālyācamya prayato bhavati |
[158]        hārītaḥ |
ekāntam utkramya ekā liṅge tisro mṛdo ‘pāne dadyāt, na vārśodoṣāt pāṇiṃ prakṣālya daśa savye ṣaṭ pṛṣṭhe saptobhyābhyām | dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām, triguṇaṃ vānaprasthānām, caturguṇaṃ bhikṣūṇām |
brahmapurāṇe |
dve liṅge mṛttike deye gude sapta yathākramam |
dvātriṃśad vāmahaste ca tathā deyās tu mṛttikāḥ ||
dvayos tu ṣoḍaśānyās tu punaḥ sapta ca sarvadā |
pādayor dve gṛhītvā ca suprakṣālitapāṇinā ||
dvir ācamya tataḥ śuddhaḥ smṛtvā viṣṇuṃ sanātanam |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.10.11–14] |
mūtre mṛdādbhiḥ prakṣālanam | triḥ pāṇau | tadvat purīṣe paryāyaḥ | tisraḥ [pāyoḥ pāṇeś ca] |
[yamaḥ |
śiśne tv ekā gude tisro vāme pāṇau caturdaśa |
tataḥ punar ubhābhyāṃ ca dātavyāḥ sapta mṛttikāḥ || ]
śaucam etad gṛhasthasya dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
triguṇaṃ ca vanasthasya bhikṣor etac caturguṇam ||
[159]        śaṅkhaḥ |
mehane mṛttikāḥ [sapta liṅge dve parikīrtite |
ekasmin viṃśatir haste dvayor deyāś caturdaśa || ]
devalaḥ |
yāvat sādhv iti manyeta tāvac chaucaṃ vidhīyate |
pramāṇaṃ dravyasaṃkhyā vā na śiṣṭair upadiśyate ||
etadvacanānusārāt manūktamṛtsaṃkhyādhikottaramṛtsaṃkhyānāṃ gandhalepānuvṛttiśaṅkayā vyavasthā | yā caturādyā vihitā saṃkhyā tadanuṣṭhāne tu saṃkhyādhigame ‘pi vihitasaṃkhyāparyantaṃ śaucam avaśyaṃ kartavyam |
tad uktaṃ dakṣeṇa |
nyūnādhikaṃ na kartavyaṃ śaucaśuddhim abhīpsatā |
prāyaścitteṣu yujyeta vihitātikrame kṛte || iti |
śaucānantaraṃ śaṅkhalikhitau |
kamaṇḍalum upaspṛśya prakṣālya pāṇipādau cācamyeśānaṃ manasā dhyāyet |
īśānaṃ sarveśvaram |
śaucānantaram hārītaḥ |
tisṛbhiḥ pādau prakṣālya gomayena mṛdā vā kamaṇḍaluṃ parimṛjya pūrvavad upaspṛśyādityaṃ somam agniṃ vīkṣet |
[160] upaspṛśya divā ādityam agniṃ rātrau somam agniṃ vā vīkṣeta |
dakṣaḥ |
yathoditaṃ divā śaucam ardhaṃ rātrau vidhīyate |
āturasya tadardhaṃ syāt tadardhaṃ ca pathi smṛtam ||
ardhaprasṛtimātrā tu prathamā mṛttikā smṛtā |
dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā syāt tadardhā parikīrtitā ||
liṅge ‘py atra samākhyātā triparvī pūryate yathā |
dātavyam udakaṃ tāvad yāvat syān mṛtparikṣayaḥ ||
mudhādravyeṇa śuddhiḥ syān na kleśo na dhanavyayaḥ |
mudhādravyeṇa sulabhadravyeṇa |
yasya śauce ‘pi śaithilyaṃ vṛttaṃ tasya parīkṣitam ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.10.16–17] |
valmīkamūṣikotkhātāṃ mṛdam antarjalāt tathā |
śaucāvaśiṣṭāṃ gehāc ca nādadyāl lepasaṃbhavām ||
antaḥ prānyavapannāṃ ca halotkhātāṃ ca pārthivaḥ |
parityajen mṛdas tv etāḥ sakalāḥ śaucasādhane ||
“antaḥ prāṇyavapannāṃ” antaḥ madhe prāṇibhiḥ avapannāṃ vyāptām |
[161]        yamaḥ |
āharen mṛttikāṃ vipraḥ kūlāt sasikatāṃ tathā |
nākhūtkṛṣṭān na valmīkāt pāṃsulān na ca kardamāt ||
na mārgān noṣarāc caiva śaucaśiṣṭāṃ parasya vā |
etās tu varjayed vidvān vṛthāśaucaṃ hi tanmatam ||
ākhuḥ mūṣikaḥ |
[162]
|| 7 ||
atha saṃdhyāvidhiḥ
tatra viṣṇuḥ [28.1–2] |
atha brahmacāriṇo gurukulavāsaḥ | saṃdhyādvayopāsanam |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi saṃdhyopāsananirṇayam |
ahorātrakṛtaiḥ pāpair yām upāsya pramucyate ||
sarvāvastho ‘pi yo vipraḥ samdhyopāsanatatparaḥ |
brāhmaṇyāc ca na hīyeta anyajanmagato ‘pi san ||
sarvāvasthaḥ ninditasevādikarmarataḥ, samyak śaucādyasamartho pīty arthaḥ |
manuḥ [1.101] |
pūrvāṃ samdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhan naiśam eno vyapohati |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīno malaṃ dahati divākṛtam ||
śātātapaḥ |
anṛtaṃ madyagandhaṃ ca divā maithunam eva ca |
[163]        punāti vṛṣalasyānnaṃ bahiḥ saṃdhyā hy upāsitā ||
vṛṣalo atra adhārmikaḥ | tad uktaṃ bhārate |
vṛṣo hi bhagavān dharmas tasya yaḥ kurute tv alam |
vṛṣalaṃ taṃ vidur devāḥ || iti |
yamaḥ |
yad ahnā kurute pāpaṃ karmaṇā manasā girā |
āsīnaḥ paścimāṃ saṃdhyāṃ prāṇāyāmaiś ca hanti tat ||
yad rātryā kurute pāpaṃ karmaṇā manasā girā |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyām upāsīnaḥ prāṇāyāmair vyapohati ||
saṃdhyām upāsate ye tu satataṃ saṃśitavratāḥ |
vidhūtapāpās te yānti brahmalokaṃ sanātanam ||
etac cājñānādikṛtapāpaviṣayam,
divā vā yadi vā rātrau yad ajñānakṛtaṃ bhavet |
trikālasaṃdhyākaraṇāt tat sarvaṃ vipraṇaśyati ||
iti yājñavalkyavacanāt |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.7.18] |
yad upasthakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ padbhyāṃ vai yat kṛtaṃ bhavet |
bāhubhyāṃ manasā caiva vācā vātha kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
sāyaṃ saṃdhyām upasthāya tena tasmāt pramucyate |
[164]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
savratī bahiḥ saṃdhyām upāsīta |
savratī saha vratena yad vartate ‘dhyayanādi tat savratam, tadvān brahmacārī |
manuḥ [2.219] |
nainaṃ grāme ‘bhinimlocet sūryo nābhyudayāt kvacit |
“enaṃ” brahmacāriṇam | abhinimlocet astam gacchet | grāmād bahiḥ brahmacāriṇā sūryodayāstamayau saṃpādyāv iti tātparyārthaḥ |
śātātapaḥ |
gṛheṣu prākṛtī saṃdhyā goṣṭhe śataguṇā smṛtā |
nadīṣu śatasāhasrā anantā śivasaṃnidhau ||
prākṛtī ekaguṇaphalā |
saṃvartaḥ |
prātaḥ saṃdhyāṃ sanakṣatrām upāsīta yathāvidhi |
[165]        sādityāṃ paścimāṃ saṃdhyām ardhāstamitabhāskarām ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
hrāsavṛddhī tu satataṃ divasānāṃ yathākramam |
saṃdhyā muhūrtamātraṃ tu hrāsaṃ vṛddhau samā smṛtā ||
muhūrtamātraṃ nāḍīdvayamātram |
tathā |
saṃdhau saṃdhyām upāsīta nāstage nodgate ravau |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.7.12–14] |
supūrvām ap pūrvām upakramyodite āditye samāpnuyāt | anastamita upakramya supaścād api paścimām | saṃdhyayoś ca saṃpattāv ahorātrayoḥ saṃtatiḥ |
supūrvāṃ suṣṭhu pūrvakālām, bahuṣu nakṣatreṣu vidyamāneṣu | apiśabdād alpaeṣu nakṣatreṣu vidyamāneṣu | supaścād api bahunakṣatradarśanāvadhi | apiśabdād alpanakṣatradarśane ‘pi samāpnuyāḍ ity arthaḥ |
gautamaḥ [2.14–17] |
apām upasparśanam | eke godānādi | bahiḥsaṃdhyatvaṃ ca | tiṣṭhet pūrvām āsīna uttarāṃ sajyotiṣyā jyotiṣo darśanād vāgyataḥ |
[166] eke ācāryāḥ godānavratād ārabhya snānaṃ bahiḥsaṃdhyatvaṃ ca vadantīty arthaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [28.2–4] |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japet tiṣṭhan paścimām āsīnaḥ | kāladvayam ekāgnikarmakaraṇam | apsu daṇḍavan majjanam |
hārītaḥ |
asnāyī snāyī vā daṇḍavat |
atra ca prayato ‘snāyī aprayataḥ snāyīti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ, “aprayato ‘bhiṣiktaḥ prayato vānabhiṣiktaḥ” iti baudhāyanavacanāt [2.7.2] |
āpastambaḥ [1.2.30] |
nāpsu ślādhamānaḥ snāyāt |
ślādhamānaḥ snānena svāpotkṛṣṭaṃ malaṃ prakāśayan |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.7.1–4] |
athātaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavidhiḥ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tīrthaṃ gatvāprayato ‘bhiṣiktaḥ prayato vānabhiṣiktaḥ prakṣālita[167]pāṇipādaḥ apa ācamya “agniś ca mā manyuś ca” iti sāyam apaḥ pītvā “sūryaś ca mā manyuś ca” iti prātaḥ, sapavitreṇa pāṇinā surabhimatyā, abliṅgābhiḥ, vāruṇībhiḥ hiraṇyavarṇābhiḥ, pāvamānībhiḥ, vyāhṛtibhir anyaiś ca pavitrair ātmānaṃ prokṣya prayato bhavati |
surabhimatī dadhikrāvṇa ityādikā | abliṅgābhiḥ āpo hi ṣṭhā ityādibhiḥ | vāruṇyaḥ varuṇadevatākā yac cid dhi te viśa ityādyās tisraḥ | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā iti catasraḥ | pāvamānyaḥ pavamānaḥ suvarjanaḥ ityādyāḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
ambho ‘vagāhanaṃ snānaṃ vihitaṃ sārvavarṇikam |
mantravat prokṣaṇaṃ cāpi dvijātīnāṃ viśiṣyate || iti |
[168]        kiṃ ca
sarvakarmaṇāṃ caivārambheṣu prāk saṃdhyopāsanakālāc caitena pavitrasamūhenātmānaṃ prokṣya plavato bhavati |
etac ca “agniś ca mā manyuś ca” “pavitrai⌈ ityantaṃ mārjanaṃ snātasyāpi | yogiyājñavalkīyaṃ “śaṃ na āpa” ityādi “aghamarṣaṇasūktena” ityantaṃ mantrair mārjanaṃ tu aprayatasyaiva karmāṅgamukhyasnānāśaktau draṣṭavyam |
asāmarthyāc charīrasya kālaśaktyādyapekṣayā |
mantrasnānāditaḥ sapta kecid icchanti sūrayaḥ ||
iti, kāladoṣād asāmarthāt iti ca yogiyājñavalkyavacanāt | sarvakarmaṇāṃ śrāddhādīnām api | pavitrasamūhaḥ surabhimatyādir anantarokto mantrasamūhaḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ sodakena pāṇinā prāṅmukhaḥ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayed aparimitakṛtvaḥ prāṇāyāmaśo vā śatakṛtva ubhayataḥ praṇavāṃ sasaptavyāhṛtikāṃ manasā vā daśakṛtvaḥ tribhiś ca prāṇāyāmaiḥ śrānto brahmahṛdayena vāruṇībhyāṃ rātim upatiṣṭhate “imaṃ me varuṇa” “tatvāyāmi” iti dvābhyām evaṃ prātaḥ prāṅmukhas tiṣṭhan maitrībhyām ahar upatiṣṭhet “mitrasya carṣaṇī ghṛto” “mitro janān yātayati prajānan” iti dvābhyām iti |
[169] “aparimita” iti pūrvoktasaṃkhyātiriktasaṃkhyatvaṃ vivakṣitam | “prāṇāyāmaśo vā śatakṛtvaḥ” iti, “prāṇāyāmaśaḥ” prāṇāyāmena | etad uktaṃ bhavati — yāvadbhiḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ sāvityrā śataṃ bhavati tāvataḥ prāṇāyāmān kuryād iti | prāṇāyāmaś cātra prāṇanirodhamātram abhihitam, pūrvaṃ sāvitrīmātrasya prakrāntatvāt | “ubhayataḥ praṇavāṃ sasaptavyāhṛtikāṃ manasā vā daśakṛtvaḥ” iti, “ubhayata” ādāv ante coṃkāraṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | eteṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ pakṣāṇāṃ śaktyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ | “tribhiś ca prāṇāyāmaiḥ śrānto brahmahṛdayena” iti, pūrvoktānām ekaṃ kalpam anuṣṭhāya tato brahmahṛdayena praṇavena trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā taiḥ “śrāntaḥ” khinna ity arthaḥ | evaṃbhūtaḥ san “imaṃ me varuṇa” “tatvāyāmi” iti dvābhyāṃ vāruṇībhyāṃ rātrim upatiṣṭhate | varuṇadevatākatvād rātreḥ varuṇopasthānena tasyā upasthānaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavatīti | etena “maitrībhyām ahar upatiṣṭhate” iti vyākhyeyam | tathā ca taittirīyaśrutiḥ: “maitraṃ vā ahar vāruṇī rātriḥ” iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.22] |
snānam abdaivatair mantrair mārjanaṃ prāṇasaṃyamaḥ |
sūryasya cāpy upasthānaṃ gāyatryāḥ pratyahaṃ japaḥ ||
tathā [1.24–25] |
prāṇān āyamya saṃprokṣya tṛcenābdaivatena tu |
japann āsīta sāvitrīṃ pratyag ā tārakodayāt ||
saṃdhyāṃ prāk prātar evaṃ hi tiṣṭhed ā sūryadarśanāt |
“abdaivataiḥ” “āpo hi ṣṭhā” ityādibhiḥ | “mārjanaṃ” devatīrthasthaiḥ kuśaiḥ śirasy udakaprakṣepaḥ,
[170]        śiraso mārjanaṃ kuryāt kuśaiḥ sodakabindubhiḥ |
iti candogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanavacanāt | “sūryasya cāpy upasthānaṃ” saureṇa mantreṇa “ud u tyaṃ jātavedasaṃ” ityādinā prakāśanam | “pratyak” paścimābhimukhaḥ | “prāk” pūrvābhimukhaḥ |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
oṃkāraṃ vyāhṛtīḥ sapta gāyatryā śirasā saha |
āpo hi ṣṭhā ṛcas tisraḥ sūktaṃ caivāghamarṣaṇam ||
ādityarakṣaṇārthāya sāyaṃ prātar dine dine |
sṛṣṭaṃ svayaṃbhuvā pūrvaṃ brahmaṇas tan mukhaṃ smṛtam ||
tathā |
yāvanto ‘syāṃ vikarmasthāḥ pṛthivyāṃ bāliśā dvijāḥ |
teṣāṃ ca pāvanārthāya saṃdhyā sṛṣṭā svayaṃbhuvā ||
saṃdhyā upāsitā yena viṣṇus tena upāsitaḥ |
vikarmasthāḥ vihitakarmātikramaniṣiddhakarmakartāraḥ |
tathā |
triṃśatkoṭyas tu vikhyātā mandehā nāma rākṣasāḥ |
prādravanti sahasrāṃśum udayantaṃ dine dine ||
ahany ahani te sarve sūryam icchanti khāditum |
atha sūryasya teṣāṃ ca yuddham āsīt sudāruṇam ||
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve ṛṣayaś ca tapodhanāḥ |
[171]        saṃdhyeti tam upāsītā yat kṣipanti mahaj jalam ||
oṃkārabrahmasaṃyuktaṃ gāyatryā cābhimantritam |
tena dahyanti te daityā vajrībhūtena vāriṇā ||
taṃ sūryam | oṃkārabrahmasaṃyuktam oṃkārarūpeṇa brahmaṇā saṃyuktam |
etad viditvā yo vipraḥ upāste śaṃsitavrataḥ |
dīrgham āyuḥ sa vindeta sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
tathā |
pūrvā saṃdhyā tu gāyatrī sāvitrī madhyamā smṛtā |
yā bhavet paścimā saṃdhyā sā vijñeyā sarasvatī ||
śvetā bhavati sāvitrī gāyatrī raktavarṇikā |
kṛṣṇā sarasvatī jñeyā saṃdhyātrayam udāhṛtam ||
tathā |
trayāṇāṃ caiva devānāṃ brahmādīnāṃ tu saṃgamaḥ |
saṃdhiḥ surāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ tena saṃdhyā udāhṛtā ||
garīyasīti bhūtānāṃ yasmāt paitrī tanur hi sā |
“paitrī tanuḥ” pitāmahasya pitṝn sṛṣṭavataḥ |
saṃdhatte tu pare sūkṣme nirguṇe guṇabodhini |
[172]        pradhānapuruṣātīte saṃdhatte saṃdhir ucyate ||
“saṃdhatte pare” sarvataḥ śreṣṭhe, paramātmanīti yāvat,
saṃdhyā upāsitā yena viṣṇus tena upāsitaḥ |
iti pūrvam uktatvāt |  sūkṣme asthūle | nirguṇe vikāraguṇarahite | guṇabodhini guṇānāṃ mānādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ jñātari | pradhānapuruṣātīte pradhānam avyaktaṃ, puruṣo jīvaḥ, tāv atīte tābhyāṃ bhinne | saṃdhite saṃdhānaṃ kurvati, sūryasyopasthitatvāt | sūrye adhikaraṇe punaḥ saṃdhatte tasmin sūrye saṃdhyayā devatayā saṃbandha iti boddhavyam, “saṃdhyeti samupāsīnā” ity uktatvāt | etenaitat saṃdhānadvayanimittas tasmin kāle saṃdhivyapadeśa ity abhisaṃdhiḥ |
tathā |
saṃdhyātrayaṃ tu kartavyaṃ dvijenātmavidā sadā |
ātmavidā śarīrendriyavyatiriktaṃ, nityākaraṇe paraloke duḥkhabhāginam ātmānaṃ vijānatā |
prasaṅgāt snānam āha |
ubhe samdhye tu snātavyaṃ brāhmaṇaiś ca gṛhāśritaiḥ |
tisṛṣv api ca saṃdhyāsu snātavyaṃ tu tapasvinā ||
tapasvī vānaprasthādiḥ |
kāladoṣād asāmarthān na śaknoti yathā hy asau |
tadā jñātvā ṛṣibhyas tu mantrair dṛṣṭaṃ tu mārjanam ||
[173] asāmarthyāt rogādyupaghātāt | kāladoṣāt atyantaṃ śītādeḥ | “mantrais tu mārjanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ ṛṣibhyo jñātvā” kuryād ity arthaḥ |
śaṃ na āpas tu drupadā āpo hi ṣṭhāghamarṣaṇam |
etaiś caturbhir ṛgmantrair mantrasnānam udāhṛtam ||
“śaṃ na āpo dhanvānyā” iti kaṭhabrāhmaṇe | caturbhiḥ iti sūktāpekṣayā, ṛcāṃ bhūystvāt |
snānam abdaivatair mantrair mārjanaṃ prāṇasaṃyamaḥ |
aghamarṣaṇasūktena aśvamedhāvabhṛtsamam ||
aprāyatye samutpanne snānam eva tu kārayet |
pūrvoddiṣṭais tathā mantrair anyathā mārjanaṃ smṛtam ||
tathā |
aghamarṣaṇasūktasya ṛṣir evāghamarṣaṇaḥ |
[ānuṣṭubhaṃ bhavec chando bhāvavṛttaś ca daivatam ||
aśvamedhāvabhṛtake viniyogas tu kalpitaḥ | ]
sarvapāpāpanodārthaṃ smṛtikārair udāhṛtaḥ ||
[“bhāvavṛttaḥ” bhāve bhāvātmake jagati vṛttaḥ pravṛtto dhātā | ]
tathā |
tatvabhūtāṇḍapiṇḍasya sthityutpattilayaṃ tathā |
sūkṣme ‘smin vyāhṛtaṃ sarvaṃ mantrārtho ‘syāyam ucyate ||
[174] ‘tatvabhūtāṇḍapiṇḍasya” brāhmāṇḍasya, jagata iti yāvat | “sūkṣme” ca brahmaṇi “sṛṣṭisthitilayā” “asmin” sūkte vyāhṛtāḥ |
hatvā lokān apīmāṃs trīn japet trir aghamarṣaṇam |
yathāśvamedhāvabhṛta eva tan manur abravīt ||
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanam ||
tathā |
sindhudvīpo bhaved ārṣaṃ gāyatrī chanda eva tu |
āpas tu daivataṃ proktaṃ viniyogas tu mārjane ||
sarvatra pāvanaṃ karma abliṅgānām udāhṛtam |
kokilo rājaputras tu drupadād ārṣam ucyate ||
ānuṣṭhubhaṃ bhavec chanda āpaś caiva tu daivatam |
sautrāmaṇy avabhṛthake snāne tadviniyojanam ||
drupadā nāma sā devī yajurvede pratiṣṭhitā |
antarjale trir āvartya mucyate brahmahatyayā ||
apaḥ pāṇyoḥ samādāya triḥ paṭhed drupadām ṛcam |
tat toyaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu mantrāṇāṃ viniyogaṃ dvijottama |
saṃdhyām upāsate yadvad yathāvat tan nibodhata ||
īśānyābhimukho bhūtvā śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ |
ācāntaḥ punar ācāmed ṛtam ity abhimantrya ca ||
āntaraṃ śuddhyati hy evam annapānamalīkṛtam |
[175]        trir āvartya tu sāvitrīṃ praṇavaṃ vyāhṛtīs tathā ||
mārjanaṃ ca tathā kṛtvā āpo hi ṣṭheti mārjayet |
sārdhām ṛcaṃ tu prakṣipya ūrdhvaṃ śardhām adhaḥ kṣipet ||
