adhobhāgavisṛṣṭāyām asurā yānti saṃkṣayam |
sarvatīrthābhiṣekas tu ūrdhvaṃ saṃmārjanād bhavet ||
[aghamarṣaṇasūktena mārjanaṃ kārayet tataḥ |
śaṃ na āpaś ca drupadāṃ vāmataḥ saṃprayojayet || ]
oṃkārapūrvāṃ gāyatrīm abliṅgādyaghamarṣaṇam |
sātatyaṃ brahma caitad vai purā dṛṣṭaṃ svayaṃbhuvā ||
evaṃ saṃmārjanaṃ kṛtvā bāhyaśuddhyarthakāraṇam |
tathābhyantaraśuddhyarthaṃ prāṇāyāmān samācaret ||
satatam eva sātatyaṃ nityānuṣṭheyatvāt | “brahma” brahmadevaḥ | “śaṃ na āpa” ityādi tu kāmyaviṣayam |
śaṅkhaḥ [9.8–10] |
ity evam uktvā kartavyaṃ tadā saṃmārjanaṃ jale |
āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ ||
hiraṇyavarṇeti ca vai ṛgbhiś catasṛbhis tathā |
śaṃ no devīr iti tathā śaṃ na āpas tathaiva ca ||
idam āpaḥ pravahatā tathā mantram udīrayet |
“ity evam uktvā” iti, “prapadye varuṇaṃ devaṃ” ityādi “māṃ ca rakṣantu sarvaśaḥ” ityantamantrasamūham |
ācamyaivaṃ purā proktās tīrthasaṃmārjane tu ye |
[176]        mantrās tair mantritaṃ toyaṃ mūrdhni bhūmau tathā kṣipet ||
kṣiptena mūrdhni toyena pāpam asya praṇaśyati |
bhūmau kṣiptena hanyante asurā devaśatravaḥ ||
vyāhṛtīḥ kīrtayec caiva tathaivāvyayam akṣaram |
“ācamyaivam” anantaroktācamanakalpena | “tīrthasaṃmārjane” antarjalamārjane “purā proktāḥ” āpo hi ṣṭādyāḥ | “avyayaṃ” oṃkārarūpam |
upaspṛśet tataḥ paścān mantreṇānena dharmataḥ |
antaś carasi bhūteṣu guhāyāṃ viśvatomukhaḥ ||
tvaṃ yajñas tvaṃ vaṣaṭkāra āpo jyotī raso ‘mṛtam |
“upaspṛśet” ācāmet |
ācamya ca tataḥ paścād ādityābhimukho jalam |
ud u tyaṃ jātavedeti mantreṇānena ca kṣipet ||
eṣa eva vidhiḥ proktaḥ saṃdhyayoś ca dvijātibhiḥ |
“eṣa eva” madhyāhnaproktaḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japet tiṣṭhann āsīnaḥ paścimāṃ tathā ||
tato japet pavitrāṇi pavitraṃ vā svaśaktitaḥ |
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvād dīrghaṃ āyur avāpnuvan ||
“pavitrāṇi” aghamarṣaṇādīni śaṅkhoktāni | “dīrghasaṃdhyatvam” japena dīrghakālavyāptā | etac ca dīrghasaṃdhyākaraṇaṃ dīrghāyuṣkāmasya | japetikartavyatāṃ naiyatikālike vakṣyāmaḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [Gobilasmṛti 2.1–17] |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi saṃdhyopāsanakaṃ vidhim |
[177]        anarhaḥ karmaṇāṃ vipraḥ saṃdhyāhīno yataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
savye pāṇau kuśān kṛtvā kuryād ācamanakriyām |
savyaḥ sopagrahaḥ kāryo dakṣiṇaḥ sapavitrakaḥ ||
“upagrahāḥ” bahavaḥ kuśāḥ |
rakṣānte vāriṇātmānaṃ pariveṣṭya samantataḥ |
śiraso mārjanaṃ kuryāt kuśaiḥ sodakabindubhiḥ ||
“ante” pūrvoktakarmānte, ātmānaṃ samantato vāriṇa veṣṭayitvā rakṣā kāryety arthaḥ |
praṇavo bhūr bhuvaḥ svaś ca sāvitrī ca tṛtīyakā |
abdaivatyas tṛcaś caiva caturtha iti mārjanam ||
bhūrādyāś tisra evaitā mahāvyāhṛtayo ‘vyayāḥ |
mahar janas tapaḥ satyaṃ gāyatrī ca śiras tathā ||
“āpo jyotī raso ‘mṛtaṃ brahma bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ” iti śiraḥ |
pratipratīkaṃ praṇavam uccārayed ante ca śirasaḥ ||
etā etāṃ sahaitena tathaibhir daśabhiḥ saha |
trir japed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate ||
kareṇoddhṛtya salilaṃ ghrāṇam āsajya tatra ca |
japed anāyatāsur vā triḥ sakṛd vāghamarṣaṇam ||
utthāyārkaṃ prati prohet trikeṇāñjalim ambhasaḥ |
“etāḥ” vyāhṛtīḥ | “etāṃ” gāyatrīm | “etena” śirasā | “ebhiḥ” praṇavaiḥ daśabhiḥ saha ity arthaḥ | “prohet” kṣipet | “trikeṇa” praṇavavyāhṛtigāyatryātmakena |
[178]        uc citram ity ṛgdvayena copatiṣṭhed anantaram ||
“ut” “ud u tyaṃ jātavedasam” ityādi | “citram” “citraṃ devānām” iti, “ud vayaṃ tamasas patiḥ” iti ca |
saṃdhyādvaye ‘py upasthānam etad āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
madhye tv ahana udaye ca bibhrāḍādīcchayā bhavet ||
“bibhrāḍādi” “bibhāḍ bṛhat” ity anuvākaḥ, śivasaṃkalpaḥ, maṇḍalabrāḥmaṇaṃ,
puruṣasūktaṃ ca |
tad asaṃsaktapārṣṇir vā ekapād ardhapād api |
kuryāt kṛtāñjalir vāpi ūrdhvabāhur athāpi vā ||
“tat” upasthānam | “asaṃsaktapārṣṇiḥ” pṛthivyām alagnagulphatalabhāgaḥ |
yatra syāt kṛcchrabhūyastvaṃ śreyaso ‘pi manīṣiṇaḥ |
bhūyastvaṃ bruvate tatra kṛcchrāc chreyo hy avāpyate ||
tiṣṭhed odayanāt pūrvāṃ madhyamām api śaktitaḥ |
āsītoḍūdgamāc cāntyāṃ saṃdhyāṃ pūrvatrikaṃ japan ||
etat saṃdhyātrayaṃ proktaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ yad adhiṣṭhitam |
yasya nāsty ādaras tatra na sa brāhmaṇa ucyate ||
saṃdhyālopasya cākartā snānaśīlaś ca yaḥ pumān |
taṃ doṣā nopasarpanti garutmantam ivoragāḥ ||
“ā uḍūdgamāt” ā tārakodayāt | “yadadhiṣṭhitaṃ” yadadhīnam |
[179]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
ācāravatāṃ dharmo hy āvaśyaka ity ācāro ṛṣīṇāṃ sthitiḥ devatānāṃ caritraṃ manuṣyāṇām | tena
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sve kāle vāparāṃ ciram |
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyātvā dīrgham āyur avāpnuyuḥ ||
“āvaśyakam” mūtrapurīṣādikarma | “ciram” ity ubhayasaṃdhyāsaṃbandhaḥ | “dīrghasamdhyatvam” japena dīrghakālavyāptā |
[manuḥ] [2.222] |
ācamya prayato nityam ubhe saṃdhye samāhitaḥ |
śucau deśe japan japyam upāsīta yathāvidhi ||
tathā [2.102] |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm arkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīnaḥ samyag ṛkṣavibhāvanāt ||
manuḥ [2.76–79] |
akāraṃ cāpy ukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ |
vedatrayān niraduhad bhūr bhuvaḥ svar itīti ca ||
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādaṃ pādam aduduhat |
tad ity ṛco ‘syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ ||
etad akṣaram etāṃ ca japan vyāhṛtipūrvikām |
saṃdhyayor vedavid vipro vedapuṇyena yujyate ||
[180] “etad akṣaram” praṇavam | “vedapuṇyena” vedajapuṇyena |
sahasrakṛtvas tv abhyasya bahir etat trikaṃ dvijaḥ |
mahato ‘py enaso māsāt tvacevāhir vimucyate ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.11.102] |
nityakālam upasthānaṃ saṃdhyayoḥ pārthiveṣyate |
anyatra sūtakāśaucavibhramāturabhītibhiḥ ||
vibhramaḥ bhayaṃ vinākulatā |
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃdhyā yena na vijñātā saṃdhyā na ca upāsitā |
jīvann eva bhavec chūdro loke bhavati ninditaḥ ||
manuḥ [2.44] |
nopatiṣṭhati yaḥ pūrvāṃ nopāste yaś ca paścimām |
sa śūdravad bahiṣkāryaḥ sarvasmād dvijakarmaṇaḥ ||
dakṣaḥ [2.27] |
saṃdhyāhīno ‘śucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
yad anyat kurute karma na tasya phalabhāg bhavet ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2.7.15] |
api cātra prajāpatigītau ślokāv udāharanti |
anāgatāṃ tu ye pūrvām atikrāntāṃ tu paścimām |
saṃdhyāṃ nopāsate viprāḥ kathaṃ te brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
[181]                sāyaṃ prātaḥ sadā saṃdhyāṃ na ye viprā upāsate |
kāmaṃ tān dhārmiko rājā śūdrakarmasu yojayet || iti |
śātātapaḥ |
anāgatāṃ tu yaḥ pūrvāṃ sādityāṃ caiva paścimām |
nopāsīta dvijaḥ saṃdhyāṃ sa dhṛṣṭo brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
aṅgirāḥ |
ubhe saṃdhye tu yo vipro maunī kāle samāhitaḥ |
divyavarṣasahasraṃ tu brahmaloke mahīyate ||
atra śaṅkha-vasiṣṭha-aṅgiro-bṛhaspati-paiṭhīnasi-baudhāyanāḥ |
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācāra 31] |
baddhvāsanaṃ niyamyāsūn smṛtvā ṛṣyādikaṃ tathā |
saṃnimīlitadṛṅ maunī prāṇāyāmaṃ samabhyaset ||
śaṅkhaḥ [12.7–12] |
ādau devatām ārṣaṃ chandaś ca saṃsmaret | [tataḥ sapraṇavā vyāhṛtīr ādāv āvartya gāyatrīm āvartayet] | athāsyāḥ savitā devatā, ṛṣir viśvāmitraḥ, gāyatrī chandaḥ | oṃkārasya praṇavākhyā | oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ svaḥ oṃ mahaḥ oṃ janaḥ oṃ tapaḥ oṃ satyam iti vyāhṛtayaḥ | āpo jyotī raso ‘mṛtaṃ brahma bhūr bhuvaḥ svar om iti śīrṣam |
[182]        dakṣaḥ [2.51] |
savitā devatā yasyāḥ mukham agnis tripāc ca yā |
viśvamitra ṛṣiś chando gāyatrī sā viśiṣyate ||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
prāṇasyāyamanaṃ kṛtvā ācāmet prayato ‘pi san |
āntaraṃ khidyate yasmāt tasmād ācamanaṃ smṛtam ||
[183]
|| 8 ||
athāgnikāryam
tatra saṃdhyāvidhyanantaram yājñavalkyaḥ [1.25] |
agnikāryaṃ tataḥ kuryāt saṃdhyayor ubhayor api |
manuḥ [2.186] bhaviṣye ca |
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād gṛhopari |
sāyaṃ prātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ ||
etena śiṣyādau dhāraṇaṃ nirastam |
āpastambaḥ [1.4.14–21] |
sadāraṇyād edhān āhṛtyādho nidadhyāt |
adhonidhānoparidhānayor vikalpaḥ |
nāstamit samiddhāro gacchet | agnim iddhvā parisamūhya samidha ādadhyāt sāyaṃ prātar yathopadeśam | sāyam evāgnipūjety eke | samiddham agniṃ pāṇinā parisamūhann eva samūhanyā | prāk tu yathākāmī |
[184] “samiddhāraḥ” samidhām āhartāraḥ | “yathopadeśam” gṛhyoktavidhināpi parisamūhanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
nāgnyudakaśeṣeṇa vṛthā karmāṇi kurvītācāmed vā |
“vṛthā karmāṇi” kevaladṛṣṭaprayojanāni |
baijavāpaḥ |
purāstamayāt prāgudīcīṃ diśaṃ gatvāhiṃsann araṇyāt samidha āharet | śuṣkā brahmavarcasakāmaḥ ārdrā annādyakāmaḥ ubhayair ubhayakāmaḥ | astamite tasminn agnau pūrvavad ādadhyāt |
“purāstamayāt” ity astamite sūte samidāharaṇaṃ niṣiddham | “prāgudīcī” aiśānī | “ahiṃsan” samidupayuktād adhikam achindan | “astam ite” sūrye | “tasminn agnau” āhavanīyānale | “pūrvavat” upanayanakālavat | “ādadhyāt” samidham iti śeṣaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
samāsiñcatv iti paryukṣya sāyaṃ prātaḥ paricared agniṃ sūryācandamasau somaṃ mitraṃ vāyum apo brāhmaṇāṃś ceti |
“paricaret” abhivādayed ity arthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
purā jagrāha vai mṛtyur hiṃsayan brahmacāriṇam |
agnis taṃ mokṣayāmāsa tasmāt paricared dhi tam ||
brahmacārī yadā tv agnāv ādadhyāt samidho na hi |
gṛhṇīyāt taṃ tadā mṛtyur ādadhyāt samidhas tataḥ ||
[185]
|| 9 ||
athābhivādanopasamgrahaṇe
tatra śaṅkhaḥ [2.4] |
prayataḥ kalyam utthāya snāto hutahutāśanaḥ |
kurvīta praṇato bhaktyā gurūṇām abhivādanam ||
viṣṇuḥ [28.14] |
kṛtasaṃdhyopāsanaś ca gurūṇām abhivādanaṃ kuryāt |
gautamaḥ [6.5] |
svanāma procyāham ayam ity abhivādo jñasamavāye |
“jñasamavāye” pratyabhivādanavidhijñasamāgame |
manuḥ [2.124–125] |
bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayed ante svasya nāmno ‘bhivādane |
nāmnā svarūpabhāvo hi bhobhāva ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ ||
“nāmnā” abhivādyasya | “bhobhāvaḥ” bho iti śabdaḥ | “asya” abhivādasya |
[186] “abhivādayīta” ity anuvṛttau sāṃkhyāyanagṛhyam [4.12.5–6] |
asāv ahaṃ bho ity ātmano nāmādiśya vyatyastapāṇiḥ asāv ity asya pāṇiṃ saṃgṛhyāśiṣam āśāste |
“asya” abhivādakasya |
manuḥ [2.126] |
yo na vetty abhivādasya vipraḥ pratyabhivādanam |
nābhivādyaḥ sa viduṣā yathā śūdras tathaiva saḥ ||
“abhivādasya” abhivādanasya | “pratyabhivādanam” āśīrvacanam, yathā “āyuṣmān bhava devadatta” iti | “nābhivādyaḥ” sarvathaiva nābhivādaned ity arthaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.5.12] |
brahmacārī sadā mahāntam apararātram utthāya guros tiṣṭhan prātarabhivādam abhivādayītāsāv ahaṃ bho iti |
abhivādanaṃ “abhivādaḥ” taṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ, odanaṃ pākaṃ pacatītivat
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.5.16–17] |
dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāḥmaṇo ‘bhivādayīta, uraḥsamaṃ rājanyo, madhyasamaṃ vaiśyo, nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ, prāñjaliḥ | plāvanaṃ ca nāmno ‘bhivādanapratyabhivādane ca pūrveṣāṃ varṇānām |
“nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ” madhyāpekṣayā nīcatvam | “plāvanam” plutakaraṇam | “nāmno” nāmadheyāntyasvarasyety arthaḥ | “pūrveṣāṃ” brahmakṣatraviśām | “abhivādya” iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
[187]        vasiṣṭhaḥ [13.44–46] |
yo vidyād abhivaditum ayam ahaṃ bho iti brūyāt | yaś ca na vidyāt | pratyabhivādanam āmantrite svaro ‘ntyo ‘sya plavate saṃdhyakṣaram apragṛhyam āyāubhāvaṃ ca yathā bhobhāv iti |
“yo vidyād abhivaditum” iti | abhivādane kṛte pratyabhivādanaṃ kartum ity arthaḥ | taṃ svanāmagrahaṇaviśeṣaṇena abhivādayet | yas (?) tu na jānīyāt pratyabhivādanaṃ taṃ prati “ayam ahaṃ bho” ity eva brūyād ity arthaḥ | caśabdāt striyaś ca | tathā ca manuḥ [2.123] |
nāmadheyasya ye kecid abhivādaṃ na jānate |
tān prājño ‘ham iti brūyāt striyaḥ sarvās tathaiva ca || iti |
kathaṃ punaḥ pratyabhivādanam | tad āha – “āmantrite” saṃbodhane prathamānte, “asya” nāmno, “antyaḥ” antyasvaraḥ, “plavatte” trimātraḥ kāryaḥ | “samdhyakṣaraṃ” ekārādi | “apragṛhyaṃ” pragṛhyasaṃjñārahitam | “āyāubhāvaṃ” āībhāvam āūbhāvam | caśabdāt plutaṃ ca prapadyata iti śeṣaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.14.20–21] |
abhivādayet sarvanāmnā striyaḥ | rājānaṃ vaiśyaṃ ca nāmnā, mātaram ācāryadāraṃ cety eke |
“sarvanāmnā” “ahaṃ bhoḥ abhivādaye” ity etāvanmātreṇa |”nāmnā” na sarvanāmnā, kiṃ tu svanāmnety ekīyaṃ matam |
manuḥ [2.72] |
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ |
[188]        savyena savyaḥ spṛṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ ||
baudhāyanaḥ [1.3.26–28] |
dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇena savyaṃ savyena copasaṃgrahaṇaṃ dīrgham āyuḥ svargaṃ cepsamānaḥ | kāmam anyasmai sādhuvṛttāya guruṇānujñāto “asāv ahaṃ bho” iti śrotre saṃspṛśya manaḥsamādhānārtham adhastāj jānvor ā padbhyām |
“ā padbhyām” iti, jānunī ārabhya pādaparyantaṃ spṛśed ity arthaḥ | “manaḥsamādhānārtham” abhivādyasyāvadhānārtham ity arthaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.5.18–23] |
udite tv āditya ācāryeṇa sametyopasaṃgrahaṇam | sadaivābhivādanam | upasaṃgrāhya ācārya ity eke | dakṣiṇena pāṇinā dakṣiṇaṃ pādam adhastād adhyabhimṛśya sakuṣṭhikam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt | ubhābhyām evobhāv abhipīḍayatopasaṃgrāhyāv ity eke |
“ubhābhyām” pāṇibhyām, “ubhau” pādau, “abhipīḍayatā” samyak pīḍayatā |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [13.41–43] |
guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam | ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvya[mātulān avaravayasaḥ pratyutthāyābhivādayet | ye caiva pādagrāhyās teṣāṃ ca bhāryā guroś ca mātāpita]rau |
ayaṃ tātparyārthaḥ | ṛtvigādīnām avaravayasāṃ pratyutthānaṃ saṃbhāṣaṇamātreṇa | “ye caiva pādagrāhyāḥ” pādopasaṃgrahaṇayogyāḥ [189] pūrvoktebhyo ṣaḍbhyo ‘nantaroktā “avaravayasaḥ” te ‘pi pratyutthāya saṃbhāṣyāḥ |
gautamaḥ [1.58] |
guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ prātaḥ |
gurvashikāre paiṭhīnasiḥ |
uttānābhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ dakṣiṇena dakṣiṇaṃ savyaṃ savyena pādāv abhivādayet |
punaḥ gautamaḥ [6.1–4] |
pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ samavāye ‘nvaham | abhigamya tu viproṣya | mātṛpitṛtatsabandhūnāṃ pūrvajānāṃ vidyāgurūṇāṃ tadgurūṇāṃ ca | saṃnidhāne parasya |
“samavāye” melake | “viproṣye” pravāsaṃ kṛtvāgatya | “tatsabandhūnām” mātṛpitṛsamānajātīyabandhūnāṃ mātulamātāmahapitṛvyapitāmahaprabhṛtīnām | “pūvajānām” jyeṣthabhrātṝṇām | “vidyāguravaḥ” aṅgādividyādhyāpitāraḥ | “tadgurūṇām” pūrvoktagurūṇām | “saṃnidhāne parasya” eteṣāṃ sarveṣām ekatra upasthāne yaḥ paraḥ pradhānabhūtas tasya prathamaṃ kartavyam upasaṃgrahaṇam |
āpastambaḥ [1.5.12–15] |
guros tiṣṭhan prātarabhivādanam abhivādayed asāv ahaṃ bho iti | samānagrāme ca vasatām anyeṣām api vṛddhatarāṇāṃ prāk prātarāśāt | proṣya ca samāgame | svargam āyuś cepsan |
[190]        sāṃkhyāyanagṛhyam [4.12.1–4] |
ahar ahar ācāryāyābhivādayet | gurubhyaś ca | sametya śrotriyasya | proṣya pratyetyāśrotriyasya |
“sametya” daivavaśāt samāgame | “aśrotriyasya proṣya pratyetya” na tu daivavaśāt samāgame ‘pīty arthaḥ |
patiṭhīnasiḥ |
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ caiva yac cānyad dharmasaṃhitam |
yato vidyāgamaṃ kuryāt taṃ pūrvam abhivādayet ||
“dharmasaṃhitaṃ” [dharmasaṃbaddham | “vidyāgamam” vidyāgrahaṇam | ]
manur [Manu 2.117–121] yamaś ca prathame, paiṭhīnasiś caturthe |
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ cāpi tathādhyātmikam eva ca |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ taṃ pūrvam abhivādayet ||
sāvitrīmātrasāro ‘pi varaṃ vipraḥ suyantritaḥ |
nāyantritas trivedo ‘pi sarvāśī sarvavikrayī ||
śayyāsane ‘dhyācarite śreyasā na samāviśet |
śayyāsanasthaś caivainaṃ pratyutthāyābhivādayet ||
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇā hy utkrāmanti yūnaḥ sthavira āyati |
pratyutthānābhivādābhyāṃ punas tān pratipadyate ||
abhivādanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopasevinaḥ |
catvāri samyag vardhante āyuḥ prajñā yaśo balam ||
[191] “laukikaṃ” arthaśāstrādi | “ādhyātmikaṃ” brahmapratipādakavidyā | “adhyācarite śreyasā” śreṣṭhena gurvādinā “na samāviśet” na samupviśet” | “tān” prāṇān |
bhaviṣye |
abhīti bhagavān viṣṇuḥ vādayāmīti śaṃkaraḥ |
dvāv etau pūjitau yena yaḥ karoty abhivādanam ||
manuḥ [2.132] |
bhrātur bhāryopasaṃgrāhyā savarṇāhany ahany api |
viproṣya tūpasaṃgrāhyā jñātisaṃbandhiyoṣitaḥ ||
“jñātayaḥ” pitṛpakṣāḥ | “saṃbandhinaḥ” mātṛpakṣāḥ śvaśurādayaś ca |
tathā [2.212] |
gurupatnī tu yuvatir nābhivādyeha pādayoḥ |
pūrṇaviṃsativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā ||
“pūrṇaviṃśativarṣeṇa” iti yauvanopalakṣaṇam | “guṇadoṣau” ca kāmajau sukhaduḥkhe |
tathā [2.216–217] |
kāmaṃ tu gurupatnīnāṃ yuvatīnāṃ yuvā bhuvi |
vidhivad vandanaṃ kuryād asāv aham iti bruvan ||
[192]        viproṣya pādagrahaṇam anvahaṃ cābhivādanam |
gurudāreṣu kurvīta satāṃ dharmam anusmaran ||
tathā [2.210] |
guruvat pratipūjyāḥ syuḥ savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ |
asavarṇās tu saṃpūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [32.5] |
hīnavarṇāṇāṃ gurupatnīnāṃ prāg abhivādanaṃ na pādopasaṃgrahaṇam |
abhivānadānuvṛttau gautamaḥ [6.6–8] |
strīpuṃyoge ‘bhivādanato ‘niyamam eke | nāviproṣya strīṇām amātṛpitṛvyabhāryābhaginīnām | nopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ bhrātur bhāryāṇāṃ śvaśrvāś ca |
“strīpuṃyoge” vivāhakāle strīṇāṃ gurutvāt pratyutthānābhivāde “aniyamam eke” manyanta ity arthaḥ | “abhivādanataḥ” abhivādane |
tathā [6.9] |
ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānāṃ tu yavīyasāṃ pratyutthānam anabhivādyāḥ |
[193] “yavīyasāṃ” kaniṣṭhānām | “nopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ bhrātur bhāryāṇām” ity asavarṇāviṣayam,
bhrātur bhāryopasaṃgrāhyā savarṇāhany ahany api |
iti manuvacanāt |
“pratyuttheyā nābhivādyāḥ” ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [6.10] |
tathānyaḥ pūrvaḥ pauro ‘śītikāvaraḥ śūdro ‘py apatyasamena |
“anyaḥ” anantaroktartvigādibhyaḥ | “pūrvaḥ’ iti vayo’pekṣayā | “pauraḥ” samānasthānavāsī | “aśītikāvaraḥ” aśītir evāśītikā sā avarā avadhitvena yasya sa tathety arthaḥ | “śūdro” yadi pitṛsakho bhavati sakhyuḥ pitā vā bhavati tadā “apatyasamena” brāhmaṇena pratyuttheya it arthaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.14.10–11] |
ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvyamātulān avaravayasaḥ pratyutthāyābhivādayet | tūṣṇīṃ vopasaṃgṛhṇīyāt |
“abhivādayet” abhibhāṣeta | “upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt” upa samīpe saṃgṛhṇīyāt aṅkapālyādinā | vidyādijyeṣṭhe upasaṃgrahaṇam, itarasminn abhibhāṣaṇamātram |
[194]        śātātapaḥ |
nābhivādyās tu vipreṇa kṣatriyādyāḥ kathaṃcana |
jñānakarmaguṇopetā yad apy ete bahuśrutāḥ ||
brāhmaṇāḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ svasti kuryād iti śrutiḥ |
savarṇais tu savarṇānāṃ kāryam evābhivādanam ||
aṅgirāḥ |
apraṇāmagate śūdre svasti kurvanti ye dvijāḥ |
śūdro ‘pi narakaṃ yāti brāhmaṇas tadanantaram ||
devīpurāṇe |
śūdro ‘pi cādhikārāḍhyo vādākṣarasuvarvitaḥ |
mahāpātakatulyaṃ tu labhate vinayād ṛte ||
gurvanuvṛttau manuḥ [2.202] |
dūrastho nārcayed enaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ |
yānāsanasthaś caivainam avaruhyābhivādayet ||
āpastambaḥ [1.14.12–19] |
trivarṣapūrvaḥ śrotriyo ‘bhivādanam arhati | jñāyamāne vayoviśeṣe vṛddhatarāyābhivādyam | viṣamagatāyāgurave nābhivādyam | anvāruhyābhivādayīta | sarvatra tu pratyutthāyābhivādanam | aprayatena nābhivādyam, tathā aprayatāyāprayataś ca na pratyabhivādayet | pativayasaḥ striyaś ca | na sopānadveṣṭitaśirāḥ avahitapāṇir vābhivādayīta |
[195] “trivarṣapūrvaḥ” tribhir varṣaiḥ pūrvo jyeṣṭha ity arthaḥ | “viṣamagatāya” uccanīcasthānagatāya | “anvāruhya” ity upalakṣaṇam | gurave viṣamagatāyāpy abhivādayet | “avahitapāṇiḥ” vastvantarāvasaktapāṇiḥ |
gautamaḥ [9.47] |
sopānatkaś cāśanāsanābhivādananamaskārān varjayet |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nodakumbhahasto ‘bhivādayet | na baikṣyaṃ caran | na puṣpādipāṇiḥ | nāśuciḥ | na japan | na devapitṛkāryaṃ kurvan | na pariṣanmadhye svairavāk |
[baudhāyanaḥ (1.3.32–33) |
samiddhāry udakumbhapuṣpānnahasto nābhivādayet] | yac cānyad apy evaṃ yuktam | na samavāye ||
“samiddhārī” samidho vahan | “evaṃ yuktaṃ” taṃ nābhivādayed ity arthaḥ | “samavāye” pariṣadi |
hārītaḥ |
japayajñajalasthaś ca samitpuṣpakuśānalān |
udakumbhaṃ ca bhaikṣyaṃ ca vahantaṃ nābhivādayet ||
“japayajñajalasthaś ca” nābhivādanakartā |
[196]        hārītaḥ |
nānyena kṛtapraṇāmo ‘nyam abhibhāśeta | abhibhāṣeta gurūn brāhmaṇāṃś ca | na prāg āhārād vṛṣalān |
“anyaṃ” praṇāmakartuḥ |
yamaḥ |
abhivāde tu yaḥ pūrvam āśiṣaṃ na prayacchati |
yad duṣkṛtaṃ bhaved asya tasmād bhāgaṃ prapadyate ||
tasmāt pūrvābhibhāṣī syāc cāṇḍālasyāpi dharmavit |
surāṃ pibeti vaktavyam evaṃ dharmo na lupyate ||
svastīti brāhmaṇe brūyād āyuṣyam iti rājani |
vardhatām iti vaiśye tu śūdre tv ārogyam eva ca ||
yat sukhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu vyādhivyasanavarjitam |
yasmin sarve sthitāḥ kāmāḥ sā svastīty abhisaṃjñitā ||
“abhivāde tu yaḥ pūrvam āśiṣaṃ na prayacchati” ity āśiṣaḥ prastutatvāt sā āśīḥ “svastīty abhisaṃjñitā” iti saṃbandhaḥ |
manu-yamau |
brāḥmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchet kṣatrabandhum anāmayam |
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdram ārogyam eva tu ||
ātithyādhikāre gautamaḥ [5.42] |
kuśalānāmayārogyāṇām anupraśno ‘ntyaṃ śūdrasya |
[197]        āpastambaḥ [1.14.23–28] |
kuśalam avaravayasaṃ vayasyaṃ vā pṛcchet | anāmayaṃ kṣatriyam | anaṣṭaṃ vaiśyam | ārogyaṃ śūdram | nāsaṃbhāṣya śrotriyam abhivrajet | araṇye ca striyam |
“śrotriyo” vedādhyāyī tathānuṣthātā ca | “araṇye striyam” bhayādinivṛtyartham |
yamaḥ |
kṣatriyas triyugaṃ hanti [vaiśyo hanti caturyugam |
hanty ekasaptatiyugaṃ śūdro brāhmaṇaṃ abruvan ||
“yugaṃ” janma | “abruvan” avajñayā asaṃbhāṣamāṇaḥ | ]
iti mahārājādhirājaśrīmadgovindacandradevamahāsaṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīhṛdayadharātmajaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
brahmacārikāṇḍe abhivādanopasaṃgrahaṇe
[198]
|| 10 ||
atha mānyāḥ
tatra śaṅkhaḥ |
atha mānyāḥ | mātā pitā pitur bhāryā jyeṣṭho bhrātā jyeṣṭhabhaginī jyeṣṭhaśyālaḥ pituḥ sakhā guru ācāryaḥ pitṛvyamātulaśvaśrūśvaśuravṛddhāḥ |
hārītaḥ |
guruśuśrūṣā nāma jñānadharmārthanimittaṃ kriyate | tatra jñānaniṣkrayārthaṃ guruśuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt | diviṣṭhā bhūmiṣṭhāś ca khalu guravaḥ | agnyādayo devā diviṣṭhāḥ, teṣāṃ japahomārcanādibhiḥ śuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt | bhūmiṣṭhāḥ khalu guravo, yathā brāhmaṇo mātāpitarau jyeṣṭho bhrātā, jyeṣṭhasya patnī upādhyāya upādhyāyapatnī dīkṣitaḥ pravrajito rājā vayovṛddhāḥ jñānavṛddhās tapovṛddhāś ceti | teṣām abhigamanābhivādanārghātithyādibhiḥ śuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt |
evaṃ hy āha —
mūlaṃ dharmasya guravo jñānasya tapasas tathā |
tasmād dhi guravaḥ sevyāḥ puruṣeṇa prayatnataḥ ||
[199]                bhūmiṣṭhā guravo yeṣāṃ tuṣyanti satataṃ nṛṇām |
devā hi teṣāṃ tuṣyanti narāṇāṃ puṇyakarmaṇām ||
atuṣṭā [guravo yeṣāṃ praṇaṣṭās te narādhamāḥ |
tuṣṭās tu] yeṣāṃ guravas teṣāṃ sarvāḥ samṛddhayaḥ ||
gurumanyupradagdhānāṃ na śubhā vidyate gatiḥ |
“dīkṣitaḥ” somakrayād anantaram | tatpūrvaṃ tu “dīkṣitaś ca prakkrayāt” iti vayasyatvasya gautamenābhidhānāt | “rājā” atra mahīpatiḥ devalavacanaikavāyatvāt, na kṣatriyamātram |
devalaḥ |
upādhyāyaḥ pitā jyeṣṭho bhrātā caiva mahīpatiḥ |
mātulaḥ śvaśuras trātā mātāmahapitāmahau ||
varṇajyeṣṭhaḥ pitṛvyaś ca puṃsy ete guravaḥ smṛtāḥ |
mātā mātāmahī gurvī pitur mātuś ca sodarāḥ ||
śvaśrūḥ pitāmahī jyeṣṭhā dhātrī ca guravaḥ striyaḥ (?) |
“gurvī” gurupatnī | “jyeṣṭhā” bhaginīti śeṣaḥ |
tathā |
ity ukto guruvargo ‘yaṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛto dvidhā |
anuvartanam eteṣāṃ manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ ||
guruṃ dṛṣṭvā samuttiṣṭhed abhivādya kṛtāñjaliḥ |
naitair upaviśet sardhaṃ vivaden nārthakāraṇāt ||
uddiṣṭaṃ sādhayed arthaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ ca varjayet |
[200]        tatpūjyān pūjayen nityaṃ vyasane na parityajet ||
“vyasane” vipadi |
krodhaṃ vāgdaṇḍapāruṣye utsekaṃ vaikṛtān svarān |
narma hāsaṃ vilāsaṃ ca varjayed gurusaṃnidhau ||
“utseko” garvaḥ | “narma” krīḍā | “hasaḥ” parihāsaḥ | “vilāsaḥ” śṛṅgāraceṣṭā |
tathā |
jīvitārtham api dveṣaṃ gurubhir naiva bhāvayet |
udito ‘pi guṇair anyair gurudveṣī pataty adhaḥ ||
gurūṇām api sarveṣāṃ pūjyāḥ pañca viśeṣataḥ |
teṣām adyās trayaḥ proktās teṣāṃ mātā supūjitā ||
yo bhāvayati yā sūte yena vidyopadiśyate |
jyeṣṭho bhrātā ca bhartā ca pañcaite guravaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
“bhartā” poṣakaḥ |
ātmanaḥ sarvayatnena prāṇatyāgena vā punaḥ |
pūjayitavyā viśeṣeṇa pañcaite bhūtim icchatā ||
yāvat pitā ca mātā ca dvāv etau nirvikāriṇau |
tāavat sarvaṃ parityajya putraḥ syāt tatparāyaṇaḥ ||
pitā mātā ca suprītau syātāṃ putraguṇair yadi |
sa putraḥ sakalaṃ dharmaṃ prāpnuyāt tena karmaṇā ||
nāsti mātṛsamaṃ daivaṃ nāsti pitrā samo guruḥ |
tayoḥ pratyupakāro hi na kathaṃcana vidyate ||
[201]        samyag ārādhya vaktāraṃ viśrṣṭas tadanujñayā |
śiṣyo vidyāphalaṃ bhuṅkte pretya cāpadyate divam ||
“visṛṣṭaḥ” prasthāpitaḥ |
yo bhrātaraṃ pitṛsamaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ mūḍho ‘vamanyate |
tena doṣeṇa saṃpretya nirayaṃ ghoram ṛcchati ||
tasmāt pitṛvad ārādhyo jyeṣṭho bhrātā ca sarvadā |
api mātari loke ‘sminn upakārād dhi gauravam ||
ye narā bhartṛpiṇḍārthaṃ svān prāṇān samtyajanti hi |
teṣām athākṣayān lokān provāca bhagavān manuḥ ||
yaṃ mātāpitarau kleśaṃ sahete saṃbhave nṛṇām |
na tasya niṣkṛtiṃ śakyā kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
yo ‘ntar nityaṃ priyaṃ kuryād ācāryasya viśeṣataḥ |
teṣu hi triṣu tuṣṭeṣu tapaḥ sarvaṃ samāpyate ||
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ apa ucyate |
naitaair anabhyanujñāto dharmam anyaṃ samācaret ||
tathā |
sarve tasyādṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ |
anādṛtās tu yasyaite sarvās tasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ ||
yāvat trayas te jīveran tāvan nānyat samācaret |
teṣāṃ nityaṃ tu śuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt priyahite rataḥ ||
[202]        teṣām anuparodhena pārakyaṃ yad yad ācaret |
tat tan nivedayet tebhyo maanovākkāyakarmabhiḥ ||
triṣv eteṣv itikṛtyaṃ hi puruṣasya samāpyate |
eṣa dharmaḥ paraḥ sākṣād upadharmo ‘nya ucyate ||
idaṃ lokaṃ mātṛbhaktyā pitṛbhaktyā tu madhyamam |
guruśuśrūṣayā tv eva brahmalokaṃ samaśnute ||
viṣṇuḥ [32.1–3] |
rājartvik śrotriyo ‘dharmapratiṣedhy upādhyāyapitṛvyamātāmahamātulaśvaśura-jyeṣṭhabhrātṛsaṃbandhinas tv ācāryavat pūjanīyāḥ | patnyaś ca teṣāṃ savarṇāḥ | mātṛṣvasā jyeṣṭhā svasā ca |
“adharmapratiṣedhī” adharmādyo nivārayati |
śātātapaḥ |
abhivādyo namaskāryaḥ pādayor vandya eva ca |
gurur agnir dvijātīnāṃ varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇo guruḥ ||
patir eko guruḥ strīṇāṃ sarvasyābhyāgato guruḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
brāhmaṇaṃ pañcavarṣaṃ tu daśavarṣaṃ tu bhūmipam |
pitāputrau vijānīyād brāhmaṇas tu tayoḥ pitā ||
ity evaṃ kṣatriyapitā vaiśyasyāpi pitāmahaḥ |
prapitāmahaś ca śūdrasya vipraḥ prokto manīṣibhiḥ ||
manur viṣṇuś ca |
vittaṃ bandhur vayaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī |
[203]        etāni mānyasthānāni garīyo yad yad uttaram ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [13.56–57] |
vidyā vittaṃ vayaḥ saṃbandhāḥ karma ca mānyam | teṣāṃ samavāye pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ garīyān |
gautamaḥ [6.20–24] |
vittabandhukarmajātividyāvayāṃsi mānyāni parabalīyāṃsi | śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ | tamūlatvād dharmasya | śruteś ca |
“jātiḥ” ābhijātyam, ādhikyāpādikā brāhmaṇātvādir vā |
atrāyaṃ nirṇayḥ — manunā vittaṃ sarvajaghanyatvenoktam, vasiṣṭhena tu vidyāmātrāt | tatra ca vasiṣṭhoktaṃ vittotkṛṣṭatvaṃ nyāyārjitadharmopayogivittābhiprāyam | evaṃ manu-gautama-vasiṣṭheṣu karmaṇa utkṛṣṭatvamadhyamatvajaghanyatvāni śraddhādikāraṇotkṛṣṭatvādi-traividhyābhiprāyeṇa bhūyastvābhiprāyeṇa vā boddhavyam | gautamena tu vidyāyā vayojaghanyatvenābhidhānaṃ pāṭhamātrābhiprāyam | tenaiva “śrutaṃ sarvebhyo ‘pi garīyaḥ” ity uktatvāt | “vayo” atra daśamyavasthā | manu-vasiṣṭhayoś ca vayomātram |
manuḥ [2.137] |
pañcānāṃ triṣu varṇāṣu bhūyāṃsi puṇavanti ca |
[204]        yatra syuḥ so ‘tra mānārho śūdro ‘pi daśamīṃ gataḥ ||
“pañcānāṃ” pūrvoktavittādīnām | “triṣu varṇeṣu” brāhmaṇatvādiṣu | “guṇavanti” prakarṣavanti | “daśamī” navatyadhikā vayo’vasthā |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [13.58–60] |
sthavirabālāturabhārikastrīcakravatāṃ panthāḥ samāgame parasmai parasmai pradeyaḥ | | [rājasnātakayoḥ samāgame rājñā snātakāya deyaḥ |] sarvair eva vadhvām uhyamānāyām |
“uhyamānāyāṃ” vivāhakāle |
“panthā deya” ity anuvṛttau mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.40–41] |
mūkāndhabadhirāṇāṃ ca mattasyonmattakasya ca |
puṃścalyāḥ kṛtavairasya bālasya patitasya ca ||
“puṃścalī” vyabhicāriṇī |
“panthā deya” ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
bālavṛddhamattonmattopahatadehabhārākrāntebhyaḥ striyai garbhiṇyai snātakapravrajitavidyāśilpaguṇaprakṛṣṭebhyaḥ |
[205] “pupahatadehaḥ” pāparogādinā | “guṇo” atra vidyādi |
devala-bodhāyanau [BauDh 2.6.30] |
panthā deyo brāhmaṇāya gave rājñe ‘pacakṣuṣe |
vṛddhāya bhārataptāya garbhiṇyai durabalāya ca ||
āpastambaḥ [2.11.5–9] |
rājñaḥ panthāḥ brāhmaṇenāsametya | sametya tu brāhmaṇasyaiva panthāḥ | yānasya bhārābhinihitasyāturasya striyā iti sarvair dātavyaḥ | varṇajyāyase cetarair varṇaiḥ | aśiṣṭapatitamattonmattānām ātmanaḥ svastyayanārthena sarvair eva dātavyaḥ |
gautamaḥ [6.25–26] |
cakridaśamīsthānugrāhyavadhūsnātakarājabhyaḥ patho dānam |
rājñā tu śrotriyāya |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.117] |
vṛddhabhārinṛpasnātastrīrogivaracakriṇām |
panthā deyo nṛpas teṣāṃ mānyaḥ snātaś ca bhūpateḥ |
iti mānyāḥ
[206]
|| 11 ||
athācāryasaṃjñāmānanakramau
tatra manuḥ [2.140] |
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyāpayed dvijaḥ |
sakalaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate ||
“kalpaḥ” pratyakṣavedamūlako ‘nuṣṭhānapratipādako granthaḥ | kalpagrahaṇam aṅgopalakṣaṇārtham | “rahasyaṃ” upaniṣat | pradhānatvāt pṛthagabhidhānam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [3.21] |
upanīya tu yaḥ kṛtsnaṃ vedam adhyāpayet sa ācāryaḥ |
kṛtsnaśabdo ‘tra sāṅgatvaparaḥ | mantrabrāhmaṇasamūhasya vedapadena lakṣyatvāt |
manuḥ [2.141–142] |
ekadeśaṃ tu vedasya vedāṅgāny atha vā punaḥ |
yo ‘dhyāpayati vṛttyartham upādhyāyaḥ sa ucyate ||
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi |
saṃbhāvayati cānnena sa vipro guru ucyate ||
“vṛttyarthaṃ” ātmajīvanārtham “ | “niṣekādīni” vedadānaparyantāni saṃskārakarmāṇi | “saṃbhāvayati” saṃvardhayati |
[207]         tathā [2.149] |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ |
tam apīha guruṃ vidyāc chrutopakriyayā tathā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.34] |
sa gurur yaḥ kriyāḥ kṛtvā vedam asmai prayacchati |
upanīya dadad vedam ācāryaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
satyavāg dhṛtimān dakṣaḥ sarvabhūtadayāparaḥ |
āstiko vedanirataḥ śucir ācārya ucyate ||
vedādhyayanasaṃpanno vṛttavān vinitendriyaḥ |
ity etair lakṣaṇair yukto guru bhavati nityaśaḥ ||
“ācārya ucyate” ācāryavat pūjyo bhavati | “gurur bhavati” guruvat pūjyo bhavatīty arthaḥ |
bhaviṣye |
upādhyāyān daśācārya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā |
sahasraṃ tu pitur mātā gauraveṇātiricyate ||
kṛtvopādhyāyam ādau tu ye pūjyāḥ kathitās tava |
mahāguru mahābāho sarveṣām adhikaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
“sarveṣām adhikaḥ” sarvebhyaḥ pūjyatamaḥ | sa ca “mahāguruḥ” “aṣṭādaśa purāṇāni” ityādinā vakṣyamāṇaślokair ūhitaḥ | sarvajñatvena sahasraśatasaṃkhyākācāryāṇām idaṃ matam |
[208]        aṣṭādaśa purāṇāni rāmasya caritaṃ tathā |
viṣṇudharmādiśāstrāṇi śivadharmāś ca bhārata ||
kārṣṇaṃ ca pañcamaṃ jñeyaṃ yan mahābhārataṃ smṛtam |
saurāś ca dharmā rājendra mānavoktā mahīpate ||
jayeti nāma eteṣāṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
evaṃ viprakadambasya vācakaḥ pravaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ sa mahāgurur ucyate |
yas tv etāni samastāni purāṇānīha jānate ||
bhārataṃ ca mahābāho sarvajño ‘sau mato nṛṇām |
tasmāt sa pūjyo rājendra varṇair viprādibhiḥ sadā ||
kiṃ tvayā na śrutaṃ vākyaṃ yathāha bhagavan vibhuḥ |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ ||
tam apīha guruṃ vidyāc chrutopakriyayā tayā |
manuḥ [2.143] |
agnyādheyaṃ pākayajñān agniṣṭomādikān makhān |
yaḥ karoti vṛto yasya sa tasyartvig ihocyate ||
hārītaḥ |
kṣīrahotā āhāryavṛto viśeṣavṛta iti |
śrotriyo ‘syāgnīn ādhatte sa “kṣīrahotā” | tasyālābhe ‘nyaṃ nityārthaṃ vṛṇīte sa “āhāryavṛtaḥ” | atha yān anyān uttarottareṣu kratuṣu vṛṇīte sa “viśeṣavṛtaḥ” |
manuḥ [2.145] |
upādhyāyān daśācārya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā |
[209]        sahasraṃ tu pitur mātā gauraveṇātiricyate ||
gautamaḥ [2.57–58] |
ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām | mātety eke |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na mātāpitror antaraṃ gacchet putraḥ | kāmaṃ mātur evānubrūyāt | sā hi dhāraṇī poṣaṇī ca | na putraḥ pratimucyetānyatra sautrāmaṇīyāgāc jīvann ṛṇān mātuḥ |
“mātāpitroḥ” vivāde sati “na antaraṃ gacchet” audāsīnyaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | “kāmam” yadīcchet, “anubrūyāt” mātur evānukūlaṃ vadet |
mātāpitradhikāre nāradaḥ |
tayor api pitā śreyān bījaprādhānyadarśanāt |
abhāve bījino mātā tadabhāve tu pūrvajaḥ ||
manu-viṣṇū [Manu 2.146–48, 150; Viṣṇu 30.44–46] |
utpādakabrahmadātror garīyān brahmadaḥ pitā |
brahmajanma hi viprasya pretya ceha ca śāśvatam ||
kāmān mātā pitā caiva yad utpādayato mithaḥ |
saṃbhūtiṃ tasya tāṃ vidyād yad yonāv abhijāyate ||
[210]        ācāryas tasya yāṃ jātiṃ vidhivad vedapāragaḥ |
utpādayati sāvitryā sā satyā sājarāmarā ||
brāhmasya janmanaḥ kartā svadharmasya ca śāsitā |
bālo ‘pi vipro vṛddhasya pitā bhavati dharmataḥ ||
yamaḥ |
na janmanā na palitair dvijaiḥ sthavira ucyate |
yo vai yuvāpy adhīyānas taṃ devā sthaviraṃ viduḥ ||
na bandhubhir na vittena palitair naiva hāyanaiḥ |
ṛṣayaś cakrire dharmaṃ yo ‘nūcānaḥ sa no mahān ||
ajño hi bālo bhavati pitā bhavati mantradaḥ |
ajñaṃ hi putram ity ūcur pitaraṃ mantradāyinam ||
[manuḥ [1.153]
ajño bhavati vai bālaḥ pitā bhavati mantradaḥ |
ajñaṃ hi putram ity ūcuḥ pitaraṃ mantradāyinam ||
manu-viṣṇū [Manu 2.155; Viṣṇu 32.18] |
viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vīryataḥ |
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇām eva janmataḥ ||
[211]
|| 12 ||
atha gurvācāryānuvṛttiḥ
tatra āpastambaḥ [1.5.23–26; 1.6.1–37; 1.7.1–2] |
sarvāhṇaṃ suyukto ‘dhyayanād anantaro ‘dhyāye | tathā gurukarmasu | manasā cānadhyāye | āhūtādhyāyī ca syāt| sadā niśāyāṃ guruṃ saṃveśayet tasya pādau prakṣālya saṃvāhya | anujñātaḥ saṃviśet | an cainam abhiprasārayīta | na khṭvāyāṃ sato ‘bhiprasāraṇam astīty eke | na cāsya sakāśe saṃviṣṭo ‘bhibhāṣeta | abhibhāṣitas tv āsīnaḥ pratibrūyāt | anūtthāya tiṣṭhantam | gacchantam anugacchet | dhāvantam anudhāvet | na sopānadveṣṭitaśirā avahitapāṇir vā sīdet | devam ivācāryam upāsītāvikathayann avimanā vācaṃ śuśrūṣamāṇo ‘sya | anupaskṛtaḥ | anuvāti vītaḥ | apratiṣṭhabdhaḥ pāṇinā | anapāśrito ‘nyatra | yajñopavītī dvivastraḥ | adhonīvītas tv ekavastraḥ | abhimukho ‘nabhimukham | anāsanno ‘natidūre ca | yāvad āsīno bāhubhyāṃ pāpnuyāt | aprativātam | ekādhyāyī dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ pratyupasīdet | yathāvakāśaṃ bahavaḥ | tiṣthati nāsītānāsanayogavihite | āsīne ca na saṃviśet | [212] ceṣṭati ca cikīrṣaṃs tacchaktiviṣaye | na cāsya sakāśe ‘nvaksthāninam upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt | gotreṇa vā kīrtayet | na cainaṃ pratyupatiṣṭhed anūttiṣṭhed vāpi  cet tasya guruḥ syāt | deśāt tv āsanāc ca saṃsarpet | nāmnā tadantevāsinaṃ gurum apy ātmana ity eke | yasmiṃs tv anācāryasaṃbandhād gauravaṃ vṛttis tasminn anvaksthānīye ‘py ācāryasya | bhuktvā cāsya sakāśe nānūtthāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet | ācāryāya vā paryavadadhyāt | ācāmed vā | kiṃ karavāṇīty āmantrya | uttiṣṭhet tūṣṇīṃ vā | nāpaparyāvarteta guroḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyāpeyat |
ayam arthaḥ | “anantaraḥ” avyavahitaḥ vedādhyayanāt | “adhyāye” adhyayanārhe dine | “tathā” iti gurukarmasv abhiyukto ‘nantaraś ca syād ity atidiśyate | “manasā cānadhyāye” iti, anadhyāyetara ity anuṣajyate | anena mānasamabhyāsaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | “āhūtādhyāyī syāt” ācāryeṇādhyayanārtham [213] āhūta evādhīyīta | etasya karma karomīti buddhyādhyayanārthaṃ prerayed ity arthaḥ | “saṃveśayet” śāyayet | “saṃvāhya” saṃmṛdya | “enaṃ” gurum | “abhiprasārayīta” pādāv ity anuṣajyate | tena “khañvyāṃ” iti anantaroktapādaprasāraṇavarṇana-niṣedhaḥ | ataḥ “khaṭvāyāṃ” pratibrūyād ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “anavahitapāṇiḥ” daṇḍādyuparuddha-pāṇiḥ | “adhvāpannas tu” iti, atra sopānatkādir apy āsīded ity arthaḥ | “na ced upāsīdet” yadi tadīyājñāgrahaṇavijñāpanāya pravṛtto na bhavet | “anupasthakṛtaḥ” itaracaraṇavyatyāsenānupa-viṣṭaḥ | “anuvāti” vāte iti śeṣaḥ | “vītaḥ” vigatayaccihno, vāti vāyau guros tasyāṃ diśi na tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ | “apratiṣṭhabdhaḥ” pāṇinā bhūmyādāv akṛtālambaḥ | “anapāśrito ‘nyatra” anyatra kuḍyādau, anāśritapṛṣṭhapārśvaḥ | “yajñopavītī dvivastraḥ” san yajñopavītadupari vastaṃ dadyāt | “adhonivītaḥ” adho vastrasyaikadeśeṇānāvṛttaḥ | “yāvad āsīno bāhubhyāṃ prāpnuyāt” tāvad āsīded ity anvayaḥ | “ceṣṭati” ceṣṭāṃ kurvati ācārye, “tacchaktiviṣaye” tasmin śaktiviṣaye śakye, “cikīrṣet” | “anvaksthāninaṃ” ācāryānyūnam | “gotreṇa” na nāmnā | “enaṃ” anvaksthāninam | “api cet tasya guruḥ syāt” tasya brahmacāriṇaḥ | “nāmnā tadantevāsinam” ity atra “na kīrtayet” ity anvayaḥ | “nāmnā” pūjyanāmnā | “yasmiṃs tv anācāryasaṃbandhād gauravaṃ vṛttiḥ” iti, yasmin dvijottame, “nācārye” ācāryasaṃbandhād vinā, vidyopādānād bahuśrutatvād [214] vayo’dhikatvāt svakarmānuṣṭhānāt “gauravaṃ” prasiddhaṃ loke, “tasminn anvaksthānīye ‘py ācāryasya” “vṛttiḥ” śuśrūṣā yathācārye tathā tasmin kartavyā ity arthaḥ | “bhuktvā cāsya” iti, ācāryasakāśe bhuktvā “nānūtthāyocchiṣṭaṃ prayacchet” | “ācāryāya vā paryavadadhyāt” iti, brahmacāriṇācāryāya bhuktaśiṣṭaṃ samarpaṇavihitaṃ tan nānūtthitena kartavyam | “nāpaparyāvarteta” kāryavattayā gantum icchan pṛṣṭhaṃ darśayan na gacchet | kiṃ tu “pradakṣiṇīkṛtya” “apeyāt” |
gautamaḥ [2.29–39] |
nāmagotre guroḥ samānato nirdiśet | arcite śreyasi caivam | śayyāsanasthānāni vihāya pratiśravaṇam | abhikramaṇaṃ vacanād adṛṣṭena | adhaḥsthānāsana-tiryagvātasevāyāṃ gurudarśane cottiṣṭhet | gacchantam anuvrajet | karma vijñāpyākhyāya | āhūtādhyāyī | yuktaḥ priyahitayoḥ |
“samānataḥ” samyag ānataḥ | “śreyān” vidyādibhir utkṛṣṭaḥ | sa yadi loke arcito bhavati tadā gurau yad yad uktam abhivādanādi tat sarvaṃ śabdātidiṣtaṃ kartavyam | “sthānaṃ” avasthānam, tad “vihāya” kiṃcid upasṛtyety arthaḥ | “pratiśravaṇaṃ” atra guror ājñāṅgīkaraṇam | “abhikramaṇaṃ vacanād adṛṣṭena” “adṛṣṭena” guruṇā “vacanāt” ājñādānāt “abhikramaṇaṃ” ābhimukhyena gamanam | kāryam iti śeṣaḥ | “tiryagvātasevā” mūtrapurīṣotsarga iti kapardī | “uttiṣṭhet” tasmāt sthānāt sthānāntaraṃ gacchet | “karma vijñāpyākhyāya” anena śiṣyeṇācāryasya karma kartavyam, tat karomīti [215] vijñāpya, kṛtvā cāpy ākhyāya tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ | “yuktaḥ” abhiyuktaḥ, syād iti śeṣaḥ | “priyaṃ” āpātapriyakaraṇam | “hitaṃ” pariṇatisukhakaraṇam |
manuḥ [2.191–203] |
codito guruṇā nityam apracodita eva vā |
kuryād adhyayane yogam ācāryasya hiteṣu ca ||
śarīraṃ caiva vācaṃ ca buddhīndriyamanāṃsi ca |
niyamya prāñjalis tiṣṭhed vīkṣyamāṇo guror mukham ||
nityam udyatapāṇiḥ syāt śadhvācāraḥ susaṃyataḥ |
āsyatām iti coktaḥ sann āsītābhimukho guroḥ ||
pratiśravaṇasaṃbhāṣe śayāno na samācaret |
nāsīno na ca bhuñjāno na tiṣṭhan na parāṅmukhaḥ ||
āsīnasya sthitaḥ kuryād abhigacchaṃs tu tiṣṭhataḥ |
pratyudgamya tv āvrajataḥ paścād dhāvaṃs tu dhāvataḥ ||
parāṅmukhasyābhimukho dūrasthasyetya cāntikam |
praṇamya to śayānasya nideśe caiva tiṣṭhataḥ ||
nīcaṃ śayyāsanaṃ cāśya sarvathā gurusaṃnidhau |
guros tu cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭāsano bhavet ||
nodāhared asya nāma parokṣam api kevalam |
na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣaṇaceṣṭitam ||
guror yatra parīvādo nindā vābhipravartate |
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato ‘nyataḥ ||
[216]        parīvādāt kahro bhavati śvā vai bhavati nindakaḥ |
sadoṣakīrtanaṃ “parīvādaḥ” | itarat “nindā” | “parīvādāt” parīvādaśrāvaṇāt, tasya niṣiddhatvena nindāviṣayatvāt | pūrvoktaniṣedhaś cāyam arthavāda iti medhātithiḥ |
paribhoktā kṛmir bhavati kīṭo bhavati matsarī ||
dūrastho nārcayed enaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ |
yānāsanasthaś caivainam avaruhyābhivādayet ||
prativāte ‘nuvāte ca nāsīta guruṇā saha |
asaṃśrave caiva guror na kiṃcid api kīrtayet ||
tathā [2.218] |
yathā khanan khanitreṇa naro vāry adhigacchati |
tathā gurugatāṃ vidyāṃ śuśrūṣur adhigacchati ||
“asaṃśrave” yasmin deśē ucyamānaṃ vyaktaṃ na śrūyate |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
āvṛṇvṛtti gurau rājan na kiṃcid api kīrtayet |
“āvṛṇvṛtti” rahasyaṃ pracchādayati |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
upaśikṣitāni cāsya na kurvītānyatrācārāt | na vāśiṣṭakathābhir abhivadet |
“upaśikṣitāni” guroḥ sakāśāt, guruṇā akathitāny api svayam eva jñātāni tadīyagatibhāṣita-ceṣṭitāni [217] asya guroḥ na kurvīta | “na vāśiṣṭakathābhir abhivadet” na gurubhir anupadiṣṭānām kathābhiḥ stotābhimukhye vadet |
hārītaḥ |
ūrdhvaretāḥ guruṇādiṣṭāni vratāni samudvahan guruśuśrūṣāṃ kuryād iti | athodakumbhakuśapuṣpasaminmūlaphalāharaṇaṃ saṃmārjanopalepanāṅga-śuśrūṣāprabhṛtibhir gacchantaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ śayānaṃ bhaktyānuvarteta | sarvadā sarvakārī | vratī | nāsya nirmālyaśayanāsane chāyāpāduke vākrāmet |
“ūrdhvaretāḥ” varjitaretaḥskandanaḥ | “aṅgaśuśrūṣā” aṅgasaṃvāhanakriyā | “sarvakārī” sarvocitakārī |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
dantakāṣṭhaśayanāsanāstaraṇasaṃvāhanapriyavacanādibhiḥ guruṃ paritoṣayet |
āpastambaḥ [1.2.19–20] |
ācāryādhīnaḥ syād anyatra patanīyebhyaḥ | hitakārī guror apratilomayan vācā |
“apratilomayan cāvā” iti, gurupratikūlāṃ vācam avadan |
śaṅkhaḥ |
na snānena na homena naivāgniparicaryayā |
brahmacārī divaṃ yāti sa yāti gurupūjanāt ||
hitapriye guroḥ kuryād bhaktyāhaṃkāravarjitaḥ |
[218]        yamaḥ |
anāpṛcchya guruṃ caiva na gantavyaṃ kadācana |
yat tu śiṣyeṇa kartavyaṃ kāryaṃ dāsena vā punaḥ ||
ācārye tu vasaṃś chiṣyo gurukāryeṣu tatparaḥ |
darbhendhanaparo nityam agnikāryāparaḥ śūciḥ ||
kṛtāñjalir guruṃ brūyād adhyeṣye bhagavann aham |
“dāsena vā” iti, dāsakarma api śiṣyeṇa kartavyam |
“varjayet” ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [2.20–21] |
gurudarśane kaṇṭhaprāvṛtāvasakthikāpāśrayaṇapādaprasāraṇāni | niṣṭhīvitahasitavijṛmbhitāvasphoṭanāni |
“kaṇṭhaprāvṛtaṃ” kaṇṭhāvaraṇam | “avasakthikā” jānunor upari karabandhaḥ | “avasphoṭanaṃ” aṅgulyādyaṅgasphoṭanam |
viṣṇuḥ [32.10–12] |
na ca guruṇā saha vigṛhyakathāḥ kuryāt | na caivāsya parīvādam | na caivānabhipretam |
“vigṛhyakathā” vijigīṣukathā |
nāradaḥ [p. 142, verse 8] |
ā vidyāgrahaṇāc chiṣyaḥ śuśrūṣāprayato bhavet |
[219]        āpastambaḥ [1.4.22–28] |
svapnaṃ ca varjayet | athāhar ahar ācāryaṃ gopāyet dharmārthayuktaiḥ karmabhiḥ | sa guptvā saṃviśan brūyāt “dharma gopāya mā jugupam aham” iti | pramādād ācāryasya buddhipūrvaṃ vā niyamātikramaṃ rahasi bodhayet | anivṛttau svayaṃ karmāṇy ārabhet | nivartayed vā | atha yaḥ pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī tam āhur na svapitīti | sa ya evaṃ praṇihitātmā brahmacāry atraivāsya sarvāṇi karmāṇi phalavanty avāptāni bhavanti | yāny api gṛhamedhe |
“svapnaṃ ca varjayet” iti | “gopāyet” pūrvaṃ parataś ceti śeṣaḥ, “atha yaḥ pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī tam āhur na svapitīti” ity agre vidhānāt | “dharmārthayuktaiḥ” dharmopayogibhiḥ samitpuṣpakuśādyāharaṇaprabhṛtibhiḥ arthopayogibhiḥ grāsāharaṇādibhiḥ | dharmagopāya mā jugupsam aham iti māṅi luṅ | tato ‘yam arthaḥ | evam ācāryam ahaṃ rakṣāmy atha māṃ dharmarakṣeti | “niyamātikramaḥ” atithipūjādyakaraṇādi |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
dhṛtimān alābho ślādhām anyatra gurave nivedayet |
“idaṃ kṛtam idaṃ kariṣya” iti nivedayet |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.3.21–22] |
satyavādī hrīmān anahaṃkāraḥ | pūrvotthāyī jaghanyasaṃveśī | sarvatrāpratihataguruvākyo ‘nyatra pātakāt |
[220]        manuḥ [2.194] |
hīnānnavastraveṣaḥ syāt sarvadā gurusaṃnidhau |
āpastambaḥ [1.7.31; 1.8.1–22] |
samāvṛttasyāpy etad eva sāmayācārikam eteṣu | yathā brahmacāriṇo vṛttam | mālyāliptamukha upaliptakeśaśmaśrur akto ‘bhyakto veṣṭity upaveṣṭitī kāñcuky upānahī pādukī | udācāreṣu cāsyaitāni na kuryāt kārayed vā | svairikarmasu ca | yathā dantaprakṣālanotsādanāvalekhanāni | taddravyāṇāṃ ca na kathayed ātmasaṃyogenācāryaḥ | snātakas tu kāle yathāvidhy abhihṛtam āhūto ‘bhyeto vā na pratisaṃhared ity eke | uccaistarāṃ nāsīta | tathā bahupāde | sarvataḥ pratiṣṭhite | śayyāsane ‘dhyācarite nāviśet | yānayukto ‘dhvany anvārohet | sabhānikaṣakaṭasvastarāṃś ca | nānabhibhāṣito gurum abhibhāṣeta priyād anyat | vyupatodavyupajāpavyabhihāsodāmantraṇanāmadheyagrahaṇapreṣaṇānīti guror varjayet | āpady arthaṃ jñāpayet | saha vasan sāyaṃ prātar anāhūto guruṃ darśanārtho gacchet | viproṣya ca tad ahar eva paśyet | ācāryaprācāryasaṃnipāte prācāryāyopasaṃgṛhyopasaṃjighṛkṣed ācāryam | pratiṣedhed itaraḥ | lupyate pūjā cāsya sakāśe | muhūṃś cācāryakulaṃ darśanārtho gacched yathāśakty adhihastyam ādāyāpi dantaprakṣālanānīti |
ayam arthaḥ — “brahmacāriṇo samāvṛttasya yathā” “vṛttaṃ” vartanam anuṣṭhānam “etad eva samāvṛttasyāpi sāmayācārikam” | “samāvṛttasya” vivāhāt prāk sāmarthyāt saha vasataḥ | “mālī” mālyavān | “āliptamukhaḥ” samantāc candanād [221] āliptamukhaḥ | “upaliptakeśaśmaśruḥ” upaśabdo ‘tra samyagarthe kartanādinā samyak kṛte keśaśmaśruṇī yasya saḥ | “aktaḥ” kṛtanetrāñjanaḥ | “abhyaktaḥ” kṛtābhyaṅgaḥ | “veṣṭitiḥ” kṛtakaṭiveṣṭanaḥ | “kañcukī” kūrpāsakavān | “upānahī” “pādukī” iti ca prasiddham | “udācāreṣu” utkṛṣṭarūpācāreṣūpasaṃgrahaṇādiṣv asya gurvādeḥ kriyamāṇeṣu etāni mālitvādīni na kuryāt svam, anyena vā na kārayed ātmani | caśabdas tu śabdārthe vartamāno mālitvādīnām apavādam āha | “svairikarmasu ca” ātmaviṣayeṣu etāni mālitvādīni na kuryāt kārayed vā | svairikarmāṇy āha — “yathā dantaprakṣālanādīni” ityādinā | “avalekhanaṃ” kaṅkatakādinā keśaprasādhanam | atra prasaṅgenācāryasya dhamam āha — “tad dravyāṇāṃ” antevāsino vastrādidravyāṇām | “ātmasaṃyogena” ātmasaṃyogo ‘tre svasvābhibhāvasaṃbandhaḥ, tena hetunā | “na kathayet” kathāṃ na kuryāt | liptasya tadīyaguṇakathāṃ na kuryād ity arthaḥ | punaḥ samāvṛttadharmān āha “snātakas tu kāla” ityadinā | samāvartanakāle “yathāvidhy abhihṛtaṃ” samantrakaṃ yad gṛhītaṃ chatrasraganulepanakuṇḍalādi tadabhihṛtaḥ | “athāhūto” guruṇā svayaṃ vā guruṃ prati “abhyetaḥ” gataḥ “na pratisaṃharet” svaśarīrād iti “eke” ācāryā āhuḥ | “bahupāde” asaṃghādau | [222] “adhyācarite” upabhukte | “yānayukto adhvany anvārohet” “yānam āroha” iti guruṇokto adhvani paścād ārohet | “sabhānikaṣe” guruṇā ārohed ity anvayaḥ | “sabhā” janamelakasthānam | “kaṭaḥ” kāṣṭhādighaṭitaṃ niṣkṛṣṭamṛttikākṣetrasamīkaraṇakāṣṭham | “vyupetodo” vividham aṅgulyādinā todanam | “vyupajāpo” vividhaḥ samīpe karṇejāpaḥ | “vyabhihāsaḥ” guror ābhimukhyena parihāsaḥ | “udāmantraṇaṃ” uccair adūre ‘badhire gurāv āhvānam | “nāmakaraṇaṃ” pitrādikṛtasya nāmno grahaṇaṃ niṣiddham, nānyasya pūjyanāmnaḥ | śiṣṭācāre tathā darśanāt | “āpadyarthaṃ jñāpayet” “āpadi” pīḍāyām | “arthaṃ” prayojanam | prayatasya prakārāntareṇa jñāpayed ācāryam | “tad ahar eva” iti, ahargrahaṇam upalakṣaṇārtham | rātrāv api tadānīm eva gacchet | “upasaṃjighṛkṣet” upasaṃgrahaṇecchāṃ prakaṭīkuryāt pāṇiprasāraṇādinā | “itaraḥ” ācāryaḥ | “asya” ācāryasya | “darśanārtho gacchet” iti, sāmarthyāt samāvṛttaviṣayam eva | “adhihastyaṃ” adhihaste bhavamūlaphalādīni svayam eva haste gṛhītvā gacched ity arthaḥ |
gurvanuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
āsanaśayaneṣu naikas tiṣṭhed anyatra yajñasaṃstaraśibikāvāhana-śakaṭopayānābhyudayebhyaḥ |
“upayānaṃ” śava evānugataḥ | “abhyudayaḥ” vivāhādāv upakartādhvari tiṣṭhan |
[223]        manuḥ [2.204] |
go’śvoṣṭrayānaprāsādasaṃstareṣu kaṭeṣu ca |
āsīta guruṇā sārdhaṃ śilāphalakanauṣu ca ||
baudhāyanaḥ [1.3.35] |
nauśilāphalakakuñjaraprāsādakaṭakeṣu cakravatsu vādoṣaṃ sahāsanam |
“cakravatsu” śakaṭādiṣu |
āpastambaḥ [1.7.20–23] |
viṣamagate tv ācārya ugrataḥ śūdrato vāharet | sarvadā śūdrata ugrato vācāryārthasyāharaṇaṃ dharmyam ity eke | datvā nānukathayet | kṛtvā ca nānusmaret |
“viṣamagataḥ” āpadgataḥ |
hārītaḥ |
ajñānapaṅkadigdhaṃ vai guruḥ śiṣyaṃ tamohatam |
drutaṃ prakṣālayati saṃskārajñānavāriṇā ||
ṛcaṃ vāpadi vārdharcaṃ pādaṃ vā yadi vākṣaram |
sakāśād yadi gṛhṇīyān niyataṃ tatra gauravam ||
guror apy avaliptasya kāryākāryam ajānataḥ |
utpathapratipannasya parityāgo vidhīyate ||
nādhyāpayati nādhīte paranīyeṣu vartate |
ity etair lakṣaṇair yuktas tyaktavyo vratinā guruḥ ||
samyak pravṛttaṃtu guruṃ śiṣyo yas tv avamanyate |
harate duṣkṛtaṃ cāsya tiryagyoniṃ ca gacchati ||
[224]        āpastambaḥ [1.14.2–4] |
adhyayanārthena yaṃ codayen na cainaṃ prayācakṣīta | na cāsmin doṣaṃ paśyet | yadṛcchāyām asamvṛttau gatir eva tasmin |
manuḥ [2.144] |
ya āvṛṇoty avitathā karmaṇā śravaṇāv ubhau |
sa mātā sa pitā jñeyas taṃ na druhyet kadācan ||
viṣṇuḥ [30.43] |
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ caiva tathādhyātmikam eva ca |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ taṃ na druhyet kadācana ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [2.10–11] |
ya āvṛṇoty avitathena karṇāv
aduḥkhaṃ śṛṇvann amṛtaṃ saṃprayacchan |
taṃ manyet pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca
tasmai na druhyet katamaccanāha ||
adhyāpitā ye guruṃ nādriyante
viprā vācā manasā karmaṇā vā |
yathaiva te na guror bhojanīyās
tathaiva tān na bhunakti śrutaṃ tat ||
[225]
tathātideśaḥ
tatra viṣṇuḥ [32.1–2] |
rājartvikśrotiyādharmapratiṣedhyupādhyāyapitṛvyamātāmahamātulaśvaśura-gurujyeṣṭhabhrātṛsaṃbandhinas tv ācāryavat pūjanīyāḥ | patnyaś caiteṣāṃ savarṇāḥ |
manuḥ [2.205–207] |
guror gurau saṃnihite guruvad vṛttim ācaret |
na cānisṛṣṭo guruṇā svān gurūn abhivādayet ||
vidyāgurus tv etad eva nityā vṛttiḥ svayoniṣu |
pratiṣedhatsu cādharmān hitaṃ copadiśatsv api ||
śreyaḥsu guruvad vṛttiṃ nityam eva samācaret |
guruputreṣu vāryeṣu guroś caiva svabandhuṣu ||
“guroḥ” upanīya vedadātuḥ | “guruvat” evaṃrūpam eva | “anisṛṣṭaḥ” anādiṣṭaḥ | “svān” guruputrādīn | “vidyāguruḥ” upādhyāyaḥ | “nityā” sārvakālikī | “svayoniṣu” pitṛvyādiṣu | “śreyaḥsu” vidyātapaḥsamṛddheṣu | “ācāreṣu” traivarṇikeṣu |
āpastambaḥ [1.14.5–9] |
mātari pitary ācāryavac chuśrūṣā | samāvṛttena sarve gurava upasaṃgrāhyāḥ | proṣya ca samāgame | bhrātṛṣu bhaginīṣu ca yathāpūrvam upasaṃgrahaṇam | nityā ca pūjā yathopadeśam |
“yathāpūrvaṃ” yathājyaiṣṭhyam | “yathāpadeśaṃ” savyena savya ityādyuktaprakāreṇa |
[226] punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.13.12–18] |
na bahirvede gatir vidyate | samādiṣṭam adhyāpayantaṃ yāvad adhyayanam upasamgṛhṇīyāt | nityam arhantam ity eke | na gatir vidyate | vṛddhānāṃ tu | brahmaṇi mitho viniyoge na gatir vidyate | brahma vardhata ity upadiśanti |
“na bahiḥ” iti, yad vedād bahirbhūtaṃ bauddhādiśāstraṃ tad yasmād apayānaṃ tasya gatir na vidyate | “gatiḥ” iti smārtānāṃ dik | śuśrūṣāyāṃ “samādiṣṭaḥ” ācāryeṇa prayojanāntaravyapekṣeṇa “samādiṣṭam adhyāpayantaṃ yāvad adhyayanam upsaṃgṛhṇīyāt” nānyadā | “arhantam” itaraṃ samāvṛttena kriyamāṇam abhivādanaṃ yo ‘rhati tam arhantaṃ, samādiṣṭādhyayanād ārabhya vināpy adhyayanena nityaṃ samāvṛtto ‘py upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt | “na gatir vidyate vṛddhānāṃ tu” iti, adhikavayasam antevāsinaṃ vidyāgurur ātmaśuśrūṣaṇāṃ na kārayed ity arthaḥ | “brahmaṇi” vede “mitho viniyoge” pradeśabhedena parasparādhyāpane sati “na gatir vidyate” na śuśrūṣāsti | “brahma vardhata ity upadiśanti” iti, brahmavardhata iti hetoḥ śuśrūṣam upadiśanty ācāryāḥ | sā ceha brahmavṛddhiḥ parasparādhyayanād eva siddhyatīti pṛthak śuśrūṣā na kāryā |
[227]
|| 13 ||
atha guruputrabhātyānuvṛttiḥ
tatra manuḥ [2.208–209] |
bālaḥ samānajanmā vā śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi |
adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvan mānam arhati ||
“yajñakarmaṇi” yajñkarmapratipādyatvena yasya vedasya tasmin yaḥ śiṣyaḥ taṃ adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvan mānam arhati |
utsādanaṃ gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane |
na kuryād guruputrasya pādayoś cāvanejanam ||
“adhyāpya taṃ” vedāntaraṃ tatpradeśāntaraṃ vā | “utsādanaṃ” udvartanam | “avanejanaṃ” prakṣālanam |
“ācāryavṛttiḥ” ity anuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.7.31] |
hīnavarṇānāṃ gurupatnīnāṃ prāgabhivādanaṃ na pādopasaṃgrahaṇam | gurupatnīnāṃ gātrotsādanāñjanakeśasaṃyamanapādaprakṣālanādīni na kuryāt |
[228]        manuḥ [2.210–215]
guruvat pratipūjyāḥ syuḥ savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ |
asavarṇās tu saṃpūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ ||
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanam eva ca |
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam ||
svabhāva eṣa nārīṇāṃ narāṇām iha dūṣaṇam |
ato ‘rthān na pramādyanti pramadāsu vipaścitaḥ ||
avidvāṃsam alaṃ loke vidvāṃsam api vā punaḥ |
pramadā hy utpathaṃ netuṃ kāmakrodhavaśānugam ||
mātrā svasrā duhitrā vā na viviktāsano bhavet |
balavān indriyagrāmo vidvāṃsam api karṣati ||
“viviktaṃ” rahasyam |
[229]
|| 14 ||
athendriyanigrahaḥ
tatra manuḥ [2.175–182] |
sevetemāṃs tu niyamān brahmacārī gurau vasan |
saṃniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ tapovṛddhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam |
devatābhyarcanaṃ caiva samidādhānam eva ca ||
varjayen madhu māṃsaṃ ca gandhamālyarasān striyaḥ |
śuktāni caiva sarvāṇi prāṇināṃ caiva hiṃsanam ||
abhyaṅgam añjanaṃ cākṣṇor upānacchatradhāraṇam |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca nartanaṃ gītavādanam ||
dyūtaṃ ca janavādaṃ ca parivādaṃ tathānṛtam |
strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambham upaghātaṃ parasya ca ||
ekaḥ śayīta sarvatra na retaḥ skandayet kvacit |
kāmād dhi skandayan reto hinasti vratam ātmanaḥ ||
udakumbhaṃ sumanaso gośakṛnmṛttikākuśān |
āhared yāvad arthāni bhaikṣaṃ cāhar ahaś caret ||
“rasān” guḍādīn rasapradhānān | “śuktāni” svabhāvamadhurāṇi āmrādīni | “kāmo” atra viṣayāntarābhilāṣātiśayaḥ, manmathasya “striya” ity anena niṣiddhatvāt | “janavāda” [230] lokavādaḥ | “strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambham” iti maithunaśaṅkāyāṃ strīṇāṃ snigdhadṛṣṭi-nipātanāliṅgane |
tathā ca varjayed ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [2.22] |
strīprekṣaṇālambhane maithunaśaṅkāyām | iti |
“yāvadarthāni” yāvatprayojanāni |
yamaḥ |
aprakīrṇendriyo dāntaḥ kṣāntaḥ śucir akopanaḥ |
svādhyāyanirato dakṣo naikāhāro jitendriaḥ ||
jitanidro nirālasyo jitakrodho jitātmavān |
śuśrūṣur anahaṃkārī gurudevārcane rataḥ ||
khaṭvāsanaṃ ca śayanaṃ varjayed dantadhāvanam |
svaped ekaḥ kuśeṣv eva na retaḥ skandayet kvacit ||
“aprakīrṇendriyo” avikṣatendriyaḥ | “dāntaḥ” tapaḥkleśasahaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskāra 224, 223]
yāni śāstraniṣiddhāni tāni yatnena varjayet |
dhyāyād ayaṃ guruhitaṃ kuryād devarṣipūjanam ||
upanīto dvijo nityaṃ gurave hi samācaret |
sraṅmālājinasikthādyair na syād aikṣaṇikaḥ kvacit ||
“sraṅmālāḥ” sragdhāraṇam | “sikthaṃ” madhūcchiṣṭam, adharasaṃskārārtham | ādyaśabdād anulepanādigrahaṇam |
[231]
|| 15 ||
atha brahmacāriniyamāḥ
viṣṇuḥ [28.11] |
śrāddhakṛllavaṇaśuktaparyuṣitanṛtyagītastrīmadhumāṃsāñjanocchiṣṭa-prāṇihiṃsāślīlaparimārjanam |
“śrāddhavarjanam” anabhyarthitasya mantavyam,
brāhmaṇaḥ kāmam aśnīyāc chrāddhe vratam apīḍayan |
iti yājñavalkyānusārāt | “kṛḷlavaṇaṃ” kṛtalavaṇam |
gautamaḥ [2.18–19] |
nādityam īkṣeta | varjayen madhumāṃsagandhamālyadivāsvapnāñjanābhyañjana-yānopānacchatrakāmakrodhalobhamohavādavādanasnānadantadhāvanaharṣa-nṛttagītaparivādabhayāni |
“vādo” bahupralāpaḥ | “vādanaṃ” vādyānām | “snānaṃ” upabhogārtham |
varjanānuvṛttau gautamaḥ [2.23–24] |
dyūtaṃ hīnasevām adattādānaṃ hiṃsām ācāryatatputrastrīdīkṣitanāmāni | śuktā vācaḥ |
[232] “śuktāḥ” niṣṭhurāḥ, parodvegakāriṇya iti vacanāt |
āpastambaḥ [1.2.22–29] |
nānuddeśyaṃ bhuñjīta | tathā kṣāralavaṇamadhumāṃsāni | adivāsvāpī | agandhasevī | maithunaṃ na caret | utsannaślāghaḥ | aṅgāni na prakṣālayīta | prakṣālayīta tv aśiciliptāniguror asaṃdarśe |
“anuddeśyaṃ” devatādyarthasaṃkapitam | “utsannaślāghaḥ” tyaktaślāghaḥ, syād iti śeṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
bhaikṣavṛttir ekānnaṃ madhumāṃsakṣāralavaṇāānnāśī, mlecchāśucistrīśūdrāsaṃbhāṣī |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.3.24] |
yāvadarthaṃ saṃbhāṣī stribhiḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.7.3–11] ||
na prekṣeta nagnāṃ striyam | oṣadhivanaspatīnām ācchidya nopajighret | upānahau chatraṃ yānam iti ca varjayet | na smayeta | yadi smayetāpigṛhya smayeteti hi brāhmaṇam | nopajighret striyaṃ mukhena | na hṛdayen prārthayet | nākāraṇād upaspṛśet | rajasvalo raktadant satyavādī syāḍ iti hi brāhmaṇam |
[233] “yadi smayeta” haret tadā “apigṛhya” pidhāya mukham iti śeṣaḥ | “nākāraṇād upaspṛśet” striyam ity anvayaḥ | prastutatvāt “rajasvalo” dhūlidhūsaraḥ | “raktadan” raktadantaḥ |
manuḥ [2.159] |
ahiṃsayaiva bhūtānāṃ kāryaṃ śreyo ‘nuśāsanam |
vāk caiva madhurā ślakṣṇā prayojyā dharmam icchatā ||
“ahiṃsayā” nātipīḍayā | bhūtaśabdenātrānuśāsanīyāḥ | “anuśāsanaṃ” śikṣā | “madhurā” priyā | “ślakṣṇā” anuccaiḥ vārtā |
etac ca prasaṅgād gurukṛtyam uktaṃ manunā [2.160–163] |
yasya vāṅmanasī śuddhe samyaggupte ca sarvadā |
sa vai sarvam avāpnoti vedāntopagataṃ phalam ||
nāruṃtudaḥ syād ārto ‘pi na paradrohakarmadhīḥ |
yayāsyodvijate vācā nālokyāṃ tām udīrayet ||
saṃmānād brāhmaṇo nityam udvijeta viṣād iva |
amṛtasyeva cākāṅkṣed avamānasya sarvadā ||
sukhaṃ hy avamataḥ śete sukhaṃ ca pratibudhyate |
sukhaṃ carati loke ‘sminn avamantā vinaśyati ||
“śuddhe” nirdoṣe | “samyaggupte” doṣaphalarahite | “sarvaṃ” sāyujyādivedāntopagatam upaniṣatpratipāditaṃ [mokṣaphalam] | “aruṃtudaḥ” marmavyathākārī | “nālokyā” asādhvī |
śātātapaḥ |
āpyāyane yathā dhenus tṛṇair amṛtasaṃbhavaiḥ |
evaṃ japyaiś ca homaiś ca punar āpyāyate dvijaḥ ||
[234]        hārītaḥ |
hayagajarathacaityavṛkṣaviṣamārohaṇaphalapracayanasaṃdhisarpaṇa-kūpāvarohaṇamahānadyavaprataraṇamahāsāhasaviruddhāni ca varjayet | calanapramādakaraṇadoṣavidhānaceṣṭāśuktavadana divāsvapnavivādam iti |
“caityaṃ” devatāyatanam | “saṃdhiḥ” atre saṃskārāvakāśaḥ | “mahānadyo” gaṅgādyāḥ | “mahāsāhasaṃ” atra durgamapradeśagamanādikam | “viruddham” rājādidviṣṭagurvāśrayaṇam | “calanam” adhyayanādāv asthiratvam | “pramādakaraṇam” anavadhānenānuṣṭhānam |
āpastambaḥ [1.3.11–15] |
anṛttadarśī | sabhāḥ samājāṃś cāgantā | ajanavādaśīlaḥ | rahaḥśīlaḥ | guror udācāreṣv akartā svairikarmaṇi |
tathā [1.3.21–22] |
dṛḍhadhṛtiḥ | aglāṃsnuḥ |
manuḥ [2.211, 219] |
sūryeṇa hy abhinirmuktaḥ śayānaṃ kāmacārataḥ |
nimloced vāpy avijñānāj japann upavased dinam ||
muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā syād atha vā syāc chikhājaṭaḥ |

jaṭī dīrghanakharomā |
manuḥ [2.223–224] |
yadi strī yady avarajaḥ śreyaḥ kiṃcit samācaret |
tat sarvam ācared yukto yatra vāsya ramen manaḥ ||
dharmārthāv ucyate śreyaḥ kāmārthau dharma eva ca |
artha eveha vā śreyas trivarga iti tu sthitiḥ ||
“avarajaḥ” śūdraḥ | yat “śreyo” gurugṛhalepanamārjanādi tat strīśūdrābhyām apy avāpyamānaṃ dṛṣṭvānutiṣṭhet |
gautamaḥ [9.48–49] |
na pūrvāhṇamadhyaṃdināparāhṇān aphalān kuryād yathāśakti dharmārthakāmebhyaḥ | teṣu tu dharmottaraḥ syāt |
pūrvādīni yathāśakti dharmārthaṃ tatsādhanair yathāsaṃkhyam avandhyāni kuryād ity arthaḥ | “teṣu” dharmādiṣu madhye “dharmottaraḥ syāt” dharmapradhāno bhavet |
hārītaḥ |
upanayanādi mekhalādaṇḍājinayajñopavītadhārī |
[236]        yamaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ caiva mauñjīṃ ca raśanāṃ tathā |
dhārayed brahmacaryaṃ ca bhaikṣānnāśī gurau vasan ||
manu-viṣṇū [Manu 2.64; Viṣṇu 27.28] |
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat ||
atha manur viṣṇuś ca dvitīye [Manu 2.173–174; Viṣṇu 27.28] |
kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanam iṣyate |
brahmaṇo grahaṇaṃ caiva krameṇa vidhipūrvakam ||
yad yasya vihitaṃ karma yat sūtraṃ yā ca mekhalā |
yo daṇḍo yac ca savanaṃ tat tat tasya vrateṣv api ||
“vrateṣu” aupaniṣadādiṣu |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
upanayanād ūrdhvaṃ vracaryā |
manu-bṛhaspatī [Manu 2.108; Bṛh. prāyaś. 67] |
agnīn dhanaṃ bhaikṣacaryām adhaḥśayyāṃ guror hitam |
ā samāvartanāt kuryāt kṛtopanayano dvijaḥ ||
brahmacāridharmānuvṛttau gautamaḥ [3.10] |
uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi |
[237] “etat” brahmacāriṇa uktaṃ dyūtavarjanādi, “avirodhi” yac chāstrād itareṇa vā bādhitaṃ tat “uttareṣāṃ” gṛhasthādīnām anuvartayet | yat tu bādhitaṃ tan nivartayet | yathordhvaretastvaṃ gṛhasthasya |
ācāryānuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.7.31] |
samāvṛttasyāpy etad eva sāmayācārikam eteṣu |
hārītaḥ |
upanayanādi mekhalādaṇḍājinayajñopavītadhārī |
“sāmayācārikaṃ” vidhiniṣedhasaṃbandha iti kapardibhāṣyam |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.13.5] |
na samāvṛtte samādeśo vidyate |
“samādeśaḥ” ācāryājñayaiva karmakaraṇam |
atha manuḥ [2.163–164] |
anena kramayogena saṃskṛtātmā dvijaḥ śanaiḥ |
gurau vasan saṃcinuyād brahmādhigamikaṃ tapaḥ ||
tapoviśeṣair vividhair vrataiś ca vidhicoditaiḥ |
vedaḥ kṛtsno ‘dhigantavyaḥ sarahasyo dvijanmanā ||
āpastambaḥ [1.5.1–2] |
niyameṣu tapaḥśabdaḥ | tadatikrame vidyākarma niḥsravati brahma sahāpatyād etasmāt |
“vidyākarma” vidyāgrahaṇam | “niḥsravati” kṣarati nirvīryaṃ bhavatīti yāvat | “brahma” vedaḥ | “sahāpatyāt” apatyasahitādhyetuḥ |
[238]        tathā [1.5.9–11] |
guruprasādanīyāni karmāṇi svastyayanam adhyayanasaṃvṛttir iti | ato ‘nyāni karmāṇi nivartante brahmacāriṇaḥ | svādhyāyadhṛg dharmarucis tapasvy ṛjur mṛduḥ sidhyati brahmacārī |
“guruprasādanīyāni” ācāryaprasādakarāṇi pādaprakṣālanādikarmāṇi | “svastyayanaṃ” ātmarakṣākaraṇam | rātrau vṛkṣamūlam upasarpatā rāgādinā (?) “adhyayanasaṃvṛttiḥ” vedābhyāsaḥ | “anyāni” devatāyatanakaraṇādīni | “svādhyāyadhṛk” svādhyāyadhṛtiḥ |
hārītaḥ |
athātrodāharanti |
vrataiś chandāṃsi sidhyanti kāmadāni bhavanti ca |
chandasāṃ sādhanārthāya vratān āha cared budhaḥ ||
laghuvyāsaḥ |
saṃskāravadbhiḥ kartavyaḥ vedasyādhyayanaṃ budhaiḥ |
śuddhasya phalavattā syād anyathā niṣphalaṃ smṛtam ||
ṛgvedam apy adhīyīta mārgeṇoktena dharmavit |
na tv eva caturo vedān anyāyena kadācana ||
ṛgyajuḥsāma caivaṃ yan nyāyato ‘dhigataṃ dvijaiḥ |
brahmatvam āpādayati nākrameṇa subahv api ||
vedaviplāvanāt tena varaṃ maunaṃ samāśritam |
vedaviplāvanād vipro narakaṃ yāty adhomukhaḥ ||
[239]        atri-vasiṣtha-aṅgiraso yamaś ca dvitīye |
yathāśvā rathahīnās tu ratho vāśvair yathā vinā |
evaṃ tapo ‘py avidyasya vidyā vāpy atapasvinaḥ ||
yathānnaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ madhu vānnena saṃyutam |
evaṃ tapaś ca vidyā ca saṃyuktaṃ bheṣajaṃ mahat ||
vidyātapobhyāṃ saṃyuktaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ japanaityakam |
sadāpi pāpakarmāṇaṃ pratiyukte na pātakam ||
atha manuḥ [12.104] |
tapo vidyā ca viprasya niḥśryasakaraṃ param |
tapasā kilbiṣaṃ hanti vidyayāmṛtam aśnute ||
devalaḥ |
vidyā vittaṃ tapaś ceti trīṇi tejāṃsi dehinām |
iha cāmutra ca śreyas tad etaiḥ sādhyate tribhiḥ ||
vidyayā vimalaṃ jyotir vittayogāt sukhodayaḥ |
tapasā vimalā bhūtiḥ prāpyate tritayaṃ tribhiḥ ||

athādhyāpyāḥ
tatra bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskāra 222] |
evaṃ daṇḍādikair yuktaṃ saṃskṛtya tanayaṃ pitā |
vedam adhyāpayec chāntaṃ śāstraṃ manvādikaṃ tathā ||
yamaḥ |
saṃvatsaroṣite śiṣye gurur jñānam anirdiśan |
hareta duṣkṛtaṃ tasya śiṣyasya vasto guruḥ ||
guruśuśrūṣānuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.8.24–28] |
tasmin guror vṛttiḥ | putram ivainam anukāṃkṣan sarvadharmeṣv anapacchādayamānaḥ suyukto vidyāṃ grāhayet | na cainam adhyayanavighnenātmārtheṣūparundhyād anāpatsu | antevāsy anantevāsī bhavati vinihitātmā gurāv anaipuṇam āpadyamānaḥ | ācāryo ‘py anācāryo bhavati śrutāt pariharamāṇaḥ |
“tasmin” guruśuśrūṣake “vṛttiḥ” ucyate | “enaṃ” śuśrūṣakam | “anukāṃkṣan” anvicchan | “sarvadharmeṣu” kaṣṭapratipādyeṣv api | “anapacchādayamānaḥ” avañcayan | “suyukto” nānyamanāḥ | “vinihitātmā” vividhaprakāreṇa gurau samarpitātmā | anekeṣu guruṣv ekasyām eva vidyāyām |
[241]        manuḥ [2.109] |
ācāryaputraḥ śuśrūṣur jñānado dhārmikaḥ śuciḥ |
āptaḥ śakto ‘rthadaḥ sādhuḥ svo ‘dhyāpyā daśa dharmataḥ ||
“jñānado” vidyāntarapradaḥ | “śakto” grahaṇadhāraṇayoḥ | “svaḥ” jñātiputrādayaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.28] |
kṛtajñādrohamedhāviśucikalyāṇasūcakāḥ |
adhyāpyā dharmataḥ sādhuśaktāptajñānvittadāḥ ||
“kalyāṇasūcakaḥ” śubhalakṣaṇaḥ | “śaktaḥ” atra grahaṇe | “arthadaḥ” atrābhṛtyartham arthapradātā | “bhṛtakādhyāpanaṃ tathā” ity upapātakamadhye pāṭhāt |
gautamaḥ [10.4] |
ācāryajñātipriyagurudhanavidyāniyameṣu brahmaṇaḥ saṃpradānam anyatra yathoktāt |
“dhanavidyāniyamaḥ” parivartaḥ | “brahmaṇo” vedasya | “anyatra yathoktāt” iti, śuśrūṣakāder anyatraiteṣv eva |
viṣṇuḥ [29.3–4] |
nāparīkṣitaṃ yājayet | nādhyāpayet |
[242]        āpastambaḥ [2.6.1–2] |
jātyācārasaṃśaye dharmārtham āgatam agnim upasamādhāya jātim ācāraṃ ca saṃpṛcchet | sādhutāṃ cet pratijānīte ‘gnir upadraṣṭā vāyur upaśrotādityo ‘nukhyātā sādhutāṃ pratijānīte sādhv asmā astu vitatha eṣa enasa ity uktvā śāstuṃ pratipadyeta |
“agnim upasamādhāya” svagṛhyoktavidhinā |
manu-yamau [Manu 2.110] |
nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyān na vānyāyena pṛcchataḥ |
jānann api hi medhāvī jaḍaval loka ācaret ||
manuḥ [2.113–115] |
vidyayaiva samaṃ kāmaṃ martavyaṃ brahmavādinā |
āpady api hi ghorāyāṃ na tv enām iriṇe vapet ||
vidyā brāhmaṇam etyāha śevadhiṣṭe ‘smi rakṣa mām |
asūyakāya mā mā dās tathā syāṃ vīryavattamā ||
yam eva tu śuciṃ vidyā niyatabrahmacāriṇam |
tasmai māṃ brūhi viprāya nidhpāyāpramādine ||
“śevadhiḥ” nidhiḥ | “iriṇe” anūṣare |
hārītaḥ |
yo vai dhīraḥ śuśrūṣur adhyāyavṛtto
