|| 17 ||
athādyāpanavidhiḥ
tatra manuḥ [2.70–75] |
adhyeṣyamāṇas tv ācānto yathāśāstram udaṅmukhaḥ |
brahmāñjalikṛto ‘dhyāpyo laghuvāsā jitendriyaḥ ||
brahmārambhe ‘vasāne ca pādau grāhyau guroḥ sadā |
saṃhatya hastāv adhyeyaṃ sa hi brahmāñjaliḥ smṛtaḥ ||
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ ||
adhyeṣyamāṇaṃ tu gurur nityakālam atandritaḥ |
adhīṣva bho iti brūyād virāmo ‘stv iti cāramet ||
brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryād ādāv ante ca sarvadā |
sravaty anoṃkṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ parastāc ca viśīryati ||
prākkūlān paryupāsīnaḥ pavitrair eva pāvitaḥ |
prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ pūtas tata oṃkāram arhati ||
“prākkūlān” prāgagrān | adhyayanārambhakāle pavitrāpādakapāvamānyādimantrāsaṃbhavād darbhaiḥ pāvanaṃ gautamokta indriyāṇām upasparśanam |
[245]        tathā [2.76–77] |
akāraṃ cāpy ukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ |
vedatrayān niraduhad bhūr bhuvaḥ svar itīti ca ||
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādaṃ pādam adūduhat |
tad ity ṛco ‘syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ ||
gautamaḥ [1.52–62] |
pāṇinā savyam upasaṃgṛhyānaṅguṣṭham adhīhi bho ityāmantrayeta gurum | tatra cakṣurmanāḥ | prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ | prāṇāyāmās trayaḥ pañcadaśamātrāḥ | prākkūleṣv āsanaṃ ca | oṃkārapūrvā vyāhṛtayaḥ pajca satyāntāḥ | guroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ prātaḥ | brahmānuvacane cādyantayoḥ | anujñāta upaviśet prāṅmukho dakṣiṇataḥ śiṣya udaṅmukho vā | sāvitrī cānuvacanam ādito brahmaṇa ādāne | oṃkāro ‘nyatrāpi |
“pāṇinā” dakṣiṇena “savyaṃ” pāṇim ātmīyaṃ brahmāñjalir yathā bhavati tathā hastau kṛtvety arthaḥ | “tatra” gurau manaścakṣuṣī vyāpṛkte yasya saḥ “tatra cakṣurmanāḥ” | “prāṇāḥ” indriyāṇi | “mātrāḥ” hrasvākṣarakālāḥ | “satyāntāḥ” bhūr bhuvaḥ svah puruṣaḥ satyam ity evaṃprakārāḥ | vyāhṛtīnāṃ puruṣāntarapāṭhāt satyāntā ity abhidhānam | “brahmaṇa āditaḥ” ārambhe sāvitrī ca vaktavyā |
āpastambaḥ [1.10.15–16] |
gurusaṃnidhau cādhīhi bho ity uktvādhīyīta | adhyāpayed vā |
[246]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
āhūtādhyāyī pavitrāñjalipāṇiḥ sapavitraṃ gāyatrīm anuśrāvya guror abhimukhaḥ svādhyāyam anupaṭhet |
yamaḥ |
kṛtāñjalir guruṃ brūyād adhyeṣye bhagavann aham |
jitendriyaḥ śuklavāsā brahmāñjalikṛtaḥ sthiraḥ ||
“brahmāñjalikṛtaḥ” kṛtabrahmāñjaliḥ | āhitāgnyāder ākṛtigaṇatvāt kṛtaśabdasya paranipātaḥ |
oṃkāraṃ prathamaṃ kṛtvā tato brahma pravartayet |
oṃkāraṃ ca tataḥ kṛtvā bhūmiṃ spṛṣṭvā virāmayet ||
anyathā viparītaṃ syād brahma tan nātra saṃśayaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
svādhyāyam ahar ahaḥ prayuñjānaḥ karṇāv apidhāya pañcākṣarāṇy anujapya mastakena pādāv ālabhyābhivādayet | anālabhya gurupatnyām |
“pañcākṣarāṇi” bhūr bhuvaḥ svar ity etāni |
saṃvartaḥ |
hastau susaṃhatau kṛtvā dhāryau jānūpari sthitau |
guror anukṛtiṃ kuryāt paṭhan nānyamatir bhavet ||
āpastambaḥ [1.13.6–8] |
oṃkāraḥ svargadvāraṃ tasmād brahmādhyeṣyamāṇa etadādi pratipadyeta | vikathāṃ cānyāṃ kṛtvaivaṃ laukikyā vācā vyāvartate brahma | yajñeṣu caitadādayaḥ prasavāḥ |
[247] “vikathāṃ cānyāṃ kṛtvā” iti, vikathādhyayanavijātīyā kathā | “etadādi” oṃkārādi | evaṃ “laukikyā vācā vyāvartate brahma” iti, vijātīyakathayā laukikyā vācā saṃsṛṣṭo vedaḥ punaḥ praṇavapūrvakavedapāṭhe vyāvartate | laukikyā vācā evaṃ vivikto bhavatīty arthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
atha brāhmaṇakarmadharmavṛtyupasargān nirūpayiṣyāmaḥ | sarvaśrāvaṇaṃ brahmatarkaṇam ādeśanaṃ chandasām anupacāro nāmnopādhyāyādhyayanayo rahasyakhyāpanam anuyogo granthabhṛtām | avratādhyayanam aniyamādhyayanam aguruśuśrūṣādhyayanaṃ krayādhyayanaṃ gurudveṣaṇam ity adhyayanasya | saṃkarasaṃkīrṇāsaṃvyavahārasyādhyayanaṃ bhṛtakādhyayanaṃ ity adhyāpyasya | sarveṣām anuvidrutavācanaṃ prakīrṇavācanam adrutavācanam akṣaravarṇapadapadārdhavihīnaṃ viparyastam anāmnātam avicchinnam asamāptam apratiṣṭhitaṃ ceti japyasyaite | brahmopasargādyair upaspṛṣṭaṃ brahma teṣām avīryam atejaskam asiddham asamṛddhārtham anāyuṣkam adaivam asvargyyam aphalaṃ bhavati | tasmād etān dharmān dhārayann adhīyītādhyāpayej japet | evaṃ brahmopasargair vimuktāni chandāṃsy adhīyan sa vai pūtaḥ samṛddhārthaḥ sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
upasargair vimuktānāṃ yo ‘dhīte yaṃ kratuṃ dvijaḥ |
tena tenedaṃ bhavati prajāpativaco yathā ||
“brāhmaṇakarmadharmavṛtti” iti | brāhmaṇakarmaṇīva te dharmavṛttī ceti karmadhārayaḥ | tatra dharmo ‘dhyayanam | “vṛttiḥ” svādhyāyādhyāpanam | “upasargāḥ” upadravāḥ | “sarvaśrāvaṇaṃ” sarvaḥ [248] śūdrādiḥ | “brahmatarkaṇaṃ” kutarkaṇaṃ vedasyāprāmānyāpādanam | “ādeśanaṃ” etad anupasannaviṣayam | “anuyogo” atrāsthāne praśnaḥ | ete granthabhṛtām upasargāḥ | “vratāni” yogābhyāsādīni vedādhyayanārthāni (?) | “niyamāḥ” adhyayanārtham araṇyāvasthānādayaḥ | “krayādhyayanam” etāvatā dhanenaitāvat paṭheyeti vyavasthayādhyayanam | "saṃkaro" anūḍhājātaḥ | "saṃkīrṇaḥ" pātakasaṃsṛṣṭaḥ | "saṃvyavahāro" lokapaṅktiprādhānyam | "sarveṣāṃ" granthabhṛdadhyetradhyāpakānām | "prakīrṇaṃ" ativilambitam | "adrutam" uccair atvaritam | "akṣarāṇi" ekārādīni | "varṇāḥ" kakārādayaḥ | "viparyastaṃ" bhrāntam | "anāmnātam" atrāpaśrutyādhītam | "avicchinnaṃ" traisvaryārdharcādivicchedaśūnyam | "asamāptaṃ" atitvarayāj (?) nimittādijātivarṇarahitam | "apratiṣṭhitam" asaṃkhyātam | “japyasya” japanīyasya mantrasya | “ete” upasargāḥ |
[249]
|| 18 ||
anadhyāyāḥ
gautamaḥ [16.22–50] |
ākālikā [nirghāta]bhūmikamparāhudarśanolkāḥ | stanayitnuvarṣavidyutaś ca prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu | ahar ṛtau | vidyuti naktaṃ cāpararātrāt | tribhāgādipravṛttau sarvam | ulkā vidyutsamety ekeṣām | stanayitnur aparāhṇe | api pradoṣe | sarvaṃ naktam ārdharātrāt | ahaś cit [?] sajyotiḥ | viṣayasthe ca rājñi prete | viproṣya cānyonyena saha | saṃkulopāhitavedasamāpticchardiśrāddhamanuṣyayajña-bhojaneṣv ahorātram | amāvāsyāyāṃ ca | dvyahaṃ vā | kārtikī phālguny āṣāḍhī paurṇamāsī | tisro ‘ṣṭakās trirātram | antyām eke | abhito vārṣikam | sarve varṣavidyutstanayitnusaṃnipāte | prasyandini saṃnipāte trirātram | ūrdhvaṃ bhojanād utsave | prādhītasya ca | niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtam | nityam eke nagare | mānasam apy aśuciḥ | śrāddhinām ākālikam | akṛtānnaśrāddhikasaṃyoge ca | pratividyaṃ ca yān smaranti |
“ahaḥ” iti praharacatuṣṭayopalakṣaṇam | “ṛtau” varṣāsu, prāduṣkṛtāgnisamaye meghavṛṣṭyor darśane ‘har anadhyāyaḥ | etenānantaroktasyākālikatvasyānṛtuviṣayatoktā bhavati | “vidyuti” [250] varṣāsu sāyaṃsaṃdhyāsamaye dṛśyamānāyāṃ naktam anadhyāyaḥ | sa cāparāhṇasya tribhāgam ārabhya sāyaṃprātaḥsaṃdhyāsamaye vidyuti ahar aparāhṇparyantam anuvartamānāyṃ anktam anadhyāyaḥ | sa cāpararātraparyantaṃ cakārāt prātaḥsaṃdhyāsamaye vidyuti ahar aparāhṇaparyantam ity uktaṃ bhavati | “tribhāgādi” iti rātrer ahnaś ca tribhāgam ārabhya sāyaṃprātaḥsaṃdhyāparyantam anuvartamānāyāṃ vidyuti sarvaṃ naktaṃ sarvam ahas tatparyantam anadhyāya it jayasvāmī | “ulkā” iti, vidyutīvolkāyām anadhyāya ity ekeṣāṃ matam | “stanayitnuḥ” meghaḥ, asāv aparāhṇe bhavan vidyutsamo ‘nadhyāyaḥ | “api pradoṣe” prodoṣe ‘pi bhavat stanayitnur vidyudbhavam anadhyāyaṃ karoti | “ahaś cit” iti, ahaś cet prāg aparāhṇād anuvartate meghas tadā divāntaṃ yāvad anadhyāyaḥ | “viṣayasthe” iti, yaddeśe ‘dhyetā bhavati taddeśarājani mṛte ‘nadhyāyo nānyadeśasthe | “viproṣya ca” iti, śiṣyādhyāpkayor vipravāse ākālikānadhyāyasya pūrvaprastutatvāt | “saṃkule” saṃkīrṇe | “upāhite” agnyutpāte | śrāddhagrahaṇenaikoddiṣṭasyāpy upādānāt pūrvam ekoddiṣṭagrahaṇaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣavad ābdikādiparaṃ doṣaviśeṣajñāpanārtham | “yajñabhojane” atithitvena bhojane | “dvyahaṃ vā” iti pratipadgrahaṇārtham | “kārttikīpaurṇamāsī” ity anadhyayane prāpte punarvacanaṃ doṣātiśayakathanārtham | "tisro 'ṣṭ”kā" iti, āgrahāyiṇyā ūrdhvam | “antyām eke” iti, antyām aṣṭakāyāṃ trirātram anadhyāya iti eke manyante | yeṣām ekasyām aṣṭakāyām aṣṭikākarma tadviṣayam idam | “prasyandini” varṣābhyo ‘nyatra | “prādhīnasya” prathamam adhyetuṃ pravṛttasya, bhojanād ūrdhvam anadhyāya ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “pratividya” iti, yān anadhyāyān [251] pāraṃparyeṇa smaranti teṣv api teṣvapi (?) nādhyetavyam | yathā ṛgvede prakṛtākhyasyādhyāyasya bhojanād ūrdhvam anadhyayanam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
nīlalohitaprādurbhāve |
“rātrāv indradhanur dṛṣṭvā” ity atraiva pūvam uktatvād ahni nīlalohitendradhanuḥprādurbhāve iha ahorātram (?) anadhyayanam |
punaḥ paiṭhīnasiḥ |
śuklacaturdaśyāṃ sarvāsu cāṣṭamīṣu punar naktam anadhyāyo ‘ṣṭakāvarjam |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.21.6] |
deśapatiśrotriyaikatīrthaprāyaṇeṣv ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ |
“deśapatiḥ” sainikādiḥ, rājñi yājñavalkyena trirātrānadhyayanābhidhānāt | “śrotriyaḥ” svaśākhetaraśākhāpāṭhaparaḥ | tena smṛtyantare śrotriye trirātrābhidhānaṃ svaśākhādhyāyiviṣayam, “svaśākhāśrotriye tathā” iti yājñavalkyavacanāt |
punaḥ baudhāyanaḥ [1.21.17–18] |
śūdrāpapātraśravaṇasaṃdarśanayoś ca tāvantaṃ kālam | naktaṃ śivāvirāve nādhīyīta svapnāntam |
“apapātraṃ” patitacāṇḍālādiḥ |
[252] punaḥ baudhāyanaḥ [1.11.22] |
anyeṣu cādbhutotpāteṣv ahorātram anadhyāyo ‘nyatra mānasāt |
gautamaḥ [16.47] |
mānasam apy aśuciḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ekādaśāhe sūtake ca bhuktvā, rātrāv indradhanur dṛṣṭvā, vajrapātanād ācāryamaraṇād indrapātanāt sūryacandramasor upaplavāt ketudarśanān nirghātabhūmivalanād ekaikasmiṃs trirātram |
āpastambaḥ [1.9.4–12] |
nigameṣv adhyayanaṃ varjayet | ānaḍuhena vā śakṛtpiṇḍenopalipte ‘dhīyīta | śmaśāne sarvataḥ śamyāprāsāt | grāmeṇādhyavasite kṣetreṇa vā nānadhyāyaḥ | jñāyamāne tu tasminn eva deśe nādhīyīta | śmaśānavac chūdrapatitau | samānāgāra ity eke | śūdrāyāṃ tu prekṣaṇa[pratiprekṣaṇayor evā]nadhyāyaḥ | tathānyasyāṃ striyāṃ varṇavyatikrāntāyāṃ maithune |
“nigamāḥ” catvarāḥ | “śamyā” yajñāyudhaviśeṣaḥ | “prāsaḥ” prakṣepaḥ | “sarvataḥ” sarvāsu dikṣu | “grāmeṇādhyavasite kṣetreṇa vā” grāmatāṃ kṣetratāṃ ca gate | “jñāyamāne” śmaśānatve pratyabhijñāyamāne | “prekṣaṇapratiprekṣaṇe” anyonya[253]darśaṇe | “tathānyasyāṃ” ityāder ayam arthaḥ — adhamavarṇena mauthunaṃ gatāyā ato ‘nyonyadarśane ‘nadhyāya iti |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.9.14–16] |
antaḥśavam | antaścāṇḍālam | abhinirhṛtānāṃ tu sīmny anadhyāyaḥ |
eteṣv eva cāṇḍālādiṣv āgatya grāmabāhyaṃ gateṣv api tad ahar anadhyāyaḥ |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.9.22] |
[upavyuṣaṃ] yāvatā vā kṛṣṇāṃ rohiṇīm it śamyāprāsād vijānīyād etasmin kāle vidyotamāne sapradoṣam [ahar anadhyāyaḥ] |
yāvatā kālena śamyāprāsād anucchinnadeśe kṛṣṇā rohiṇī vā gaur jñāyate, etasmin kāle vidyuti jñāyamānāyāṃ rātriprathamapraharasahitaṃ dinam anadhyāyaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
grāmanagaradvārarathyācatvaratrisaṃdhyam ātiṣṭhann amedhyāśuciś ca nādhīyīta | samūhasaṃtatastanitavidyudabhiśravasāṃ śītavato ‘śanivarṣeṣu mahāsaṃbhramasamgrāmanagaragobrāhmaṇoprodhāntaḥśavacāṇḍālapulkasa-mauṣṭikeṣu tatkālam anadhyāyaḥ |
“grāmanagaradvārarathyācatvaratrisaṃdhyam” iti dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | atra cādhītiṃ varjayed ity anuvartate | “samūho” nānājātijanamelakaḥ | saṃtataśabdo nairantarye | saṃtataśabdaḥ stanitādibhir varṣāntaiḥ pratyekam abhisambadhyate | “abhiśravaḥ” [254] śṛgālādīnāṃ militānāṃ dīrghaḥ śabdaḥ | śītādayaś saṃtatā anṛtāv anadhyayanahetavaḥ | “mahāsaṃbhramaḥ” grāmadāhādinā janānām ākulatā | antaḥśavādayo yatra | “puṣkaso” (?) mlecchaviśeṣaḥ | “mauṣṭiko” mallaḥ | “tatkālam” tāvatkālam |
punaḥ hārītaḥ |
nirvātabhūmikampolkāvātādigdāhapāṃsuśoṇamāṃsāsthikeśadadhijalāṅgāra-varṣeṣu śāntisvatyayanakṛtyabhyuditābhinirmuktavāntaskannanagnaduḥsvapna-darśaneṣv ekoddiṣṭasūtakeṣv ādyaprāyaścittaṃ kṛtvaiteṣu nimitteṣu śānti-svastyayanādikaraṇāvadhir anadhyāyaḥ |
“abhyudita” iti, yasmin praśne sūrya udeti so ‘bhyuditaḥ | yasmiṃś ca praśne ‘stameti saḥ “abhinirmuktaḥ” | “vāntaḥ” bhuktvā kṛtavamanaḥ | “skannaḥ” skhalitaretāḥ | “ādya” ābhimukhyena prādhānyena bhuktvā | atra cāṅpūrvādaderly api “bahulaṃ chandasi” iti bahulavacanāj jagdhir ādeśo na bhavati | etac cābhyuditeṣu nimitteṣu prāyaścittākaraṇe ‘nadhyāyaḥ |
punaḥ hārītaḥ |
caitraśrāvaṇamārgaśīrṣāṇām ādipratipado nityāḥ |
“ādipratipadaḥ” śuklapratipadaḥ | “nityā” ity anena pratipadantare ‘nadhyāyasya kāmyatvam uktam |
“adhīyīta” ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
baliśrāddhamahotsaveṣūlkāvad anadhyāyaḥ |
[255] “baliḥ” devatopahāraḥ | atra cānadhyāye ‘vadhiviśeṣānupādānād ahaḥśeṣam ahar anadhyāyaḥ |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.9.23–26] |
dahre cāpararātre ca stanayitnunā | ūrdhvam ardharātrād ity eke | gavāṃ cāvarodhe | vadhyānāṃ ca yāvatā vā hanyante |
“apararātre” rātres tṛtīye bhāge, “dahre” atrāpi pradoṣe, “ahar anadhyāyaḥ” ity etena saṃbandhaḥ | “stanayitnunā” śabdavatā meghena nimittena | “avarodhe” prasabhān nirodhe | “vadhyānāṃ” vadhyahananaparyanto ‘nadhyāyaḥ |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.10.2–4] |
vairamaṇo guruṣv āṣṭākya aupākaraṇa iti tryahāḥ | tathā saṃbandhiṣu jñātiṣu | mātari pitary ācārya iti dvādaśāhāḥ |
“vairamaṇe” adhyayanasamāptidine | “guruṣu” saṃsthiteṣu | “āṣṭakye” aṣṭakāyām | ayaṃ ca dvādaśāhānadhyāyaḥ | “mātari” jananyām | “pitari” janake | “ācārye” upanīya sāṅgasarahasyavedādhyāpitari | ācārye tu ahar anadhyayanaṃ gauṇādhyāyaviṣayam |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.10.20–23] |
śākhāntare ca sāmnām anadhyāyaḥ | sarveṣu ca śabdakarmasu yatra saṃsṛjyeran | chardayitvā svapnāntam | sarpir vā prāśya |
“śākhāntare” pāṭhya iti śeṣaḥ | “sarveṣu ca” iti, tālagītādiśabdeṣu militeṣv ity arthaḥ |
[256] punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.10.27] |
prodakayoś ca pāṇyoḥ |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.11.1–7] |
kāṇḍopākaraṇe vāmātṛkasya | kāṇḍasamāpane vāpitṛkasya | manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ ca devānāṃ yajñe bhuktvety eke | paryuṣitais taṇḍulair āmamāṃsena ca nānadhyāyaḥ | tathaiṣadhivanaspatimūlaphalaiḥ | yat kāṇḍam upākurvanti tasya cānuvākyaṃ kurvīta na tat tad ahar adhīyīta | upākaraṇasamāpanayoś ca pārāyaṇasya tāṃ vidyām |
“kāṇḍopākaraṇe” kāṇḍopakrame | “manuṣyaprakṛtīnāṃ” iti, ye manuṣyāḥ santaḥ karmaṇā devatvam āpannāḥ nandīśvarādayaḥ teṣāṃ yajñe bhuktvānadhyāya ity arthaḥ | “paryuṣitais taṇḍulaiḥ” iti, taṇḍulagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | tena dināntaraṃ manuṣyaprakṛtidevayajñeṣu taṇḍulādiṣu bhukteṣu māṃsena ca bhuktena nānadhyāya ity arthaḥ | “upākaraṇa” ityādi, “pārāyaṇaṃ” ādita ārabhyādṛṣṭārthaṃ niyamaviśeṣaṇāntaṃ yāvat paṭhanaṃ tad yajñavidyāyāḥ | tām eva tadupakrame samāptau ca nādhīyīta |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.11.10–12] |
svairikarmasu ca | yathā pādaprakṣālanotsādanānulepanānīti | tāvantaṃ kālaṃ nādhīyītādhyāpayed vā |
“svairikarma” svecchākarma | “utsādanaṃ” udvartanam |
[257] punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.11.28–34] |
muhūrtaṃ virate vāte | salāvṛkyām ekasṛka iti svapnaparyantam | naktaṃ cāraṇye ‘nagnāv ahiraṇye vā | ananūktaṃ cāpartau chandaso nādhīyīta | pradoṣe ca | sārvakālikam āmnātam | yathoktam anyad ataḥ pariṣatsu |
“ananūktam” anadhītapūrvam | “apartau” vyapagate vedādhyayanakāle, upākaraṇād arvāg iti yāvat | “salāvṛkī” gṛdhravāsinī | “ekasṛkaḥ” ekacaraḥ sṛgāla iti kapardī | etayoḥ śabde svapnaparyantama nadhyāyaḥ |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [1.32.12–14] |
anadhyāyo niśāyām anyatra dharmopadeśāc chiṣyebhyaḥ | manasā vā svayam | ūrdhvam ardharātrād adhyāpanam |
“anadhyāyaḥ” anadhyayanam | “niśāyāṃ” mahāniśāyām | “manasā vā” iti, mānasam ardharātrād adhyayanaṃ kāryam |
“anadhyāya” ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [13.35] |
diṅnāgaparvatārcāprapāteṣūpalarudhirapāṃsuvarṣeṣv ākālikam |
“arcā” pratimā |
“adhīyīta” ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
na nagaracatuṣpathamahāpathasaṃkrameṣu |
[258]        tathā |
na rājamahāpuruṣāṇāṃ svarjāte |
“mahāpuruṣaḥ” utkṛṣṭacaritaḥ puruṣaḥ |
tathā |
na kulamitre ‘tīte |
tathā |
nāvabhṛthayajñavāṭau | nāpaśrūyataḥ |
yajñārthaṃ parivṛttā bhūmiḥ “yajñavāṭiḥ” | “nāpaśrūyataḥ” nānyonyaṃ mahati saṃbādha ity arthaḥ | anyathā viplavaḥ |
tathā |
na kaumudyām |
sarvarātrā “kaumudī” āśvayukpaurṇamāsī |
tathā |
nendracāpadarśanāt |
tathā [30.19–21] |
na vāntaḥ | na viraktaḥ | nājīrṇī |
aṅgirāḥ |
nādhyetavye dvijair ṛgyajuṣī sāmadhvanau | kathaĕmid yajñe ‘dhyeye
tadaṅgatvāt |
manuḥ [44, 99 ??] |
nāvispṛṣṭam adhīyīta na śūdraṃ janasaṃnidhau |
[259]        na niśānte pariśrānto brahmādhītya punaḥ svapet ||
adhyayanam ity anuvṛttau devīpurāṇe |
na saṃkīrṇajane kuryān na ca taskarasaṃnidhau |
na śmaśrukarake rakte karavādisamāvṛte ||
“saṃkīrṇajane” deśe | “śmaśrukaro” nāpitaḥ | “karavādiḥ” ulūkaḥ |
na śrāddhabhuktau na śrānto nājīrṇo na ca kāmitaḥ |
indrotsave ca durdeśe na saṃkrāntau tahāpaṇe ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe |
mahānavamyāṃ dvādaśyāṃ bharaṇyām api caiva hi |
tathākṣayatṛtīyāyāṃ śiṣyān nādhyāpayed budhaḥ ||
māghamāse ca saptamyāṃ rathākhyāyāṃ ca varjayet |
athāpaṇe tathābhyaktaḥ snānakāle ca varjayet ||
nīyamānaṃ śavaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahīsthaṃ vā dvijottama |
“dvādaśyāṃ” śayanotthānadvādaśyām | “bharaṇyāṃ śakrapātabharaṇyām | “rathākhyāyāṃ” saptamyām |
“nādhīyīta” ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhaḥ |
devāyatanavalmīkaśmaśānaśavasaṃnidhau |
rājatsu ca karoṣṭreṣu gajeṣu ca hayeṣu ca ||
chidrāṇy āhur dvijātīnām anadhyāyān manīṣiṇaḥ |
chidrebhyaḥ sravati brahma brāhmaṇena yadārjitam ||
[260]        chidreṣv evāvatiṣṭhanti ye kecit puruṣādakāḥ |
tatrasthā brahma te ghnanti brāhmaṇair yad upārjitam ||
tathā |
āyuḥ prajāṃ paśūn medhāṃ kṛtyāni sukṛtāni ca |
anadhyāyeṣv abhyasato brahma vyāhāratas tathā ||
aṣṭamī hanty upādhyāyaṃ śiṣyaṃ hanti caturdaśī |
hanti pañcadaśī medhāṃ tasmāt parvāṇi varjayet ||
prajāpatir hi tiṣṭhati sarvavidyāsv aparvasu |
tasmād dharmārthakāmo vai naitāḥ parvasu kīrtayet ||
nityapraśnaśabditabrahmayajñānuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.11.17–20] |
tasya vidhiḥ | akṛtaprātarāsa udakāntaṃ gatvā prayataḥ śucau deśe ‘dhīyīta yathādhyāyam utsṛjan vācā | manasā vānadhyāye |
manuḥ [2.104–107] |
apāṃ samīpe niyato naityakaṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ |
sāvitrīm apy adhīyīta gatvāraṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ ||
vedopakaraṇe caiva svādhyāye caiva naityake |
nānurodho ‘sty anadhyāye homamantreṣu caiva hi ||
“vedopakaraṇaṃ” vyākaraṇādi | homamantragrahaṇaṃ karmamātrānuṣṭhānakālīnastotra-śastrādirūpamantrapradarśanārtham, [261] “vidyāṃ praty anadhyāyaḥ śrūyate na karmayoge mantrāṇām” ity āpastambavacanāt |
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo brahmasatraṃ hi tat smṛtam |
brahmāhutihutaṃ puṇyam anadhyāyavaṣaṭkṛtam ||
yaḥ svādhyāyam adhīte ‘bdaṃ vidhinā niyato dvijaḥ |
tasya nityaṃ kṣaraty eṣa payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu ||
ity anadhyayanam
[262]
|| 19 ||
athādhyeyam
tatra bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskāra 226] |
brāhmaṇair dharmamūlatvaṃ śrutismṛtayoḥ samaṃ smṛtam |
sadācārasya ca tathā jñeyam etat trikaṃ sadā ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na śāstram aphalaṃ kuryāt | na vedam anadhītyānyāṃ vidyām adhīyītānyatra vedāṅgasmṛtibhyaḥ |
manuḥ [12.95–96] |
yā vedabāhyāḥ śrutayo yāś ca kāś ca kudṛṣṭayaḥ |
sarvās tā niṣphalāḥ proktāḥ tamoniṣṭhā hi tāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
utpadyante cyavante ca yāny ato ‘nyāni kānicit |
tāny arvākkālikatayā niṣphalāny anṛtāni ca ||
“vedabāhyāḥ śrutayaḥ” caityavandanādinodikāḥ | “kudṛṣṭayaḥ” kudarśanāni bauddhādiśāstrāṇi |
[263]  tathā [2.168] |
yo ‘nadhītya dvijo vedam anyatra kurute śramam |
sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ ||
“sānvayaḥ” samantāt |
atha brahmacaryakālāvadhiḥ
devalaḥ |
ataḥ param aṣṭācatvāriṃśadvārṣikīṃ vedacaryām adhitiṣṭhet | aśaktaś ced vārṣikīṃ caturviṃśativārṣikīṃ dvādaśavārṣikīṃ vā |
baudhāyanaḥ [1.2.1–6] |
aṣṭācatvāriṃad varṣāṇi paurāṇaṃ vedabrahmacaryam | caturviṃśatiṃ dvādaśa vā prativedam | saṃvatsarārdhaṃ vā pratikāṇḍam | grahaṇāntaṃ vā | jīvitasyāsthiratvāt | “kṛṣṇakeśo 'gnīn ādadhīteti” śruteḥ |
“paurāṇaṃ” purātanair manvādibhir vācaritam | “saṃvatsarārdhaṃ vā pratikāṇḍaṃ” kāṇḍaṃ kāṇḍaṃ prati saṃvatsarārdham | “grahaṇāntaṃ” iti, brahmacarye “grahaṇāntaṃ vā” ity atra hetuḥ “jīvitasyāsthiratvāt” iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.36] |
prativedaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dvādaśābdāni pañca vā |
grahaṇāntikam ity eke keśāntaś caiva ṣoḍaśe ||
[264]        manuḥ [2.65] |
keśāntaḥ ṣoḍaśe varṣe brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
rājanyabandhor dvāviṃśe vaiśyasya dvyadhike tataḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
dīrghakālaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dhāraṇaṃ ca kamaṇḍaloḥ |
gotrān mātṛsapiṇḍād vā vivāho govadhas tathā |
narāśvamedhaṃ madyaṃ ca kalau varjyaṃ dvijātibhiḥ ||
athācāryābhāve ‘nukalpaḥ
āpastambaḥ [1.7.12–13] |
yāṃ vidyāṃ kurute gurau te ‘py asyācāryā ye tasyāṃ guror vaṃśyāḥ | yān anyān paśyato ‘syopasaṃgṛhṇīyāt tadā tv eta upasaṃgrāhyāḥ |
“yāṃ vidyāṃ” ṛgvedādirūpām, “gurau kurute” ācārye brahmacāry ātiṣṭhet, ācāryasya sakāśāt prāpnoti, “asyāḥ” vidyāyāḥ ācāryāḥ asyāpi ye ācāryāḥ tadvaṃśyāḥ, te ‘pi “asya” brahmacāriṇaḥ ācāryāḥ | “yān” iti, “asya” brahmacāriṇaḥ “paśyataḥ” sataḥ “yān anyān” natvā guruḥ “upasaṃgṛhṇīyāt” “tadā tu” evaṃkāle “ete” upasaṃgrāhyāḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
brahmacaryasyā samāpteḥ mṛtaś ced ācāryo guruvṛttiṃ samastāṃ putre vartayet | avidyamāne putre bhrātari vaset |
iti sāṃdhivigrahikabhaṭṭaśrīhṛdayadharātmaja
śrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
ācārākhye brahmacārikāṇḍe upakurvāṇakaḥ |
[265]
|| 20 ||
atha cāturāśramyavyavasthā
tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ [7.1-17] |
catvāra āśramāḥ brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ | teṣāṃ vedaṃ samadhītya vedau vedān vāviśīrṇabrahmacaryo yam icchet tam āviśet | brahmacāry ācāryaṃ paricared ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt | ācārye ca prete ‘gniṃ paricaret | vijñāyate hy agnir ācāryas taveti | saṃyatavāk | caturthaṣaṣṭhāṣtamakālabhojī bhaikṣyasya | gurvādhīnaḥ | jaṭilaḥ śikhājaṭo vā | guruṃ gacchantam anugacchet | āsīnaṃ cet tiṣṭhan śayānaṃ ced āsīna upāsīta | āhūtādhyāyī | sarvaṃ labdhaṃ nivedya tadanujñayā bhuñjīta | khaṭvāśayanadantaprakṣālanāñjanābhyañjanavarjyaṃ sthānāsanaśīlaḥ trir ahno ‘bhyupeyād apa iti |
“aviśīrṇabrahmacaryaḥ” aviplutabrahmacaryaḥ | “saṃyatavāk” maunī | “caturthaṣaṣṭhāṣṭama-kālabhojī” iti, autsargikaṃ yāvad bhojyaṃ tadapekṣayā caturthādikālasaṃkhyā | “trir ahnyo ‘bhyupeyād apa” iti, triṣavaṇasnānaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
[266]        baudhāyanaḥ |
brahmacāriṇo ‘tyantam ātmānam upasaṃgṛhyācāryakule śrāmyanti | eke savaneṣūpaspṛśanto vānyenānyenaikāgniṃ juhvānāḥ | saṃnyasyaike karmāṇy anagnayo ‘niketāḥ | kaupīnācchādanāḥ | varṣāsv ekasthāḥ | pariśrutābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kurvāṇāḥ | kāṣāyavāsāḥ | sannamusale vyaṅgāre nivṛttaśarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣeta | vāṅmanaḥkarmadaṇḍair bhūtānām adrohī | pavitraṃ bibhṛyāc chaucārtham | uddhṛtābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kuryāt | apavidhya vaidikāni karmāṇy ubhayataḥ paricchinnā madhyamaṃ padaṃ saṃśliṣyāmaha iti vadantaḥ |
“brahmacāriṇo” naiṣṭhikāḥ “ātmānaṃ” śarīraṃ “upasaṃgṛhya” saṃnirudhya “ācāryakule śrāmyanti” iti saṃbandhaḥ | “śrāmyanti” tapaḥ kurvanti | “eke” vānaprasthāḥ | “savaneṣu” prātarādiṣu | “upaspṛśantaḥ” snāsyantaḥ | “vanyena” nīvārādinā | “eke” saṃnyāsino yoganiṣṭhāḥ | “karmāṇi” agnisādhyāni | “saṃnyasya” parivrajya | “kaupīnaṃ” kutsitaṃ prajananaṃ tāvanmātre “ācchādanaṃ” yeṣāṃ te kaupīnācchādanāḥ | “ekasthāḥ” ekatra vasantaḥ | “pariśrutābhiḥ” jalapavitragālitābhiḥ | “karmāṇi vaidikāni” ca japasvādhyāyādīni | “apavidhya ubhayataḥ pricchinnāḥ” sukhaduḥkhamānāvamānādiprakāradvaṃdvāt pratyakkṛtāḥ | “madhyamaṃ padaṃ” sukhaduḥkhasaṃnibham aiśvaraṃ prakṛtipuruṣayor vā madhyavarti brahmākhyaṃ padaṃ vadantaḥ śrāmyantīti saṃbandhaḥ |
laghuvyāsaḥ |
naiṣṭhiko vā munir vāpi parivrājaka eva vā |
yam icched āśramaṃ yatra vaset tatraiva saṃyataḥ ||
[267]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
ūrdhvaretasa evaike pārivrājyam icchanti na gṛhasthavānaprasthayoḥ | pūrvasyānucchedād ubhayadharmopapatter uttarasyānapekṣyatvād aikāśramyam |
“ūrdhvaretasa eva” ity atra hetuḥ “pūrvasya” ityādi | tasyācāryapūrvasyordhvaretastvasya pārivrājye ‘py anucchedāt “ubhayadharmopapatteḥ” ubhayatra prathamacaturthāśramayoḥ ye dharmāḥ yamaniyamādayaḥ teṣām upapatteḥ saṃbhavāt “uttarasya” gārhasthyasya paripakvakaṣāyeṇānapekṣaṇīyatvāt “aikāśramyaṃ” ādyāntimayor āśramayor ekarūpatvam |
punaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
caturviṃśakam aṣṭācatvāriṃśakaṃ vā brahmacaryam | tad gurave samādheyam | naiṣṭhikam ātmajñānasaṃstham | pūrvottarayor ekajñānam anārambhatvāt | ācāryaśrāntaḥ yantrī śuddhim āpnotīty evam hy āha |
tapasā bhāvitātmānaḥ satyadharmaparāyaṇāḥ |
brahmaṇaivopasaṃpannā brāhmaṇā brahmalaukikāḥ ||
na cyavante punas tasmāc chuddhā dharmatapomayāḥ |
lokās tejomayās teṣāṃ ye caranty ūrdhvaretasaḥ ||
“caturviṃśakaṃ” caturviṃśatiparimitam | evaṃ “aṣṭācatvāriṃśakam” | “gurave” gurvārādhanāya | “samādheyaṃ” anuṣṭheyam | “ātmajñānasaṃsthaṃ” ātmajñānapratiṣṭhitam | “pūrvottarayoḥ” ādyacaturthayoḥ | “ekajñānaṃ” ekatvena jñānam | “anārambhatvāt” ṛtviksādhyārambhayajña-paratvābhāvāt | [268] “yantrī” yantravān, yantrāṇi yamanāni caturthakālabhojanādīni |
hārītaḥ |
sarveṣv āśrameṣv ācārantaḥ puṇyalokāḥ bhavanti | saiṣāśramaniśreṇir yayā devā svapitaraś cāmṛtatvam agacchan | prathamapuṇyo brahmacārī brahmacaryaniṣṭho brahmasiddhim avāpnoti | dvipuṇyo gṛhāśramaniṣṭho bhūyo mahimānaṃ brahmalokam avāpnoti | āśramānupūrvo vānaprasthaḥ pratiṣṭhobhayastaramahimānaṃ brahmalokam | āśramād āśramaṃ gacchan bhikṣur āśu siddhiṃ vindet | naiṣṭhiko brahmacāriṇāṃ śreṣṭhatamaḥ | yāyāvaro gṛhasthānām | ānuprasthiko vānaprasthānām | cāturāśramyasiddho yatīnām |
“dvipuṇyaḥ” dve puṇye āśramadvayakarmaṇī yasya sa tathā | “siddhiḥ brahmapāptiḥ |
manuḥ [6.89–91] |
brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho yatis tathā |
ete gṛhasthaprabhavāś catvāraḥ pṛthagāśramā ||
[sarve ‘pi kramaśs tv ete yajñāśāstraṃ niṣevitāḥ |]
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim ||
sarveṣām api caiteṣāṃ vedasmṛtividhānataḥ |
gṛhastha ucyate śreṣṭhaḥ sa trīn etān bibhati hi |\
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.10.13–15] |
gṛhītavidho gurave datvā ca gurudakṣiṇām |
gārhasthyam icchan bhūpāla kuryād dāraparigraham ||
[269]        brahmacaryeṇa vā kālaṃ kuryāt saṃkalpapūrvakam |
guroḥ śuśrūṣaṇaṃ kuryāt tatputrāder athāpi vā ||
vaikhānaso vāpi bhavet parivrāḍ atha vecchayā |
prajāpatir ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
sa caturdhātmānaṃ vidhāya brahmacryeṇa brahmayajñais tapasā dhyānena cātmānam aśrāmyat tad āśramā abhavan |
harītaḥ |
anāśramī vyapetaś ca āśramī ca nirarthakaḥ |
mithyāśramī ca sarve te nirayaṃ yānti mānavāḥ ||
“anāśramī vyapetaḥ” anāśramī san yo vyapeto bhūtaḥ | “nirarthakaḥ” svāśramoktakarmānuṣṭhānarahito liṅgamātradhārī | “mithyāśramī” dambhārtham āśramadharmān yo ‘nutiṣṭhati |
tathā |
brahmacaryaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ retodhāraṇam ucyate |
ṛtukālaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ nityaṃ vā parvavarjanam ||
“ṛtukālaṃ” ṛtukāla eva gamanaṃ yat tad gṛhasthānāṃ brahmacaryam ucyata ity arthaḥ |
tathā |
mantradravyāgnisaṃyogaṃ yajñamārgaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ |
ātmayajñas tu bhikṣūṇām ātmany eva vyavasthitaḥ ||
vratopavāsayantrādi tayor nityaṃ praśasyate |
trisatyapūtaṃ bhikṣūṇāṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
“ātmayajñaḥ” ātmajñānam evābhyudayalakṣaṇayajñakāryakāritvād yajñaḥ | “vrataṃ” śāstrato ‘gnīndhanādi |
[270]        yamaḥ |
brahmacāriṇām agnīndhanādir niyamaḥ | gṛhiṇaḥ pañcamahāyajñādiḥ | vanavāsino vanyāśādiḥ | bhikṣor ahaṃkāraparigrahatyāgaprāṇāyāmakṛcchrādiḥ trisatyād vāṅmanaḥkarmabhiḥ parapīḍāsaṃnyāso devatādyartham api vṛtte | upādānaparasya yācñādir ahiṃsayā |
“ahiṃsayā” apīḍanayā | ato devatāpūrvam anādikṛtā śramatrayād ūrdhvaṃ bhikṣoś caturvidhaṃ hiṃsāvarjanaṃ nyāyyam |
tathā |
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vaded vācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret ||
anena vidhinā yo hi āśramān upasevate |
sa sarvalokān nirjitya brahmalokāya kalpate || iti |
dakṣaḥ |
yo gṛhāśramam āsthāya brahmacārī bhavet punaḥ |
na yatir na gṛhasthaś ca sa sarvāśramavarjitaḥ ||
anāśramī na tiṣṭhet tu dinam ekam api dvijaḥ |
āśrameṇa vinā tiṣṭhan prāyaścittīyate dvijaḥ ||
jape home tathā dāne svādhyāye ca rataḥ sadā |
nāsau phalam avāpnoti kurvāṇo ‘py āśramacyutaḥ ||
trayāṇām ānulomyaṃ syāt prātilomyaṃ na vidyate |
prātilomye tv adho yāti na tasmāt pāpakṛttamaḥ ||
“ānulomyaṃ” brahmacārī gṛhastho vānaprastho yatiś cety ayaṃ kramaḥ | “prātilomyaṃ” uktavaiparītyam |
[271]
|| 21 ||
naiṣṭhikakriyā
atha hārītaḥ |
dvividha eva brahmacārī bhavati | upakurvāṇako naiṣṭhikaś ca | tayor upakurvāṇako vyākhyātaḥ | niṣṭhāṃ ced gacched ā śarīranipātād gurum anvāsīta, tadabhāve gurupatnyām, tadabhāve guruputre | guruputrābhāve ‘gnim eva paricaret | yajñiyāḥ samidha āhṛtya saṃmārjanopalepanodbodhanasamūhana-samindhanaparyagnikaraṇaparikramopasthānahomastotranamaskārādibhir agniṃ paricaret | nāgnim adhitiṣṭhet | na padbhyāṃ karṣet | na mukhenopadhamet | nāpaś cāgniṃ ca yugapad dhārayet | nājīrṇabhukta ucchiṣṭo vābhyādadhyāt | nābhyādadhed vātikrāmet | na niṣṭhīvet | vividhacaruhavirdānaviśeṣair āgneyair ahar ahar agniṃ samindhet | āmantrya gacchet | āhṛtya nivedayet | tanmanāḥ śarīroparamānte brāhmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ gacchati | saṃnyasya vā trayodaśaguṇaṃ brahmacaryam | aṣṭāṅgaṃ brahmacaryam eke | viṣṇuniṣṭhāḥ paramam acalaṃ padam āpnuvanti bhavanty eke |
anena vidhinā yo hi brahmacaryaṃ niṣevate |
sarvalokān vinirjitya brahmalokāya kalpate ||
[272]                kṣīraṃ pibanti madhu te pibanti
somaṃ pibanti hy aṣṭa tena sārdham |
mṛtyoḥ purastād amṛtaṃ bhavanti
ye brāḥmaṇā brahmacaryaṃ caranti ||
gurusamīpe ‘rthajñānaparyantaṃ svādhyāyasvarūpaṃ prayojanaṃ kurvāṇaḥ vasatīti “upakurvāṇakaḥ” | “udbodhanaṃ” agneḥ prakaṭīkaraṇam | “samūhanaṃ” vikīrṇasyāgneḥ kūṭīkaraṇam | “samindhanaṃ” prajvālanam | “parikramaṇaṃ” pradakṣiṇīkaraṇam | “upasthānaṃ” tadgatavāṅmanastvena samīpe ‘vasthānam | ādiśabdo jaya jayetyādiśabdaprayogair ity arthaḥ | “ajīrṇabhuktaḥ” na jīrṇaṃ hy astanaṃ bhuktaṃ yasya sa tathā | “abhyādadhyāt” mantrapūrvakaṃ samidādhānaṃ kuryāt | “caruḥ” anāśrāvitam ūṣmapakvam annam | “havīṃṣi” ājyādhānasaktvāmikṣādīny akṣāralavaṇāni | etāny uddeśenāgnau pratikṣipet | samindhanam apy evam eva siddhaṃ bhavati | “āmantrya gacchet” ācāryaṃ pṛṣṭvā gacchet | “tanmanāḥ” gurugatacittaḥ | “trayodaśaguṇaṃ” strīmadyanivāsapraveśāṅgasaṃgasparśadṛṣṭimelana-saṃdarśanatiryagālokanasaṃkathanapraśnatadvacanākarṇanadūrāvekṣaṇanikaṭabhramaṇatajjana-priyavacanaṃ caite trayodaśaguṇāḥ |
devalaḥ |
brahmacāridharmo brahmacaryaṃ svādhyāyāvaśyakabrahmasūtrākṣamālā-daṇḍakaupīnakuṇḍikāmauñjīmekhalādhāraṇaṃ sakṛdbhojanam asakṛdavagāhanam ubhayakālam agnihotraṃ saṃdhyopasthānam aluptaloma-nakhatvaggandhamālyasnehābhyañjanaveṣālaṅkārachatropā[273]nadvāhana-laṅghanaplavanadhāvanacikitsājyotirlakṣaṇavāśtuvidyāmaṅgalapauṣṭikaśānti-karmagāndharvasaṅghasamayabandhanaśilpalekhanakārukarmaveśmakṣetra-dravyadhānyaparicchedaśastradyūtavyavahārājñābhāvalīlāparihāsapraṇaya-kuhakavismāpanaviḍambanavivādotsekaparidevanarodanavastinasta-pādodvartanavarjanam | guroḥ pūrvotthānaṃ jaghanyasaṃveśanaṃ pramāṇasaṃdiṣṭakaraṇam iṣṭaśuśrūṣā nityopāsanam apacitiḥ | tadabhāve tatputre ‘sya dāreṣu cānuvṛttir ā maraṇād iti |
“kuṇḍikā” kamaṇḍaluḥ | “luptaṃ” chinnaṃ | “veṣaḥ” maṇḍanam | “plavanaṃ” utplavanam | “lakṣaṇavāstuvidyā” sā śūdravidyā | “saṅghaḥ” samūhaḥ | “samayaḥ” saṅketaḥ, “bandhanaṃ” tanniyamaḥ | “kāravaḥ” sūpakārādyāḥ | “bhāvaḥ” śṛṅgārādirasanidānamanovikāraḥ | “utseko” darpaḥ | “vastiḥ” nirūhādirūpā | “nasta” iti nasyaṃ lakṣyate | “pramāṇasaṃdiṣṭakaraṇaṃ” pramāṇena saṃdiṣṭasya karaṇaṃ, na bhrāntyādineṣṭaśuśrūṣā | apacitiḥ pūjā |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
kāmato vāśminn eva svādhyāyaḥ | prayojanāpekṣī gāyatrīrudrakalpopaniṣatsv abhirataḥ | savanopasparśī śeṣavṛttisaṃbandhatvenoktam upāsīta | ātmaratir gurukulaniṣṭha ātmaniṣṭho vā | so ‘mṛtatvam āpnoti |
“prayojanāpekṣī” ācāryasyeti śeṣaḥ | “rudrakalpaḥ” śatarudriyavidhānam | “savanopasparśī” trikālasnāyī |
[274]        manuḥ [3.242–244] |
nābrāhmaṇe gurau śiṣyo vāsam ātyantikaṃ vaset |
brāhmaṇe cānanūcāne kāṅkṣan gatim uttamām ||
yadi tv ātyantikaṃ vāsaṃ rocayet guroḥ kule |
yuktaḥ paricared enam ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt ||
ā samāpteḥ śarīrasya yas tu śuśrūṣate gurum |
sa gacchaty añjasā vipro brahmaṇaḥ sadma śāśvatam ||
tathā [3.247–249] |
ācārye tu khalu prete guruputre guṇānvite |
gurudāre sapiṇḍe vā guruvad vṛttim ācaret ||
eṣu tv avidyamāneṣu sthānāsanavihāravān |
prayuñjāno ‘gniśuśrūṣāṃ sādhayed deham ātmanaḥ ||
evaṃ carati yo vipro brahmacaryam aviplutam |
sa gacchaty uttamaṃ sthānaṃ na cehājāyate punaḥ ||
“ācāryaprete ‘nubandha” ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ |
gurudāreṣu savarṇeṣu vā |
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃskāra 259–262] |
upakurvāṇakasyaitat samādiṣṭaṃ mayā vratam |
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi naiṣṭhikasya samāsataḥ ||
saṃdhyāgnikāryaṃ svādhyāyaṃ bhaikṣyādhaḥśayanaṃ damaḥ |
prāṅniṣiddhaparityāgaḥ manobhavaṃ [?] guror hitam ||
gurvabhāve tu taddārasutajñātyagnisevanam |
ā mṛtyor naiṣṭhikaḥ kurvan brahmalokam avāpnuyāt ||
[275]
|| 22 ||
atha gurudakṣiṇāsnāne
tatra vyāsaḥ |
guruśuśrūṣayā vidyāṃ saṃprāpya vidhivad dvijaḥ |
snāyīta gurvanujñāto datvāsmai dakṣiṇāṃ ca gām ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.51] |
gurave tu varaṃ datvā snāyāc ca tadanujñayā |
vedavratāni vā pāraṃ nītvāpy ubhayam eva vā ||
“varo” gavādiḥ |
manuḥ [2.245–246] |
na pūrvaṃ gurave kiṃcid upakurvīta dharmavit |
snāsyaṃs tu guruṇājñaptaḥ śaktyā gurvartham āharet ||
kṣetraṃ hiraṇyaṃ gām aśvaṃ chatropānaham antataḥ |
dhānyaṃ śākaṃ ca vāsāṃsi gurave prītim āharan ||
“prītiṃ” priyam |
āpastambaḥ [1.7.19–25] |
kṛtvā vidyāṃ yāvatīṃ śaknuyād vedadakṣiṇām āhared dharmato yathāśakti | viṣamagate tv ācārya ugrataḥ śūdrato [276] vāharet | sarvadā śūdrata ugrato vācāryārthasyāharaṇaṃ dharmam ity eke | datvā vā nānukathayet | kṛtvā ca nānusmaret | ātmapraśaṃsāṃ paragarhām iti varjayet |
“yāvatīṃ vidyāṃ śaknuyāt” iti, vedaṃ vedān vā tāvatīṃ gṛhītvety arthaḥ | “vedadakṣiṇāṃ” vedārthāṃ dakṣiṇām | “ugraḥ” kṣatriyeṇa śūdrāyāṃ jātaḥ | tat kṣātravād brāhmaṇyāṃ iti kapardī | “iti” hetoḥ, yat svadakṣiṇādānaṃ praśaṃsāyām, paradakṣiṇādānaṃ garyāyāṃ ca, anukathanasmaraṇe ca syātām | praśaṃsāṃ vā garhāṃ vā varjayed ity arthaḥ |
vasiṣṭha-baudhāyanau [Bau 4.4.10] |
sahasraṃ dakṣiṇā ṛṣabhaikādaśaṃ guruprasādo vā |
“sahasraṃ” iti gāvaḥ | “ṛṣabhaikādaśa” iti sahasradānāśaktāv iti bhartṛyajñaḥ |
laghuhārītaḥ |
ekam apy akṣaraṃ yas tu guruḥ śiṣye nivedayet |
pṛthivyāṃ nāsti tad dravyaṃ yad datvā tv anṛṇī bhavet ||
atrārthajñānam śrutvānantaraṃ laghuvyāsaḥ |
śaktyā samāpya yo vidyāṃ nyāyatas tu tadarthavit |
samyag vratāni samsevya samāvartanam arhati ||
brahmacaryaṃ samāpya yan mantravat sugandhādinā snānaṃ tat “samāvartanam” |
[277]        hārītaḥ |
trayaḥ snātakā bhavanti vidyāsnātako vratasnātako vidyāvratasnātaka iti | yaḥ samāpya vedam asamāpya vratāni samāvartate sa vidyāsnātakaḥ | yaḥ samāpya vratāny asamāpya vedaṃ samāvartate sa vratasnātakaḥ | ubhayaṃ samāpya samāvartate sa vidyāvratasnātaka iti | tad evaṃ vidyāsnāto ‘laṅkārādīni gṛhṇīyāt | yathārthaṃ cailān bibhṛyāt |
“yatharthaṃ” yathāprayojanam |
āpastambaḥ [1.30.1–4] |
vidyayā snātīty eke | tathā vratenāṣṭācatvāriṃśatparimāṇena | vidyāvratena cety eke | teṣu sarveṣu snātakavad vṛttiḥ |
punaḥ āpastambaḥ [2.18.4] |
adhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya triṣavaṇam udakam upaspṛśann anagnipakvavṛttir acchāyopagataḥ sthānāsanikaḥ saṃvatsaram etad vrataṃ caret | etad aṣṭācatvāriṃśatsaṃmitam ity ācakṣate |
dantaprakṣālanādimantrān abhidhāya pāraskaraḥ [2.6.32] |
dantaprakṣālanādīni nityam api | vāsaśchatropānahaś cāpūrvāṇi cen mantraḥ |
[278]        baudhāyanaḥ [1.5.1–6] |
antarvāsa uttarīyavān | vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍaṃ dhārayet | sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum | dviyajñopavītī | uṣṇīṣam ajinottarīyam upānahau chatraṃ ca |
“antarvāsaḥ” paridhānavastram | “uttarīyaṃ” uparivastram | “ajinottarīyaṃ” vāsasā saha vaikalpikam, “ajinaṃ vāso vā dakṣiṇata upavītī” iti śruteḥ |
manuḥ [4.35] |
kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśrur dāntaḥ śuklāmbaraḥ śuciḥ |
svādhyāye caiva yuktaḥ syān nityam ātmahiteṣu ca ||
“kḷptaṃ” chinnam |
tathā [4.36] |
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhe raukme ca kuṇḍale |
“vedo” darbhamuṣṭiḥ | “raukme” sauvarṇe |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [12.39] |
na bahir mālāṃ dārayet |
hārītaḥ |
nāgandhāṃ raktāṃ vā srajam ābadhnīyād anyatra kāñcanakamalakuvalayebhyaḥ |
“kuvalayaṃ” utpalam |
āpstambaḥ |
susraggandhalepanaḥ syāt |
[279]        jābāliḥ |
nityaṃ śuciḥ sugandhiḥ syāt śuklāmbaradharaḥ kṣamī |
svānuṣṭhānaparo gehī sapatnīko vratāṃś caret ||
gautamaḥ [9.1–7] |
sa vidhipūrvaṃ snātvā bhāryām adhigamya [yathoktān] gṛhasthadharmān prayuñjāna imāni vratāny anukarṣet snātakaḥ | nityaṃ [śuciḥ] sugandhiḥ snānaśīlaḥ | sati vibhave na jīrṇamalavadvāsāḥ syāt | na raktam ulbaṇam anyadhṛtaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt | na sragupānahau | nirṇiktam aśaktau | na rūḍhaśmaśrur akasmāt |
kāraṇavyatirekeṇa na dīrghaśmaśruḥ syād ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ [4.66] |
upānahau ca vāsaś ca dhṛtam anyair na dhārayet |
upavītam alaṅkāraṃ srajaṃ karakam eva ca ||
“karakaṃ” kamaṇḍaluḥ |
[iti kṛtyakalpatarau brahmacārikāṇḍe gurudakṣiṇāsnāne]
[iti mahārājādhirājaśrīgovindacandradevamahāsaṃdhivigrahika-
śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajaśrīlakṣmīdharabhaṭṭasya kṛtau kṛtyakalpatarau
brahmacārikāṇḍam]
